   28: def utc_now() -> datetime:
   29:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   30: 
   31: 
   32: def parse_iso_date(
   33:     value: str | date | None,
   34: ) -> date | None:
   35:     if value is None:
   36:         return None
   37: 
   38:     if isinstance(value, date):
   39:         return value
   40: 
   41:     try:
   42:         return date.fromisoformat(value)
   43:     except ValueError:
   44:         return None
   45: 
   46: 
   47: def serialize_audit_value(
   48:     value: Any,
   49: ) -> Any:
   50:     if isinstance(value, Decimal):
   51:         return format(value, ".2f")
   52: 
   53:     if isinstance(value, (date, datetime)):
   54:         return value.isoformat()
   55: 
   56:     if isinstance(value, list):
   57:         return [
   58:             serialize_audit_value(item)
   59:             for item in value
   60:         ]
   61: 
   62:     if isinstance(value, dict):
   63:         return {
   64:             key: serialize_audit_value(item)
   65:             for key, item in value.items()
   66:         }
   67: 
   68:     return value
   69: 
   70: 
   71: def add_audit_event(
   72:     database: Session,
   73:     *,
   74:     action: str,
   75:     entity_type: str,
   76:     entity_id: int | str,
   77:     actor: str = "system",
   78:     event_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   79: ) -> AuditEvent:
   80:     event = AuditEvent(
   81:         action=action,
   82:         entity_type=entity_type,
   83:         entity_id=str(entity_id),
   84:         actor=actor,
   85:         event_data=serialize_audit_value(
   86:             event_data or {}
   87:         ),
   88:     )
   89: 
   90:     database.add(event)
   91: 
   92:     return event
   93: 
   94: 
   95: def validate_invoice_values(
   96:     *,
   97:     supplier_name: str | None,
   98:     supplier_tax_id: str | None,
   99:     invoice_number: str | None,
  100:     invoice_date: date | None,
  101:     subtotal: Decimal | None,
  102:     tax_total: Decimal | None,
  103:     withholding_total: Decimal | None,
  104:     surcharge_total: Decimal | None,
  105:     total: Decimal | None,
  106:     currency: str | None,
  107: ) -> tuple[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
  108:     messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  109: 
  110:     required_fields = {
  111:         "supplier_name": supplier_name,
  112:         "supplier_tax_id": supplier_tax_id,
  113:         "invoice_number": invoice_number,
  114:         "invoice_date": invoice_date,
  115:         "total": total,
  116:         "currency": currency,
  117:     }
  118: 
  119:     for field_name, field_value in required_fields.items():
  120:         if field_value in (None, ""):
  121:             messages.append(
  122:                 {
  123:                     "code": "required_field_missing",
  124:                     "field": field_name,
  125:                     "severity": "error",
  126:                     "message": (
  127:                         f"Falta el campo obligatorio: "
  128:                         f"{field_name}."
  129:                     ),
  130:                 }
  131:             )
  132: 
  133:     if total is not None and total < 0:
  134:         messages.append(
  135:             {
  136:                 "code": "negative_total",
  137:                 "field": "total",
  138:                 "severity": "warning",
  139:                 "message": (
  140:                     "El total es negativo. Comprueba si "
  141:                     "se trata de una factura rectificativa."
  142:                 ),
  143:             }
  144:         )
  145: 
  146:     if invoice_date is not None:
  147:         today = date.today()
  148: 
  149:         if invoice_date > today:
  150:             messages.append(
  151:                 {
  152:                     "code": "future_invoice_date",
  153:                     "field": "invoice_date",
  154:                     "severity": "warning",
  155:                     "message": (
  156:                         "La fecha de la factura es futura."
  157:                     ),
  158:                 }
  159:             )
  160: 
  161:     amounts_available = (
  162:         subtotal is not None
  163:         and tax_total is not None
  164:         and total is not None
  165:     )
  166: 
  167:     if amounts_available:
  168:         withholding = (
  169:             withholding_total
  170:             or Decimal("0.00")
  171:         )
  172:         surcharge = (
  173:             surcharge_total
  174:             or Decimal("0.00")
  175:         )
  176: 
  177:         expected_total = (
  178:             subtotal
  179:             + tax_total
  180:             + surcharge
  181:             - withholding
  182:         ).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
  183: 
  184:         difference = abs(
  185:             total - expected_total
  186:         ).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
  187: 
  188:         tolerance = Decimal(
  189:             str(settings.amount_tolerance)
  190:         )
  191: 
  192:         if difference > tolerance:
  193:             messages.append(
  194:                 {
  195:                     "code": "amount_mismatch",
  196:                     "field": "total",
  197:                     "severity": "error",
  198:                     "message": (
  199:                         "Los importes no cuadran. "
  200:                         f"El total esperado es "
  201:                         f"{expected_total:.2f} y el total "
  202:                         f"extraído es {total:.2f}."
  203:                     ),
  204:                     "difference": format(
  205:                         difference,
  206:                         ".2f",
  207:                     ),
  208:                     "expected_total": format(
  209:                         expected_total,
  210:                         ".2f",
  211:                     ),
  212:                 }
  213:             )
  214:     else:
  215:         messages.append(
  216:             {
  217:                 "code": "amounts_incomplete",
  218:                 "field": "amounts",
  219:                 "severity": "warning",
  220:                 "message": (
  221:                     "No se puede comprobar el cuadre "
  222:                     "porque faltan base, impuestos o total."
  223:                 ),
  224:             }
  225:         )
  226: 
  227:     error_messages = [
  228:         message
  229:         for message in messages
  230:         if message["severity"] == "error"
  231:     ]
  232: 
  233:     missing_messages = [
  234:         message
  235:         for message in messages
  236:         if message["code"]
  237:         == "required_field_missing"
  238:     ]
  239: 
  240:     if missing_messages:
  241:         return "INCOMPLETE", messages
  242: 
  243:     if error_messages:
  244:         return "MISMATCH", messages
  245: 
  246:     if not amounts_available:
  247:         return "INCOMPLETE", messages
  248: 
  249:     return "VALID", messages
  250: 
  251: 
  252: def get_document(
  253:     database: Session,
  254:     document_id: int,
  255: ) -> Document | None:
  256:     statement = (
  257:         select(Document)
  258:         .where(Document.id == document_id)
  259:         .options(
  260:             selectinload(Document.invoice).selectinload(
  261:                 Invoice.tax_lines
  262:             ),
  263:             selectinload(
  264:                 Document.extraction_runs
  265:             ),
  266:         )
  267:     )
  268: 
  269:     return database.scalar(statement)
  270: 
  271: 
  272: def get_invoice(
  273:     database: Session,
  274:     invoice_id: int,
  275: ) -> Invoice | None:
  276:     statement = (
  277:         select(Invoice)
  278:         .where(Invoice.id == invoice_id)
  279:         .options(
  280:             selectinload(Invoice.tax_lines),
  281:             selectinload(Invoice.document),
  282:         )
  283:     )
  284: 
  285:     return database.scalar(statement)
  286: 
  287: 
  288: def field_value(
  289:     result: dict[str, Any],
  290:     field_name: str,
  291: ) -> Any:
  292:     return (
  293:         result
  294:         .get("fields", {})
  295:         .get(field_name, {})
  296:         .get("value")
  297:     )
  298: 
  299: 
  300: def field_confidences(
  301:     result: dict[str, Any],
  302: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  303:     confidences: dict[str, Any] = {}
  304: 
  305:     for name, field in result.get(
  306:         "fields",
  307:         {},
  308:     ).items():
  309:         confidences[name] = {
  310:             "confidence": field.get(
  311:                 "confidence",
  312:                 0,
  313:             ),
  314:             "source": field.get("source"),
  315:             "evidence": field.get(
  316:                 "evidence"
  317:             ),
  318:         }
  319: 
  320:     return confidences
  321: 
  322: 
  323: def find_fiscal_duplicate(
  324:     database: Session,
  325:     *,
  326:     invoice: Invoice,
  327: ) -> tuple[str, Invoice | None]:
  328:     supplier_tax_id = normalize_tax_id(
  329:         invoice.supplier_tax_id
  330:     )
  331: 
  332:     invoice_number = normalize_invoice_number(
  333:         invoice.invoice_number
  334:     )
  335: 
  336:     if supplier_tax_id and invoice_number:
  337:         strong_statement = (
  338:             select(Invoice)
  339:             .where(
  340:                 Invoice.id != invoice.id,
  341:                 Invoice.supplier_tax_id
  342:                 == supplier_tax_id,
  343:                 Invoice.invoice_number
  344:                 == invoice_number,
  345:             )
  346:             .order_by(Invoice.id.asc())
  347:         )
  348: 
  349:         duplicate = database.scalar(
  350:             strong_statement
  351:         )
  352: 
  353:         if duplicate:
  354:             return "STRONG", duplicate
  355: 
  356:     if (
  357:         supplier_tax_id
  358:         and invoice.invoice_date
  359:         and invoice.total is not None
  360:     ):
  361:         probable_statement = (
  362:             select(Invoice)
  363:             .where(
  364:                 Invoice.id != invoice.id,
  365:                 Invoice.supplier_tax_id
  366:                 == supplier_tax_id,
  367:                 Invoice.invoice_date
  368:                 == invoice.invoice_date,
  369:                 Invoice.total == invoice.total,
  370:             )
  371:             .order_by(Invoice.id.asc())
  372:         )
  373: 
  374:         duplicate = database.scalar(
  375:             probable_statement
  376:         )
  377: 
  378:         if duplicate:
  379:             return "PROBABLE", duplicate
  380: 
  381:     return "NONE", None
  382: 
  383: 
  384: def apply_duplicate_detection(
  385:     database: Session,
  386:     invoice: Invoice,
  387: ) -> None:
  388:     duplicate_status, duplicate = (
  389:         find_fiscal_duplicate(
  390:             database,
  391:             invoice=invoice,
  392:         )
  393:     )
  394: 
  395:     invoice.duplicate_status = (
  396:         duplicate_status
  397:     )
  398:     invoice.duplicate_of_invoice_id = (
  399:         duplicate.id
  400:         if duplicate
  401:         else None
  402:     )
  403: 
  404: 
  405: def update_document_status(
  406:     document: Document,
  407:     invoice: Invoice | None,
  408: ) -> None:
  409:     if document.requires_ocr:
  410:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  411:         document.extraction_status = (
  412:             "OCR_REQUIRED"
  413:         )
  414:         return
  415: 
  416:     if invoice is None:
  417:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  418:         document.extraction_status = (
  419:             "COMPLETED"
  420:         )
  421:         return
  422: 
  423:     if invoice.review_status == "APPROVED":
  424:         document.status = "APPROVED"
  425:         return
  426: 
  427:     if invoice.review_status == "REJECTED":
  428:         document.status = "REJECTED"
  429:         return
  430: 
  431:     if invoice.duplicate_status != "NONE":
  432:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  433:         return
  434: 
  435:     if (
  436:         invoice.validation_status == "VALID"
  437:         and invoice.confidence
  438:         >= settings.minimum_auto_confidence
  439:     ):
  440:         document.status = (
  441:             "READY_FOR_APPROVAL"
  442:         )
  443:     else:
  444:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  445: 
  446: 
  447: def create_or_replace_tax_lines(
  448:     database: Session,
  449:     invoice: Invoice,
  450:     tax_lines: list[dict[str, Any]],
  451: ) -> None:
  452:     invoice.tax_lines.clear()
  453:     database.flush()
  454: 
  455:     for line in tax_lines:
  456:         invoice.tax_lines.append(
  457:             InvoiceTaxLine(
  458:                 tax_type=(
  459:                     line.get("tax_type")
  460:                     or "IVA"
  461:                 ),
  462:                 tax_rate=normalize_amount(
  463:                     line.get("tax_rate")
  464:                 ),
  465:                 tax_base=normalize_amount(
  466:                     line.get("tax_base")
  467:                 ),
  468:                 tax_amount=normalize_amount(
  469:                     line.get("tax_amount")
  470:                 ),
  471:                 source=line.get("source"),
  472:                 confidence=int(
  473:                     line.get("confidence")
  474:                     or 0
  475:                 ),
  476:             )
  477:         )
  478: 
  479: 
  480: def persist_extraction_result(
  481:     database: Session,
  482:     *,
  483:     document: Document,
  484:     extraction_run: ExtractionRun,
  485:     result: dict[str, Any],
  486: ) -> Invoice | None:
  487:     document.page_count = result.get(
  488:         "page_count"
  489:     )
  490:     document.requires_ocr = bool(
  491:         result.get("requires_ocr")
  492:     )
  493: 
  494:     extraction_run.status = "COMPLETED"
  495:     extraction_run.raw_text = result.get(
  496:         "raw_text"
  497:     )
  498:     extraction_run.result_json = {
  499:         key: value
  500:         for key, value in result.items()
  501:         if key != "raw_text"
  502:     }
  503:     extraction_run.finished_at = utc_now()
  504: 
  505:     if document.requires_ocr:
  506:         document.extraction_status = (
  507:             "OCR_REQUIRED"
  508:         )
  509:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  510:         return None
  511: 
  512:     document.extraction_status = "COMPLETED"
  513: 
  514:     if not result.get("is_invoice"):
  515:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  516: 
  517:         add_audit_event(
  518:             database,
  519:             action=(
  520:                 "document.not_identified_as_invoice"
  521:             ),
  522:             entity_type="document",
  523:             entity_id=document.id,
  524:             actor="extractor",
  525:             event_data={
  526:                 "invoice_likelihood": result.get(
  527:                     "invoice_likelihood"
  528:                 ),
  529:                 "signals": result.get(
  530:                     "signals",
  531:                     [],
  532:                 ),
  533:             },
  534:         )
  535: 
  536:         return None
  537: 
  538:     invoice = document.invoice
  539: 
  540:     if invoice is None:
  541:         invoice = Invoice(
  542:             document=document,
  543:         )
  544:         database.add(invoice)
  545: 
  546:     invoice.supplier_name = field_value(
  547:         result,
  548:         "supplier_name",
  549:     )
  550:     invoice.supplier_tax_id = normalize_tax_id(
  551:         field_value(
  552:             result,
  553:             "supplier_tax_id",
  554:         )
  555:     )
  556: 
  557:     invoice.customer_name = field_value(
  558:         result,
  559:         "customer_name",
  560:     )
  561:     invoice.customer_tax_id = normalize_tax_id(
  562:         field_value(
  563:             result,
  564:             "customer_tax_id",
  565:         )
  566:     )
  567: 
  568:     invoice.invoice_number = normalize_invoice_number(
  569:         field_value(
  570:             result,
  571:             "invoice_number",
  572:         )
  573:     )
  574:     invoice.invoice_date = parse_iso_date(
  575:         field_value(
  576:             result,
  577:             "invoice_date",
  578:         )
  579:     )
  580:     invoice.due_date = parse_iso_date(
  581:         field_value(
  582:             result,
  583:             "due_date",
  584:         )
  585:     )
  586: 
  587:     invoice.subtotal = normalize_amount(
  588:         field_value(
  589:             result,
  590:             "subtotal",
  591:         )
  592:     )
  593:     invoice.tax_total = normalize_amount(
  594:         field_value(
  595:             result,
  596:             "tax_total",
  597:         )
  598:     )
  599:     invoice.withholding_total = normalize_amount(
  600:         field_value(
  601:             result,
  602:             "withholding_total",
  603:         )
  604:     )
  605:     invoice.surcharge_total = normalize_amount(
  606:         field_value(
  607:             result,
  608:             "surcharge_total",
  609:         )
  610:     )
  611:     invoice.total = normalize_amount(
  612:         field_value(
  613:             result,
  614:             "total",
  615:         )
  616:     )
  617: 
  618:     invoice.currency = (
  619:         field_value(
  620:             result,
  621:             "currency",
  622:         )
  623:         or "EUR"
  624:     )
  625:     invoice.concept = field_value(
  626:         result,
  627:         "concept",
  628:     )
  629:     invoice.category = field_value(
  630:         result,
  631:         "category",
  632:     )
  633: 
  634:     invoice.confidence = int(
  635:         result.get(
  636:             "overall_confidence",
  637:             0,
  638:         )
  639:     )
  640:     invoice.field_confidences = (
  641:         field_confidences(result)
  642:     )
  643: 
  644:     database.flush()
  645: 
  646:     create_or_replace_tax_lines(
  647:         database,
  648:         invoice,
  649:         result.get(
  650:             "tax_lines",
  651:             [],
  652:         ),
  653:     )
  654: 
  655:     (
  656:         invoice.validation_status,
  657:         invoice.validation_messages,
  658:     ) = validate_invoice_values(
  659:         supplier_name=invoice.supplier_name,
  660:         supplier_tax_id=invoice.supplier_tax_id,
  661:         invoice_number=invoice.invoice_number,
  662:         invoice_date=invoice.invoice_date,
  663:         subtotal=invoice.subtotal,
  664:         tax_total=invoice.tax_total,
  665:         withholding_total=(
  666:             invoice.withholding_total
  667:         ),
  668:         surcharge_total=(
  669:             invoice.surcharge_total
  670:         ),
  671:         total=invoice.total,
  672:         currency=invoice.currency,
  673:     )
  674: 
  675:     database.flush()
  676: 
  677:     apply_duplicate_detection(
  678:         database,
  679:         invoice,
  680:     )
  681: 
  682:     update_document_status(
  683:         document,
  684:         invoice,
  685:     )
  686: 
  687:     return invoice
  688: 
  689: 
  690: def process_document(
  691:     database: Session,
  692:     *,
  693:     document: Document,
  694:     file_path: Path,
  695:     actor: str = "system",
  696: ) -> Invoice | None:
  697:     document.status = "PROCESSING"
  698:     document.extraction_status = "RUNNING"
  699:     document.failure_reason = None
  700: 
  701:     extraction_run = ExtractionRun(
  702:         document=document,
  703:         extractor_name=(
  704:             "capafiscal.generic_invoice"
  705:         ),
  706:         extractor_version="2.0.0",
  707:         status="RUNNING",
  708:     )
  709: 
  710:     database.add(extraction_run)
  711:     database.flush()
  712: 
  713:     add_audit_event(
  714:         database,
  715:         action="document.processing.started",
  716:         entity_type="document",
  717:         entity_id=document.id,
  718:         actor=actor,
  719:         event_data={
  720:             "extractor": (
  721:                 extraction_run.extractor_name
  722:             ),
  723:             "version": (
  724:                 extraction_run.extractor_version
  725:             ),
  726:         },
  727:     )
  728: 
  729:     try:
  730:         configured_tax_ids = (
  731:             settings.company_tax_ids
  732:             or settings.company_tax_id
  733:         )
  734: 
  735:         result = extract_invoice(
  736:             file_path,
  737:             company_tax_id=configured_tax_ids,
  738:             company_name=settings.company_name,
  739:         )
  740: 
  741:         invoice = persist_extraction_result(
  742:             database,
  743:             document=document,
  744:             extraction_run=extraction_run,
  745:             result=result,
  746:         )
  747: 
  748:         add_audit_event(
  749:             database,
  750:             action=(
  751:                 "document.processing.completed"
  752:             ),
  753:             entity_type="document",
  754:             entity_id=document.id,
  755:             actor="extractor",
  756:             event_data={
  757:                 "is_invoice": result.get(
  758:                     "is_invoice"
  759:                 ),
  760:                 "requires_ocr": result.get(
  761:                     "requires_ocr"
  762:                 ),
  763:                 "overall_confidence": (
  764:                     result.get(
  765:                         "overall_confidence"
  766:                     )
  767:                 ),
  768:                 "document_status": (
  769:                     document.status
  770:                 ),
  771:                 "invoice_id": (
  772:                     invoice.id
  773:                     if invoice
  774:                     else None
  775:                 ),
  776:             },
  777:         )
  778: 
  779:         database.commit()
  780: 
  781:         return invoice
  782: 
  783:     except Exception as error:
  784:         document.status = "FAILED"
  785:         document.extraction_status = "FAILED"
  786:         document.failure_reason = str(error)
  787: 
  788:         extraction_run.status = "FAILED"
  789:         extraction_run.error_message = str(error)
  790:         extraction_run.finished_at = utc_now()
  791: 
  792:         add_audit_event(
  793:             database,
  794:             action="document.processing.failed",
  795:             entity_type="document",
  796:             entity_id=document.id,
  797:             actor="extractor",
  798:             event_data={
  799:                 "error": str(error),
  800:             },
  801:         )
  802: 
  803:         database.commit()
  804: 
  805:         raise
  806: 
  807: 
  808: def invoice_snapshot(
  809:     invoice: Invoice,
  810: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  811:     return {
  812:         "supplier_name": invoice.supplier_name,
  813:         "supplier_tax_id": (
  814:             invoice.supplier_tax_id
  815:         ),
  816:         "customer_name": invoice.customer_name,
  817:         "customer_tax_id": (
  818:             invoice.customer_tax_id
  819:         ),
  820:         "invoice_number": (
  821:             invoice.invoice_number
  822:         ),
  823:         "invoice_date": (
  824:             invoice.invoice_date
  825:         ),
  826:         "due_date": invoice.due_date,
  827:         "subtotal": invoice.subtotal,
  828:         "tax_total": invoice.tax_total,
  829:         "withholding_total": (
  830:             invoice.withholding_total
  831:         ),
  832:         "surcharge_total": (
  833:             invoice.surcharge_total
  834:         ),
  835:         "total": invoice.total,
  836:         "currency": invoice.currency,
  837:         "concept": invoice.concept,
  838:         "category": invoice.category,
  839:         "tax_lines": [
  840:             {
  841:                 "tax_type": line.tax_type,
  842:                 "tax_rate": line.tax_rate,
  843:                 "tax_base": line.tax_base,
  844:                 "tax_amount": line.tax_amount,
  845:             }
  846:             for line in invoice.tax_lines
  847:         ],
  848:     }
  849: 
  850: 
  851: def update_invoice(
  852:     database: Session,
  853:     *,
  854:     invoice: Invoice,
  855:     payload: InvoiceUpdate,
  856:     actor: str = "user",
  857: ) -> Invoice:
  858:     before = invoice_snapshot(invoice)
  859: 
  860:     changes = payload.model_dump(
  861:         exclude_unset=True,
  862:     )
  863:     tax_lines = changes.pop(
  864:         "tax_lines",
  865:         None,
  866:     )
  867: 
  868:     if "supplier_tax_id" in changes:
  869:         changes["supplier_tax_id"] = (
  870:             normalize_tax_id(
  871:                 changes["supplier_tax_id"]
  872:             )
  873:         )
  874: 
  875:     if "customer_tax_id" in changes:
  876:         changes["customer_tax_id"] = (
  877:             normalize_tax_id(
  878:                 changes["customer_tax_id"]
  879:             )
  880:         )
  881: 
  882:     amount_fields = (
  883:         "subtotal",
  884:         "tax_total",
  885:         "withholding_total",
  886:         "surcharge_total",
  887:         "total",
  888:     )
  889: 
  890:     for amount_field in amount_fields:
  891:         if amount_field in changes:
  892:             changes[amount_field] = (
  893:                 normalize_amount(
  894:                     changes[amount_field]
  895:                 )
  896:             )
  897: 
  898:     for field_name, field_value in changes.items():
  899:         setattr(
  900:             invoice,
  901:             field_name,
  902:             field_value,
  903:         )
  904: 
  905:     if tax_lines is not None:
  906:         create_or_replace_tax_lines(
  907:             database,
  908:             invoice,
  909:             tax_lines,
  910:         )
  911: 
  912:     invoice.review_status = "PENDING"
  913:     invoice.approved_at = None
  914:     invoice.rejected_at = None
  915:     invoice.rejection_reason = None
  916: 
  917:     (
  918:         invoice.validation_status,
  919:         invoice.validation_messages,
  920:     ) = validate_invoice_values(
  921:         supplier_name=invoice.supplier_name,
  922:         supplier_tax_id=invoice.supplier_tax_id,
  923:         invoice_number=invoice.invoice_number,
  924:         invoice_date=invoice.invoice_date,
  925:         subtotal=invoice.subtotal,
  926:         tax_total=invoice.tax_total,
  927:         withholding_total=(
  928:             invoice.withholding_total
  929:         ),
  930:         surcharge_total=(
  931:             invoice.surcharge_total
  932:         ),
  933:         total=invoice.total,
  934:         currency=invoice.currency,
  935:     )
  936: 
  937:     database.flush()
  938: 
  939:     apply_duplicate_detection(
  940:         database,
  941:         invoice,
  942:     )
  943: 
  944:     update_document_status(
  945:         invoice.document,
  946:         invoice,
  947:     )
  948: 
  949:     after = invoice_snapshot(invoice)
  950: 
  951:     changed_fields = {
  952:         field_name: {
  953:             "before": before.get(field_name),
  954:             "after": after.get(field_name),
  955:         }
  956:         for field_name in after
  957:         if before.get(field_name)
  958:         != after.get(field_name)
  959:     }
  960: 
  961:     add_audit_event(
  962:         database,
  963:         action="invoice.updated",
  964:         entity_type="invoice",
  965:         entity_id=invoice.id,
  966:         actor=actor,
  967:         event_data={
  968:             "changed_fields": changed_fields,
  969:             "validation_status": (
  970:                 invoice.validation_status
  971:             ),
  972:         },
  973:     )
  974: 
  975:     database.commit()
  976:     database.refresh(invoice)
  977: 
  978:     return invoice
  979: 
  980: 
  981: def approve_invoice(
  982:     database: Session,
  983:     *,
  984:     invoice: Invoice,
  985:     actor: str = "user",
  986: ) -> Invoice:
  987:     if invoice.validation_status != "VALID":
  988:         raise ValueError(
  989:             "La factura no puede aprobarse porque "
  990:             "tiene errores o campos obligatorios "
  991:             "pendientes."
  992:         )
  993: 
  994:     if invoice.duplicate_status == "STRONG":
  995:         raise ValueError(
  996:             "La factura coincide con otro CIF y "
  997:             "número de factura. Debes resolver el "
  998:             "posible duplicado antes de aprobar."
  999:         )
 1000: 
 1001:     invoice.review_status = "APPROVED"
 1002:     invoice.approved_at = utc_now()
 1003:     invoice.rejected_at = None
 1004:     invoice.rejection_reason = None
 1005: 
 1006:     update_document_status(
 1007:         invoice.document,
 1008:         invoice,
 1009:     )
 1010: 
 1011:     add_audit_event(
 1012:         database,
 1013:         action="invoice.approved",
 1014:         entity_type="invoice",
 1015:         entity_id=invoice.id,
 1016:         actor=actor,
 1017:         event_data={
 1018:             "document_id": (
 1019:                 invoice.document_id
 1020:             ),
 1021:             "total": invoice.total,
 1022:             "validation_status": (
 1023:                 invoice.validation_status
 1024:             ),
 1025:         },
 1026:     )
 1027: 
 1028:     database.commit()
 1029:     database.refresh(invoice)
 1030: 
 1031:     return invoice
 1032: 
 1033: 
 1034: def reject_invoice(
 1035:     database: Session,
 1036:     *,
 1037:     invoice: Invoice,
 1038:     reason: str,
 1039:     actor: str = "user",
 1040: ) -> Invoice:
 1041:     invoice.review_status = "REJECTED"
 1042:     invoice.rejected_at = utc_now()
 1043:     invoice.approved_at = None
 1044:     invoice.rejection_reason = reason
 1045: 
 1046:     update_document_status(
 1047:         invoice.document,
 1048:         invoice,
 1049:     )
 1050: 
 1051:     add_audit_event(
 1052:         database,
 1053:         action="invoice.rejected",
 1054:         entity_type="invoice",
 1055:         entity_id=invoice.id,
 1056:         actor=actor,
 1057:         event_data={
 1058:             "document_id": (
 1059:                 invoice.document_id
 1060:             ),
 1061:             "reason": reason,
 1062:         },
 1063:     )
 1064: 
 1065:     database.commit()
 1066:     database.refresh(invoice)
 1067: 
 1068:     return invoice
 1069: 
 1070: def normalize_invoice_number(
 1071:     value: str | None,
 1072: ) -> str | None:
 1073:     if not value:
 1074:         return None
 1075: 
 1076:     normalized = value.strip().upper()
 1077:     normalized = " ".join(normalized.split())
 1078: 
 1079:     return normalized or None

   49:     @property

   54:             normalized = extension.strip().lower()

   57:                 continue
   58: 
   59:             if not normalized.startswith("."):

   61:

   64:         return extensions

   76: def format_eur(value: float) -> str:

   85: def load_connectors() -> list[dict]:
   86:     if not CONNECTORS_FILE.exists():
   87:         return []
   88: 
   89:     try:
   90:         with CONNECTORS_FILE.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
   91:             data = json.load(file)
   92: 
   93:         return data if isinstance(data, list) else []
   94:     except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError):
   95:         return []
   96: 
   97: 
   98: def save_connectors(connectors: list[dict]) -> None:
   99:     """
  100:     Escritura atómica para no dejar connectors.json incompleto.
  101:     """
  102:     CONNECTORS_FILE.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
  103: 
  104:     fd, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
  105:         prefix="connectors_",
  106:         suffix=".tmp",
  107:         dir=str(CONNECTORS_FILE.parent),
  108:     )
  109: 
  110:     try:
  111:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as temporary_file:
  112:             json.dump(
  113:                 connectors,
  114:                 temporary_file,
  115:                 ensure_ascii=False,
  116:                 indent=2,
  117:             )
  118:             temporary_file.flush()
  119:             os.fsync(temporary_file.fileno())
  120: 
  121:         os.replace(temporary_name, CONNECTORS_FILE)
  122: 
  123:     except Exception:
  124:         try:
  125:             os.unlink(temporary_name)
  126:         except OSError:
  127:             pass
  128: 
  129:         raise

  140: def find_existing_document(

  148:     Para datos antiguos que todavía no tengan source_key, utiliza
  149:     el nombre de archivo como mecanismo de compatibilidad.

  151:     for document in documents:
  152:         if document.get("source_key") == source_key:
  153:             return document

  156:         if (
  157:             document.get("source") == "correo"
  158:             and document.get("filename") == filename

  160:             return document

  179:             "La base más el IVA no coincide con el total."

  183:             "entre base, IVA y total."
  184:         )
  185:     else:

  187:             "Factura importada y clasificada automáticamente. "

  190:         reason = (
  191:             "Proveedor reconocido e importes correctamente cuadrados."
  192:         )

  243: def update_outlook_connector(
  244:     documents: list[dict],
  245:     imported_count: int,
  246: ) -> None:
  247:     connectors = load_connectors()
  248: 
  249:     outlook = next(
  250:         (
  251:             connector
  252:             for connector in connectors
  253:             if connector.get("provider") == "Outlook"
  254:         ),
  255:         None,
  256:     )
  257: 
  258:     if outlook is None:
  259:         outlook = {
  260:             "provider": "Outlook",
  261:             "status": "Simulado",
  262:             "last_sync": None,
  263:             "documents_found": 0,
  264:         }
  265:         connectors.append(outlook)
  266: 
  267:     email_documents = [
  268:         document
  269:         for document in documents
  270:         if document.get("source") == "correo"
  271:         and document.get("source_provider", "Outlook") == "Outlook"
  272:     ]
  273: 
  274:     outlook["status"] = "Simulado"
  275:     outlook["last_sync"] = datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y %H:%M")
  276:     outlook["documents_found"] = len(email_documents)
  277:     outlook["last_imported"] = imported_count
  278:     outlook["is_demo"] = True
  279: 
  280:     if not any(
  281:         connector.get("provider") == "Gmail"
  282:         for connector in connectors
  283:     ):
  284:         connectors.append(
  285:             {
  286:                 "provider": "Gmail",
  287:                 "status": "No disponible",
  288:                 "last_sync": None,
  289:                 "documents_found": 0,
  290:                 "is_demo": False,
  291:             }
  292:         )
  293: 
  294:     manual = next(
  295:         (
  296:             connector
  297:             for connector in connectors
  298:             if connector.get("provider") == "Carga manual"
  299:         ),
  300:         None,
  301:     )
  302: 
  303:     if manual is None:
  304:         manual = {
  305:             "provider": "Carga manual",
  306:             "status": "Activo",
  307:             "last_sync": None,
  308:             "documents_found": 0,
  309:             "is_demo": False,
  310:         }
  311:         connectors.append(manual)
  312: 
  313:     manual["documents_found"] = len(
  314:         [
  315:             document
  316:             for document in documents
  317:             if document.get("source") == "subida manual"
  318:         ]
  319:     )
  320: 
  321:     save_connectors(connectors)
  322: 
  323: 
  324: def sync_demo_emails() -> dict:
  325:     """
  326:     Sincronización idempotente.
  327: 
  328:     Ejecutarla varias veces no vuelve a insertar las mismas facturas.
  329:     """
  330:     documents = load_documents()
  331: 
  332:     imported_count = 0
  333:     skipped_count = 0
  334:     metadata_updated = False
  335: 
  336:     for invoice in DEMO_INVOICES:
  337:         source_key = get_source_key(invoice)
  338: 
  339:         existing = find_existing_document(
  340:             documents,
  341:             source_key,
  342:             invoice["filename"],
  343:         )
  344: 
  345:         if existing:
  346:             skipped_count += 1
  347: 
  348:             # Compatibilidad con documentos antiguos.
  349:             if not existing.get("source_key"):
  350:                 existing["source_key"] = source_key
  351:                 existing["source_hash"] = get_source_hash(source_key)
  352:                 existing["source_provider"] = "Outlook"
  353:                 existing["external_id"] = invoice["external_id"]
  354:                 metadata_updated = True
  355: 
  356:             continue
  357: 
  358:         document_id = next_document_id(documents)
  359: 
  360:         document = build_demo_document(
  361:             invoice,
  362:             document_id,
  363:         )
  364: 
  365:         documents.append(document)
  366:         imported_count += 1
  367: 
  368:         add_audit_event(
  369:             action="document.created",
  370:             entity_type="document",
  371:             entity_id=document_id,
  372:             actor="connector.outlook.demo",
  373:             metadata={
  374:                 "filename": document["filename"],
  375:                 "source_key": document["source_key"],
  376:                 "supplier": document["supplier"],
  377:             },
  378:         )
  379: 
  380:         add_audit_event(
  381:             action="document.classified",
  382:             entity_type="document",
  383:             entity_id=document_id,
  384:             actor="agent",
  385:             metadata={
  386:                 "confidence": document["confidence"],
  387:                 "risk": document["risk"],
  388:                 "status": document["status"],
  389:             },
  390:         )
  391: 
  392:     if imported_count > 0 or metadata_updated:
  393:         save_documents(documents)
  394: 
  395:     update_outlook_connector(
  396:         documents,
  397:         imported_count,
  398:     )
  399: 
  400:     current_time = datetime.now().strftime("%H:%M")
  401: 
  402:     if imported_count > 0:
  403:         add_activity(
  404:             current_time,
  405:             (
  406:                 f"Outlook simulado · {imported_count} factura(s) "
  407:                 "nueva(s) importada(s)"
  408:             ),
  409:         )
  410:     else:
  411:         add_activity(
  412:             current_time,
  413:             "Outlook simulado · sincronización completada sin novedades",
  414:         )
  415: 
  416:     add_audit_event(
  417:         action="connector.sync.completed",
  418:         entity_type="connector",
  419:         entity_id="outlook-demo",
  420:         actor="user",
  421:         metadata={
  422:             "imported": imported_count,
  423:             "skipped": skipped_count,
  424:             "mode": "demo",
  425:         },
  426:     )
  427: 
  428:     return {
  429:         "ok": True,
  430:         "count": imported_count,
  431:         "skipped": skipped_count,
  432:         "mode": "demo",
  433:     }

   23:     pytesseract = None
   24:     Image = None
   25:

   27: EXTRACTOR_NAME = "capafiscal.generic_invoice"
   28: EXTRACTOR_VERSION = "2.1.0"
   29: 
   30: CENT = Decimal("0.01")
   31: AMOUNT_TOLERANCE = Decimal("0.03")

   33: MONEY_TOKEN_PATTERN = re.compile(
   34:     r"""
   35:     (?<![A-Za-z0-9])
   36:     \(?
   37:     [-+]?
   38:     \.?
   39:     \d{1,3}
   40:     (?:
   41:         [.\s,]\d{3}
   42:     )*
   43:     (?:
   44:         [.,]\d{2,4}
   45:     )?
   46:     \)?
   47:     \s*(?:€|EUR)?
   48:     (?![A-Za-z0-9])
   49:     """,
   50:     re.IGNORECASE | re.VERBOSE,
   51: )
   52: 
   53: TAX_ID_PATTERN = re.compile(
   54:     r"""
   55:     (?<![A-Z0-9])
   56:     (?:ES[\s\-]?)?
   57:     (?:
   58:         [ABCDEFGHJNPQRSUVW][\s.\-]*\d[\d\s.\-]{5,10}[0-9A-J]
   59:         |
   60:         \d{1,2}(?:[\s.\-]?\d{3}){2}[\s.\-]?[A-Z]
   61:         |
   62:         \d{8}[\s.\-]?[A-Z]
   63:         |
   64:         [XYZ][\s.\-]?\d{7}[\s.\-]?[A-Z]
   65:     )
   66:     (?![A-Z0-9])
   67:     """,
   68:     re.IGNORECASE | re.VERBOSE,
   69: )
   70: 
   71: DATE_PATTERN = re.compile(
   72:     r"\b("
   73:     r"\d{1,2}[/-]\d{1,2}[/-]\d{2,4}"
   74:     r"|"
   75:     r"\d{4}[/-]\d{1,2}[/-]\d{1,2}"
   76:     r")\b"
   77: )
   78: 
   79: INVOICE_NUMBER_PATTERNS = (

   81:         r"(?:factura|fra\.?)\s*"

   83:         r"\s*[:#\-]?\s*"
   84:         r"([A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9 ./_-]{1,30})",
   85:         re.IGNORECASE,

   90:         r"\s*[:#\-]?\s*"
   91:         r"([A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9 ./_-]{1,30})",
   92:         re.IGNORECASE,

   94: )

  175: 
  176: def clean_text(value: str | None) -> str:
  177:     if not value:
  178:         return ""
  179: 
  180:     value = value.replace("\x00", " ")
  181:     value = value.replace("\u00a0", " ")
  182:     value = value.replace("\r\n", "\n")
  183:     value = value.replace("\r", "\n")
  184: 
  185:     cleaned_lines: list[str] = []
  186: 
  187:     for line in value.splitlines():
  188:         line = re.sub(r"[ \t]+", " ", line).strip()
  189: 
  190:         if line:
  191:             cleaned_lines.append(line)
  192: 
  193:     return "\n".join(cleaned_lines)
  194: 
  195: 
  196: def normalize_search_text(value: str) -> str:
  197:     normalized = unicodedata.normalize("NFKD", value)
  198: 
  199:     normalized = "".join(
  200:         character
  201:         for character in normalized
  202:         if not unicodedata.combining(character)
  203:     )
  204: 
  205:     return normalized.lower()
  206: 
  207: 
  208: def get_lines(text: str) -> list[str]:
  209:     return [
  210:         line.strip()
  211:         for line in text.splitlines()
  212:         if line.strip()
  213:     ]
  214: 
  215: 
  216: def normalize_tax_id(value: str | None) -> str | None:
  217:     if not value:
  218:         return None
  219: 
  220:     normalized = re.sub(
  221:         r"[^A-Z0-9]",
  222:         "",
  223:         value.upper(),
  224:     )
  225: 
  226:     if normalized.startswith("ES") and len(normalized) > 9:
  227:         normalized = normalized[2:]
  228: 
  229:     return normalized or None
  230: 
  231: 
  232: def is_valid_spanish_tax_id(value: str | None) -> bool:
  233:     tax_id = normalize_tax_id(value)
  234: 
  235:     if not tax_id:
  236:         return False
  237: 
  238:     if re.fullmatch(r"\d{8}[A-Z]", tax_id):
  239:         letters = "TRWAGMYFPDXBNJZSQVHLCKE"
  240:         return letters[int(tax_id[:8]) % 23] == tax_id[-1]
  241: 
  242:     if re.fullmatch(r"[XYZ]\d{7}[A-Z]", tax_id):
  243:         prefix = {
  244:             "X": "0",
  245:             "Y": "1",
  246:             "Z": "2",
  247:         }[tax_id[0]]
  248: 
  249:         number = int(prefix + tax_id[1:8])
  250:         letters = "TRWAGMYFPDXBNJZSQVHLCKE"
  251:         return letters[number % 23] == tax_id[-1]
  252: 
  253:     if re.fullmatch(r"[ABCDEFGHJNPQRSUVW]\d{7}[0-9A-J]", tax_id):
  254:         digits = [int(character) for character in tax_id[1:8]]
  255: 
  256:         even_sum = digits[1] + digits[3] + digits[5]
  257:         odd_sum = 0
  258: 
  259:         for digit in (digits[0], digits[2], digits[4], digits[6]):
  260:             doubled = digit * 2
  261:             odd_sum += doubled // 10 + doubled % 10
  262: 
  263:         control_number = (10 - ((even_sum + odd_sum) % 10)) % 10
  264:         control_letter = "JABCDEFGHI"[control_number]
  265:         expected_control = tax_id[-1]
  266: 
  267:         if tax_id[0] in "PQRSNW":
  268:             return expected_control == control_letter
  269: 
  270:         if tax_id[0] in "ABEH":
  271:             return expected_control == str(control_number)
  272: 
  273:         return expected_control in {
  274:             str(control_number),
  275:             control_letter,
  276:         }
  277: 
  278:     return False
  279: 
  280: 
  281: def normalize_amount(
  282:     raw_value: str | int | float | Decimal | None,
  283: ) -> Decimal | None:
  284:     if raw_value is None:
  285:         return None
  286: 
  287:     if isinstance(raw_value, Decimal):
  288:         return raw_value.quantize(CENT, rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP)
  289: 
  290:     if isinstance(raw_value, (int, float)):
  291:         return Decimal(str(raw_value)).quantize(
  292:             CENT,
  293:             rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
  294:         )
  295: 
  296:     cleaned = str(raw_value).strip()
  297: 
  298:     if not cleaned:
  299:         return None
  300: 
  301:     negative = False
  302: 
  303:     if cleaned.startswith("(") and cleaned.endswith(")"):
  304:         negative = True
  305:         cleaned = cleaned[1:-1]
  306: 
  307:     if cleaned.startswith("-"):
  308:         negative = True
  309: 
  310:     cleaned = re.sub(
  311:         r"(?i)(EUR|EUROS?)",
  312:         "",
  313:         cleaned,
  314:     )
  315:     cleaned = cleaned.replace("€", "")
  316:     cleaned = cleaned.replace("\u00a0", "")
  317:     cleaned = cleaned.replace(" ", "")
  318:     cleaned = cleaned.strip("+-()")
  319: 
  320:     # Algunos PDF producen importes como .1.177,77
  321:     cleaned = re.sub(r"^\.(?=\d)", "", cleaned)
  322: 
  323:     if not cleaned:
  324:         return None
  325: 
  326:     separator_positions = [
  327:         index
  328:         for index, character in enumerate(cleaned)
  329:         if character in ".,"
  330:     ]
  331: 
  332:     decimal_position: int | None = None
  333: 
  334:     if separator_positions:
  335:         last_position = separator_positions[-1]
  336:         final_digits = cleaned[last_position + 1:]
  337: 
  338:         # En facturas, dos decimales es el caso prioritario.
  339:         if final_digits.isdigit() and len(final_digits) == 2:
  340:             decimal_position = last_position
  341:         elif (
  342:             len(separator_positions) == 1
  343:             and final_digits.isdigit()
  344:             and len(final_digits) in {1, 3, 4}
  345:         ):
  346:             decimal_position = last_position
  347: 
  348:     if decimal_position is not None:
  349:         integer_part = re.sub(
  350:             r"[^\d]",
  351:             "",
  352:             cleaned[:decimal_position],
  353:         )
  354:         decimal_part = re.sub(
  355:             r"[^\d]",
  356:             "",
  357:             cleaned[decimal_position + 1:],
  358:         )
  359: 
  360:         if not integer_part:
  361:             integer_part = "0"
  362: 
  363:         normalized = f"{integer_part}.{decimal_part}"
  364:     else:
  365:         normalized = re.sub(r"[^\d]", "", cleaned)
  366: 
  367:     if not normalized:
  368:         return None
  369: 
  370:     try:
  371:         amount = Decimal(normalized)
  372: 
  373:         if negative:
  374:             amount = -amount
  375: 
  376:         return amount.quantize(
  377:             CENT,
  378:             rounding=ROUND_HALF_UP,
  379:         )
  380:     except InvalidOperation:
  381:         return None
  382: 
  383: 
  384: def decimal_to_string(value: Decimal | None) -> str | None:
  385:     if value is None:
  386:         return None
  387: 
  388:     return format(value, ".2f")
  389: 
  390: 
  391: def parse_date_value(raw_value: str | None) -> date | None:
  392:     if not raw_value:
  393:         return None
  394: 
  395:     value = raw_value.strip()
  396: 
  397:     formats = (
  398:         "%d/%m/%Y",
  399:         "%d-%m-%Y",
  400:         "%d/%m/%y",
  401:         "%d-%m-%y",
  402:         "%Y/%m/%d",
  403:         "%Y-%m-%d",
  404:     )
  405: 
  406:     for date_format in formats:
  407:         try:
  408:             parsed = datetime.strptime(value, date_format).date()
  409: 
  410:             if 1990 <= parsed.year <= datetime.now().year + 5:
  411:                 return parsed
  412:         except ValueError:
  413:             continue
  414: 
  415:     return None
  416: 
  417: 
  418: def date_to_string(value: date | None) -> str | None:
  419:     return value.isoformat() if value else None
  420: 
  421: 
  422: def extract_page_with_pdfplumber(page: Any) -> str:
  423:     try:
  424:         return page.extract_text(
  425:             x_tolerance=2,
  426:             y_tolerance=3,
  427:             layout=False,
  428:         ) or ""
  429:     except Exception:
  430:         return ""
  431: 
  432: def extract_page_with_ocr(
  433:     page: Any,
  434: ) -> str:
  435:     """
  436:     Ejecuta OCR sobre una página de PyMuPDF.
  437: 
  438:     Solo se utiliza cuando la página no contiene suficiente texto
  439:     seleccionable.
  440:     """
  441:     if pytesseract is None or Image is None:
  442:         return ""
  443: 
  444:     try:
  445:         matrix = fitz.Matrix(2.5, 2.5)
  446:         pixmap = page.get_pixmap(
  447:             matrix=matrix,
  448:             alpha=False,
  449:         )
  450: 
  451:         image = Image.open(
  452:             io.BytesIO(
  453:                 pixmap.tobytes("png")
  454:             )
  455:         )
  456: 
  457:         try:
  458:             text = pytesseract.image_to_string(
  459:                 image,
  460:                 lang="spa+eng",
  461:                 config="--psm 6",
  462:             )
  463:         except pytesseract.TesseractError:
  464:             text = pytesseract.image_to_string(
  465:                 image,
  466:                 config="--psm 6",
  467:             )
  468: 
  469:         return clean_text(text)
  470: 
  471:     except Exception:
  472:         return ""
  473: 
  474: def read_document(
  475:     path: Path,
  476: ) -> tuple[str, int | None, bool, list[str]]:
  477:     suffix = path.suffix.lower()
  478: 
  479:     if suffix == ".txt":
  480:         text = clean_text(
  481:             path.read_text(
  482:                 encoding="utf-8",
  483:                 errors="ignore",
  484:             )
  485:         )
  486:         return text, 1, not bool(text), [text]
  487: 
  488:     if suffix != ".pdf":
  489:         return "", None, False, []
  490: 
  491:     page_texts: list[str] = []
  492:     page_count: int | None = None
  493: 
  494:     # Primera opción: PyMuPDF.
  495:     if fitz is not None:
  496:         try:
  497:             with fitz.open(path) as document:
  498:                 page_count = len(document)
  499: 
  500:                 for page in document:
  501:                     page_text = clean_text(
  502:                         page.get_text(
  503:                             "text",
  504:                             sort=True,
  505:                         )
  506:                     )
  507: 
  508:                     # Si la página no tiene texto suficiente,
  509:                     # intenta OCR.
  510:                     if len(page_text.strip()) < 20:
  511:                         ocr_text = extract_page_with_ocr(page)
  512: 
  513:                         if len(ocr_text) > len(page_text):
  514:                             page_text = ocr_text
  515: 
  516:                     page_texts.append(page_text)
  517: 
  518:         except Exception:
  519:             page_texts = []
  520: 
  521:     # Segunda opción: pdfplumber.
  522:     try:
  523:         with pdfplumber.open(path) as pdf:
  524:             if page_count is None:
  525:                 page_count = len(pdf.pages)
  526: 
  527:             for index, page in enumerate(pdf.pages):
  528:                 plumber_text = clean_text(
  529:                     extract_page_with_pdfplumber(page)
  530:                 )
  531: 
  532:                 if index >= len(page_texts):
  533:                     page_texts.append(plumber_text)
  534:                     continue
  535: 
  536:                 # Conserva el resultado que tenga más información.
  537:                 if len(plumber_text) > len(page_texts[index]) * 1.10:
  538:                     page_texts[index] = plumber_text
  539: 
  540:     except Exception:
  541:         pass
  542: 
  543:     if page_count is None:
  544:         page_count = len(page_texts)
  545: 
  546:     while len(page_texts) < page_count:
  547:         page_texts.append("")
  548: 
  549:     non_empty_pages = [
  550:         page_text
  551:         for page_text in page_texts
  552:         if len(page_text.strip()) >= 20
  553:     ]
  554: 
  555:     complete_text = clean_text(
  556:         "\n".join(page_texts)
  557:     )
  558: 
  559:     requires_ocr = not bool(non_empty_pages)
  560: 
  561:     return (
  562:         complete_text,
  563:         page_count,
  564:         requires_ocr,
  565:         page_texts,
  566:     )
  567: 
  568: 
  569: def money_values(line: str) -> list[tuple[Decimal, str]]:
  570:     values: list[tuple[Decimal, str]] = []
  571: 
  572:     # Evita interpretar porcentajes como importes.
  573:     sanitized = re.sub(
  574:         r"\b\d{1,2}(?:[,.]\d+)?\s*%",
  575:         " ",
  576:         line,
  577:     )
  578: 
  579:     for match in MONEY_TOKEN_PATTERN.finditer(sanitized):
  580:         raw_value = match.group(0).strip()
  581:         amount = normalize_amount(raw_value)
  582: 
  583:         if amount is None:
  584:             continue
  585: 
  586:         values.append((amount, raw_value))
  587: 
  588:     return values
  589: 
  590: 
  591: def normalized_company_tax_ids(
  592:     company_tax_id: Any,
  593: ) -> set[str]:
  594:     if not company_tax_id:
  595:         return set()
  596: 
  597:     if isinstance(company_tax_id, str):
  598:         raw_values = re.split(r"[,;|]", company_tax_id)
  599:     elif isinstance(company_tax_id, (list, tuple, set)):
  600:         raw_values = list(company_tax_id)
  601:     else:
  602:         raw_values = [str(company_tax_id)]
  603: 
  604:     result: set[str] = set()
  605: 
  606:     for raw_value in raw_values:
  607:         normalized = normalize_tax_id(str(raw_value))
  608:         if normalized:
  609:             result.add(normalized)
  610: 
  611:     return result
  612: 
  613: 
  614: def raw_invoice_numbers(text: str) -> list[str]:
  615:     numbers: list[str] = []
  616: 
  617:     for pattern in INVOICE_NUMBER_PATTERNS:
  618:         for match in pattern.finditer(text):
  619:             candidate = clean_invoice_number(match.group(1))
  620: 
  621:             if candidate and candidate not in numbers:
  622:                 numbers.append(candidate)
  623: 
  624:     return numbers
  625: 
  626: 
  627: def clean_invoice_number(value: str | None) -> str | None:
  628:     if not value:
  629:         return None
  630: 
  631:     candidate = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", value)
  632:     candidate = candidate.strip(" .,:;#-")
  633: 
  634:     # El patrón puede capturar texto posterior.
  635:     candidate = re.split(
  636:         r"\b(?:fecha|cliente|cif|nif|pagina|página)\b",
  637:         candidate,
  638:         maxsplit=1,
  639:         flags=re.IGNORECASE,
  640:     )[0].strip(" .,:;-")
  641: 
  642:     if len(candidate) < 3 or len(candidate) > 30:
  643:         return None
  644: 
  645:     if not re.search(r"\d", candidate):
  646:         return None
  647: 
  648:     if re.fullmatch(r"\d{1,2}", candidate):
  649:         return None
  650: 
  651:     return candidate.upper()
  652: 
  653: 
  654: def select_primary_pages(page_texts: list[str]) -> tuple[str, list[int]]:
  655:     if len(page_texts) <= 1:
  656:         return clean_text("\n".join(page_texts)), [1]
  657: 
  658:     selected: list[str] = []
  659:     selected_indexes: list[int] = []
  660: 
  661:     first_page = page_texts[0]
  662:     first_numbers = raw_invoice_numbers(first_page)
  663:     primary_number = first_numbers[0] if first_numbers else None
  664: 
  665:     first_has_summary = (
  666:         bool(find_labeled_amount(first_page, "total", allow_inference=False).value)
  667:         and (
  668:             "base imponible" in normalize_search_text(first_page)
  669:             or "subtotal" in normalize_search_text(first_page)
  670:         )
  671:     )
  672: 
  673:     selected.append(first_page)
  674:     selected_indexes.append(1)
  675: 
  676:     for page_index, page_text in enumerate(page_texts[1:], start=2):
  677:         normalized_page = normalize_search_text(page_text)
  678:         page_numbers = raw_invoice_numbers(page_text)
  679: 
  680:         repeats_primary = (
  681:             primary_number is not None
  682:             and any(
  683:                 number.replace(" ", "") == primary_number.replace(" ", "")
  684:                 for number in page_numbers
  685:             )
  686:         )
  687: 
  688:         looks_like_new_invoice = (
  689:             bool(page_numbers)
  690:             and primary_number is not None
  691:             and not repeats_primary
  692:             and "factura" in normalized_page
  693:             and (
  694:                 "base" in normalized_page
  695:                 or "iva" in normalized_page
  696:                 or "total" in normalized_page
  697:             )
  698:         )
  699: 
  700:         # Si la primera factura ya está completa y la siguiente página
  701:         # empieza otra factura, se trata como anexo.
  702:         if first_has_summary and looks_like_new_invoice:
  703:             break
  704: 
  705:         selected.append(page_text)
  706:         selected_indexes.append(page_index)
  707: 
  708:     return clean_text("\n".join(selected)), selected_indexes
  709: 
  710: 
  711: def find_tax_id_candidates(
  712:     text: str,
  713: ) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
  714:     lines = get_lines(text)
  715:     candidates: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  716:     seen: set[tuple[str, int]] = set()
  717: 
  718:     for line_index, line in enumerate(lines):
  719:         for match in TAX_ID_PATTERN.finditer(line):
  720:             tax_id = normalize_tax_id(match.group(0))
  721: 
  722:             if not tax_id:
  723:                 continue
  724: 
  725:             signature = (tax_id, line_index)
  726: 
  727:             if signature in seen:
  728:                 continue
  729: 
  730:             seen.add(signature)
  731: 
  732:             start = max(0, line_index - 3)
  733:             end = min(len(lines), line_index + 4)
  734:             context = " | ".join(lines[start:end])
  735:             normalized_context = normalize_search_text(context)
  736: 
  737:             role = "unknown"
  738:             confidence = 60
  739: 
  740:             if any(
  741:                 label in normalized_context
  742:                 for label in CUSTOMER_LABELS
  743:             ):
  744:                 role = "customer"
  745:                 confidence = 85
  746:             elif any(
  747:                 label in normalized_context
  748:                 for label in SUPPLIER_LABELS
  749:             ):
  750:                 role = "supplier"
  751:                 confidence = 90
  752:             elif line_index <= max(10, len(lines) // 4):
  753:                 role = "supplier_candidate"
  754:                 confidence = 75
  755: 
  756:             if is_valid_spanish_tax_id(tax_id):
  757:                 confidence += 8
  758:             else:
  759:                 confidence -= 8
  760: 
  761:             candidates.append(
  762:                 {
  763:                     "value": tax_id,
  764:                     "role": role,
  765:                     "confidence": max(20, min(confidence, 98)),
  766:                     "line_index": line_index,
  767:                     "context": context[:600],
  768:                     "valid_checksum": is_valid_spanish_tax_id(tax_id),
  769:                 }
  770:             )
  771: 
  772:     return candidates
  773: 
  774: 
  775: def choose_tax_ids(
  776:     candidates: list[dict[str, Any]],
  777:     company_tax_id: str | None,
  778: ) -> tuple[ExtractedField, ExtractedField]:
  779:     configured_ids = normalized_company_tax_ids(company_tax_id)
  780: 
  781:     supplier = ExtractedField()
  782:     customer = ExtractedField()
  783: 
  784:     configured_candidates = [
  785:         candidate
  786:         for candidate in candidates
  787:         if candidate["value"] in configured_ids
  788:     ]
  789: 
  790:     if configured_candidates:
  791:         selected = configured_candidates[0]
  792:         customer = ExtractedField(
  793:             value=selected["value"],
  794:             confidence=99,
  795:             source="configured_company_tax_id",
  796:             evidence=selected["context"],
  797:         )
  798: 
  799:     supplier_candidates = [
  800:         candidate
  801:         for candidate in candidates
  802:         if candidate["value"] not in configured_ids
  803:     ]
  804: 
  805:     def supplier_score(candidate: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[int, int]:
  806:         score = int(candidate["confidence"])
  807: 
  808:         if candidate["role"] == "supplier":
  809:             score += 25
  810:         elif candidate["role"] == "supplier_candidate":
  811:             score += 12
  812:         elif candidate["role"] == "customer":
  813:             score -= 20
  814: 
  815:         if candidate["valid_checksum"]:
  816:             score += 5
  817: 
  818:         return score, -int(candidate["line_index"])
  819: 
  820:     if supplier_candidates:
  821:         selected = max(
  822:             supplier_candidates,
  823:             key=supplier_score,
  824:         )
  825: 
  826:         supplier = ExtractedField(
  827:             value=selected["value"],
  828:             confidence=min(
  829:                 98,
  830:                 max(45, supplier_score(selected)[0]),
  831:             ),
  832:             source="ranked_supplier_tax_id",
  833:             evidence=selected["context"],
  834:         )
  835: 
  836:     if customer.value is None:
  837:         customer_candidates = [
  838:             candidate
  839:             for candidate in candidates
  840:             if (
  841:                 candidate["role"] == "customer"
  842:                 and candidate["value"] != supplier.value
  843:             )
  844:         ]
  845: 
  846:         if customer_candidates:
  847:             selected = max(
  848:                 customer_candidates,
  849:                 key=lambda item: item["confidence"],
  850:             )
  851: 
  852:             customer = ExtractedField(
  853:                 value=selected["value"],
  854:                 confidence=selected["confidence"],
  855:                 source="customer_tax_id_label",
  856:                 evidence=selected["context"],
  857:             )
  858: 
  859:     return supplier, customer
  860: 
  861: 
  862: def looks_like_company_name(line: str) -> bool:
  863:     cleaned = line.strip(" :-|")
  864:     normalized = normalize_search_text(cleaned)
  865: 
  866:     if len(cleaned) < 3 or len(cleaned) > 140:
  867:         return False
  868: 
  869:     if any(word in normalized for word in NAME_REJECT_WORDS):
  870:         return False
  871: 
  872:     if DATE_PATTERN.search(cleaned):
  873:         return False
  874: 
  875:     if MONEY_TOKEN_PATTERN.fullmatch(cleaned):
  876:         return False
  877: 
  878:     letters = sum(character.isalpha() for character in cleaned)
  879:     digits = sum(character.isdigit() for character in cleaned)
  880: 
  881:     if letters < 4:
  882:         return False
  883: 
  884:     if digits > max(4, letters // 2):
  885:         return False
  886: 
  887:     return True
  888: 
  889: def clean_company_name_candidate(
  890:     line: str,
  891:     tax_id: str | None,
  892: ) -> str:
  893:     candidate = line
  894: 
  895:     if tax_id:
  896:         for match in TAX_ID_PATTERN.finditer(candidate):
  897:             matched_tax_id = normalize_tax_id(
  898:                 match.group(0)
  899:             )
  900: 
  901:             if matched_tax_id == normalize_tax_id(tax_id):
  902:                 candidate = candidate[:match.start()]
  903:                 break
  904: 
  905:     # Recorta información legal posterior al nombre.
  906:     candidate = re.split(
  907:         r"(?i)"
  908:         r"\|\s*C\.?\s*I\.?\s*F\.?"
  909:         r"|\bC\.?\s*I\.?\s*F\.?\s*[:.]"
  910:         r"|\bN\.?\s*I\.?\s*F\.?\s*[:.]"
  911:         r"|\binscrita?\b"
  912:         r"|\bregistro mercantil\b",
  913:         candidate,
  914:         maxsplit=1,
  915:     )[0]
  916: 
  917:     candidate = re.sub(
  918:         r"(?i)\b(?:cif|nif|dni|vat|tax\s*id)\b",
  919:         "",
  920:         candidate,
  921:     )
  922: 
  923:     candidate = candidate.strip(
  924:         " \t:-|,.;"
  925:     )
  926: 
  927:     candidate = re.sub(
  928:         r"\s+",
  929:         " ",
  930:         candidate,
  931:     )
  932: 
  933:     return candidate
  934: 
  935: def company_name_near_tax_id(
  936:     text: str,
  937:     tax_id: str | None,
  938:     role: str,
  939: ) -> ExtractedField:
  940:     if not tax_id:
  941:         return ExtractedField()
  942: 
  943:     lines = get_lines(text)
  944:     normalized_tax_id = normalize_tax_id(tax_id)
  945: 
  946:     scored: list[tuple[int, str, str]] = []
  947: 
  948:     for index, line in enumerate(lines):
  949:         line_tax_ids = [
  950:             normalize_tax_id(match.group(0))
  951:             for match in TAX_ID_PATTERN.finditer(line)
  952:         ]
  953: 
  954:         if normalized_tax_id not in line_tax_ids:
  955:             continue
  956: 
  957:         # La propia línea es prioritaria porque existen formatos:
  958:         # EMPRESA S.L. | C.I.F.: B12345678
  959:         same_line_candidate = clean_company_name_candidate(
  960:             line,
  961:             tax_id,
  962:         )
  963: 
  964:         if looks_like_company_name(
  965:             same_line_candidate
  966:         ):
  967:             score = 96
  968: 
  969:             normalized_candidate = normalize_search_text(
  970:                 same_line_candidate
  971:             )
  972: 
  973:             if any(
  974:                 suffix in normalized_candidate
  975:                 for suffix in LEGAL_SUFFIXES
  976:             ):
  977:                 score += 2
  978: 
  979:             scored.append(
  980:                 (
  981:                     min(score, 99),
  982:                     same_line_candidate,
  983:                     line,
  984:                 )
  985:             )
  986: 
  987:         # Busca también líneas cercanas.
  988:         for offset in (-4, -3, -2, -1, 1, 2):
  989:             candidate_index = index + offset
  990: 
  991:             if not 0 <= candidate_index < len(lines):
  992:                 continue
  993: 
  994:             candidate_line = lines[candidate_index]
  995: 
  996:             candidate = clean_company_name_candidate(
  997:                 candidate_line,
  998:                 None,
  999:             )
 1000: 
 1001:             if not looks_like_company_name(candidate):
 1002:                 continue
 1003: 
 1004:             normalized_candidate = normalize_search_text(
 1005:                 candidate
 1006:             )
 1007: 
 1008:             distance = abs(offset)
 1009:             score = 87 - distance * 6
 1010: 
 1011:             labels = (
 1012:                 SUPPLIER_LABELS
 1013:                 if role == "supplier"
 1014:                 else CUSTOMER_LABELS
 1015:             )
 1016: 
 1017:             context_start = max(
 1018:                 0,
 1019:                 candidate_index - 2,
 1020:             )
 1021:             context_end = min(
 1022:                 len(lines),
 1023:                 candidate_index + 3,
 1024:             )
 1025: 
 1026:             context = " ".join(
 1027:                 lines[context_start:context_end]
 1028:             )
 1029: 
 1030:             normalized_context = normalize_search_text(
 1031:                 context
 1032:             )
 1033: 
 1034:             if any(
 1035:                 label in normalized_context
 1036:                 for label in labels
 1037:             ):
 1038:                 score += 10
 1039: 
 1040:             if any(
 1041:                 suffix in normalized_candidate
 1042:                 for suffix in LEGAL_SUFFIXES
 1043:             ):
 1044:                 score += 8
 1045: 
 1046:             if candidate.isupper():
 1047:                 score += 3
 1048: 
 1049:             scored.append(
 1050:                 (
 1051:                     min(score, 98),
 1052:                     candidate,
 1053:                     candidate_line,
 1054:                 )
 1055:             )
 1056: 
 1057:     if not scored:
 1058:         return ExtractedField()
 1059: 
 1060:     score, value, evidence = max(
 1061:         scored,
 1062:         key=lambda item: item[0],
 1063:     )
 1064: 
 1065:     return ExtractedField(
 1066:         value=value,
 1067:         confidence=score,
 1068:         source=f"{role}_name_near_tax_id",
 1069:         evidence=evidence[:300],
 1070:     )
 1071: 
 1072: 
 1073: def find_invoice_number(
 1074:     text: str,
 1075: ) -> ExtractedField:
 1076:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1077:     candidates: list[tuple[int, str, str]] = []
 1078: 
 1079:     standalone_number_pattern = re.compile(
 1080:         r"^[A-Z0-9]{1,12}"
 1081:         r"(?:[/_-][A-Z0-9]{1,12})+$",
 1082:         re.IGNORECASE,
 1083:     )
 1084: 
 1085:     for line_index, line in enumerate(lines):
 1086:         normalized_line = normalize_search_text(line)
 1087: 
 1088:         has_invoice_label = (
 1089:             "factura" in normalized_line
 1090:             or bool(
 1091:                 re.search(
 1092:                     r"\bfra\.?\b",
 1093:                     normalized_line,
 1094:                 )
 1095:             )
 1096:         )
 1097: 
 1098:         if not has_invoice_label:
 1099:             continue
 1100: 
 1101:         # Etiqueta y número en la misma línea.
 1102:         for pattern in INVOICE_NUMBER_PATTERNS:
 1103:             match = pattern.search(line)
 1104: 
 1105:             if not match:
 1106:                 continue
 1107: 
 1108:             value = clean_invoice_number(
 1109:                 match.group(1)
 1110:             )
 1111: 
 1112:             if value:
 1113:                 candidates.append(
 1114:                     (
 1115:                         97,
 1116:                         value,
 1117:                         line,
 1118:                     )
 1119:                 )
 1120: 
 1121:         # Etiqueta en una línea y número debajo.
 1122:         for offset in range(1, 5):
 1123:             candidate_index = line_index + offset
 1124: 
 1125:             if candidate_index >= len(lines):
 1126:                 break
 1127: 
 1128:             candidate_line = lines[candidate_index].strip()
 1129:             normalized_candidate = normalize_search_text(
 1130:                 candidate_line
 1131:             )
 1132: 
 1133:             if normalized_candidate in {
 1134:                 "fecha",
 1135:                 "fecha factura",
 1136:                 "fecha de factura",
 1137:                 "fecha exped.",
 1138:             }:
 1139:                 continue
 1140: 
 1141:             compact_candidate = re.sub(
 1142:                 r"\s+",
 1143:                 "",
 1144:                 candidate_line,
 1145:             )
 1146: 
 1147:             if standalone_number_pattern.fullmatch(
 1148:                 compact_candidate
 1149:             ):
 1150:                 value = clean_invoice_number(
 1151:                     compact_candidate
 1152:                 )
 1153: 
 1154:                 if value:
 1155:                     score = 96 - offset * 2
 1156: 
 1157:                     candidates.append(
 1158:                         (
 1159:                             score,
 1160:                             value,
 1161:                             f"{line} | {candidate_line}",
 1162:                         )
 1163:                     )
 1164: 
 1165:                     break
 1166: 
 1167:             matches = re.findall(
 1168:                 r"\b[A-Z0-9][A-Z0-9/_\-]{2,24}\b",
 1169:                 candidate_line,
 1170:                 re.IGNORECASE,
 1171:             )
 1172: 
 1173:             for raw_candidate in matches:
 1174:                 value = clean_invoice_number(
 1175:                     raw_candidate
 1176:                 )
 1177: 
 1178:                 if not value:
 1179:                     continue
 1180: 
 1181:                 if DATE_PATTERN.fullmatch(value):
 1182:                     continue
 1183: 
 1184:                 score = 90 - offset * 3
 1185: 
 1186:                 if "/" in value or "-" in value:
 1187:                     score += 4
 1188: 
 1189:                 candidates.append(
 1190:                     (
 1191:                         score,
 1192:                         value,
 1193:                         f"{line} | {candidate_line}",
 1194:                     )
 1195:                 )
 1196: 
 1197:     if not candidates:
 1198:         return ExtractedField()
 1199: 
 1200:     score, value, evidence = max(
 1201:         candidates,
 1202:         key=lambda item: item[0],
 1203:     )
 1204: 
 1205:     return ExtractedField(
 1206:         value=value,
 1207:         confidence=min(score, 99),
 1208:         source="invoice_number_label",
 1209:         evidence=evidence[:300],
 1210:     )
 1211: 
 1212: 
 1213: def find_date_near_labels(
 1214:     text: str,
 1215:     labels: tuple[str, ...],
 1216:     source: str,
 1217: ) -> ExtractedField:
 1218:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1219:     candidates: list[tuple[int, date, str]] = []
 1220: 
 1221:     for line_index, line in enumerate(lines):
 1222:         normalized_line = normalize_search_text(line)
 1223: 
 1224:         if not any(label in normalized_line for label in labels):
 1225:             continue
 1226: 
 1227:         for offset in range(0, 5):
 1228:             candidate_index = line_index + offset
 1229: 
 1230:             if candidate_index >= len(lines):
 1231:                 break
 1232: 
 1233:             candidate_line = lines[candidate_index]
 1234: 
 1235:             for match in DATE_PATTERN.finditer(candidate_line):
 1236:                 parsed = parse_date_value(match.group(1))
 1237: 
 1238:                 if not parsed:
 1239:                     continue
 1240: 
 1241:                 score = 95 - offset * 5
 1242: 
 1243:                 if "vencimiento" in normalized_line:
 1244:                     score += 2
 1245: 
 1246:                 candidates.append(
 1247:                     (
 1248:                         score,
 1249:                         parsed,
 1250:                         f"{line} | {candidate_line}",
 1251:                     )
 1252:                 )
 1253: 
 1254:     if not candidates:
 1255:         return ExtractedField()
 1256: 
 1257:     score, parsed, evidence = max(
 1258:         candidates,
 1259:         key=lambda item: item[0],
 1260:     )
 1261: 
 1262:     return ExtractedField(
 1263:         value=date_to_string(parsed),
 1264:         confidence=min(score, 98),
 1265:         source=source,
 1266:         evidence=evidence[:300],
 1267:     )

 1269: 
 1270: def find_fallback_invoice_date(text: str) -> ExtractedField:
 1271:     candidates: list[date] = []
 1272: 
 1273:     for raw_date in DATE_PATTERN.findall(text):
 1274:         parsed = parse_date_value(raw_date)
 1275: 
 1276:         if parsed:
 1277:             candidates.append(parsed)
 1278: 
 1279:     if not candidates:
 1280:         return ExtractedField()
 1281: 
 1282:     selected = candidates[0]
 1283: 
 1284:     return ExtractedField(
 1285:         value=date_to_string(selected),
 1286:         confidence=55,
 1287:         source="first_plausible_date",
 1288:         evidence=selected.isoformat(),
 1289:     )

 1291: 
 1292: def amount_labels(field_name: str) -> tuple[str, ...]:
 1293:     labels = {
 1294:         "subtotal": (
 1295:             "base imponible",
 1296:             "subtotal",
 1297:             "importe neto",
 1298:             "base iva",
 1299:             "suma parcial",
 1300:             "honorarios",
 1301:         ),
 1302:         "tax_total": (
 1303:             "total iva",
 1304:             "cuota iva",
 1305:             "importe iva",
 1306:             "i.v.a.",
 1307:             "iva 21%",
 1308:             "iva",
 1309:         ),
 1310:         "withholding_total": (
 1311:             "retencion",
 1312:             "retención",
 1313:             "irpf",
 1314:         ),
 1315:         "surcharge_total": (
 1316:             "recargo equivalencia",
 1317:             "recargo de equivalencia",
 1318:             "cuota r.e.",
 1319:         ),
 1320:         "total": (
 1321:             "total a pagar",
 1322:             "total factura",
 1323:             "importe total",
 1324:             "total impuestos incluidos",
 1325:             "total eur",
 1326:             "total:",
 1327:             "total",
 1328:         ),
 1329:     }
 1330: 
 1331:     return labels[field_name]
