 1333: 
 1334: def find_labeled_amount(
 1335:     text: str,
 1336:     field_name: str,
 1337:     *,
 1338:     allow_inference: bool = True,
 1339: ) -> ExtractedField:
 1340:     del allow_inference
 1341: 
 1342:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1343:     labels = amount_labels(field_name)
 1344:     candidates: list[tuple[int, int, Decimal, str]] = []
 1345: 
 1346:     for line_index, line in enumerate(lines):
 1347:         normalized_line = normalize_search_text(line)
 1348: 
 1349:         matching_labels = [
 1350:             label
 1351:             for label in labels
 1352:             if normalize_search_text(label) in normalized_line
 1353:         ]
 1354: 
 1355:         if not matching_labels:
 1356:             continue
 1357: 
 1358:         # Impide que "TOTAL IVA" se interprete como total de factura.
 1359:         if (
 1360:             field_name == "total"
 1361:             and (
 1362:                 "total iva" in normalized_line
 1363:                 or "total impuestos" in normalized_line
 1364:                 and "incluidos" not in normalized_line
 1365:             )
 1366:         ):
 1367:             continue
 1368: 
 1369:         values = money_values(line)
 1370: 
 1371:         if field_name == "tax_total":
 1372:             values = [
 1373:                 item
 1374:                 for item in values
 1375:                 if item[0] <= Decimal("100000000")
 1376:             ]
 1377: 
 1378:         if values:
 1379:             amount, raw_value = values[-1]
 1380: 
 1381:             score = 90
 1382: 
 1383:             if field_name == "total":
 1384:                 if "total a pagar" in normalized_line:
 1385:                     score = 99
 1386:                 elif "total factura" in normalized_line:
 1387:                     score = 98
 1388:                 elif "importe total" in normalized_line:
 1389:                     score = 97
 1390:                 elif "total impuestos incluidos" in normalized_line:
 1391:                     score = 96
 1392: 
 1393:             candidates.append(
 1394:                 (
 1395:                     score,
 1396:                     line_index,
 1397:                     abs(amount)
 1398:                     if field_name == "withholding_total"
 1399:                     else amount,
 1400:                     line,
 1401:                 )
 1402:             )
 1403:             continue
 1404: 
 1405:         # Etiqueta e importe pueden estar separados.
 1406:         for offset in range(1, 5):
 1407:             candidate_index = line_index + offset
 1408: 
 1409:             if candidate_index >= len(lines):
 1410:                 break
 1411: 
 1412:             candidate_line = lines[candidate_index]
 1413:             values = money_values(candidate_line)
 1414: 
 1415:             if not values:
 1416:                 continue
 1417: 
 1418:             # Las líneas puramente monetarias son más fiables.
 1419:             amount, raw_value = values[-1]
 1420:             score = 85 - offset * 5
 1421: 
 1422:             candidates.append(
 1423:                 (
 1424:                     score,
 1425:                     candidate_index,
 1426:                     abs(amount)
 1427:                     if field_name == "withholding_total"
 1428:                     else amount,
 1429:                     f"{line} | {candidate_line}",
 1430:                 )
 1431:             )
 1432:             break
 1433: 
 1434:     if not candidates:
 1435:         return ExtractedField()
 1436: 
 1437:     # En multipágina suele ser más fiable el último resumen.
 1438:     if field_name in {
 1439:         "subtotal",
 1440:         "tax_total",
 1441:         "withholding_total",
 1442:         "surcharge_total",
 1443:         "total",
 1444:     }:
 1445:         selected = max(
 1446:             candidates,
 1447:             key=lambda item: (
 1448:                 item[0],
 1449:                 item[1],
 1450:             ),
 1451:         )
 1452:     else:
 1453:         selected = candidates[-1]
 1454: 
 1455:     score, _, amount, evidence = selected
 1456: 
 1457:     return ExtractedField(
 1458:         value=decimal_to_string(amount),
 1459:         confidence=min(score, 99),
 1460:         source=f"{field_name}_label",
 1461:         evidence=evidence[:300],
 1462:     )
 1463: 
 1464: 
 1465: def extract_stacked_summary(
 1466:     text: str,
 1467: ) -> dict[str, ExtractedField]:
 1468:     """
 1469:     Resuelve bloques como:
 1470: 
 1471:         HONORARIOS
 1472:         21% I.V.A.
 1473:         Retención 15% IRPF
 1474:         TOTAL
 1475:         1.111,11
 1476:         233,33
 1477:         -166,67
 1478:         1.177,77
 1479:     """
 1480:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1481:     result: dict[str, ExtractedField] = {}
 1482: 
 1483:     for start_index in range(len(lines)):
 1484:         labels: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
 1485: 
 1486:         for index in range(start_index, min(len(lines), start_index + 8)):
 1487:             normalized = normalize_search_text(lines[index])
 1488: 
 1489:             field_name: str | None = None
 1490: 
 1491:             if "retencion" in normalized or "irpf" in normalized:
 1492:                 field_name = "withholding_total"
 1493:             elif "iva" in normalized:
 1494:                 field_name = "tax_total"
 1495:             elif (
 1496:                 "base imponible" in normalized
 1497:                 or "subtotal" in normalized
 1498:                 or "honorarios" in normalized
 1499:             ):
 1500:                 field_name = "subtotal"
 1501:             elif normalized == "total" or "total factura" in normalized:
 1502:                 field_name = "total"
 1503: 
 1504:             if field_name and field_name not in [
 1505:                 item[0]
 1506:                 for item in labels
 1507:             ]:
 1508:                 labels.append((field_name, index))
 1509: 
 1510:         if len(labels) < 3:
 1511:             continue
 1512: 
 1513:         search_start = max(index for _, index in labels) + 1
 1514:         amounts: list[tuple[Decimal, str]] = []
 1515: 
 1516:         for index in range(
 1517:             search_start,
 1518:             min(len(lines), search_start + 10),
 1519:         ):
 1520:             values = money_values(lines[index])
 1521: 
 1522:             if len(values) == 1:
 1523:                 amounts.append((values[0][0], lines[index]))
 1524: 
 1525:             if len(amounts) >= len(labels):
 1526:                 break
 1527: 
 1528:         if len(amounts) < len(labels):
 1529:             continue
 1530: 
 1531:         for position, (field_name, _) in enumerate(labels):
 1532:             amount, evidence = amounts[position]
 1533: 
 1534:             if field_name == "withholding_total":
 1535:                 amount = abs(amount)
 1536: 
 1537:             result[field_name] = ExtractedField(
 1538:                 value=decimal_to_string(amount),
 1539:                 confidence=94,
 1540:                 source="stacked_fiscal_summary",
 1541:                 evidence=evidence,
 1542:             )
 1543: 
 1544:         break
 1545: 
 1546:     return result
 1547: 
 1548: 
 1549: def detect_tax_rate(text: str) -> Decimal | None:
 1550:     rates: list[Decimal] = []
 1551: 
 1552:     for match in re.finditer(
 1553:         r"\b(?:IVA|I\.V\.A\.)?\s*"
 1554:         r"(\d{1,2}(?:[,.]\d{1,3})?)\s*%",
 1555:         text,
 1556:         re.IGNORECASE,
 1557:     ):
 1558:         rate = normalize_amount(match.group(1))
 1559: 
 1560:         if rate is not None and Decimal("0") <= rate <= Decimal("30"):
 1561:             rates.append(rate)
 1562: 
 1563:     if Decimal("21.00") in rates:
 1564:         return Decimal("21.00")
 1565: 
 1566:     return rates[0] if rates else None
 1567: 
 1568: 
 1569: def reconcile_amounts(
 1570:     subtotal: ExtractedField,
 1571:     tax_total: ExtractedField,
 1572:     withholding_total: ExtractedField,
 1573:     surcharge_total: ExtractedField,
 1574:     total: ExtractedField,
 1575:     text: str,
 1576: ) -> tuple[
 1577:     ExtractedField,
 1578:     ExtractedField,
 1579:     ExtractedField,
 1580:     ExtractedField,
 1581:     ExtractedField,
 1582: ]:
 1583:     base = normalize_amount(subtotal.value)
 1584:     tax = normalize_amount(tax_total.value)
 1585:     withholding = (
 1586:         normalize_amount(withholding_total.value)
 1587:         or Decimal("0.00")
 1588:     )
 1589:     surcharge = (
 1590:         normalize_amount(surcharge_total.value)
 1591:         or Decimal("0.00")
 1592:     )
 1593:     grand_total = normalize_amount(total.value)
 1594:     rate = detect_tax_rate(text)
 1595: 
 1596:     if (
 1597:         base is not None
 1598:         and grand_total is not None
 1599:         and tax is None
 1600:     ):
 1601:         calculated_tax = (
 1602:             grand_total
 1603:             - base
 1604:             + withholding
 1605:             - surcharge
 1606:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1607: 
 1608:         if calculated_tax >= 0:
 1609:             tax_total = ExtractedField(
 1610:                 value=decimal_to_string(calculated_tax),
 1611:                 confidence=84,
 1612:                 source="arithmetic_total_minus_base",
 1613:                 evidence=(
 1614:                     f"{grand_total} - {base} "
 1615:                     f"+ {withholding} - {surcharge}"
 1616:                 ),
 1617:             )
 1618:             tax = calculated_tax
 1619: 
 1620:     if (
 1621:         tax is not None
 1622:         and grand_total is not None
 1623:         and base is None
 1624:     ):
 1625:         calculated_base = (
 1626:             grand_total
 1627:             - tax
 1628:             + withholding
 1629:             - surcharge
 1630:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1631: 
 1632:         if calculated_base >= 0:
 1633:             subtotal = ExtractedField(
 1634:                 value=decimal_to_string(calculated_base),
 1635:                 confidence=84,
 1636:                 source="arithmetic_total_minus_tax",
 1637:                 evidence=(
 1638:                     f"{grand_total} - {tax} "
 1639:                     f"+ {withholding} - {surcharge}"
 1640:                 ),
 1641:             )
 1642:             base = calculated_base
 1643: 
 1644:     if (
 1645:         base is not None
 1646:         and tax is not None
 1647:         and grand_total is None
 1648:     ):
 1649:         calculated_total = (
 1650:             base
 1651:             + tax
 1652:             + surcharge
 1653:             - withholding
 1654:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1655: 
 1656:         total = ExtractedField(
 1657:             value=decimal_to_string(calculated_total),
 1658:             confidence=82,
 1659:             source="arithmetic_total",
 1660:             evidence=(
 1661:                 f"{base} + {tax} "
 1662:                 f"+ {surcharge} - {withholding}"
 1663:             ),
 1664:         )
 1665:         grand_total = calculated_total
 1666: 
 1667:     # Si solo existe total y un IVA inequívoco, se puede inferir base e IVA.
 1668:     if (
 1669:         grand_total is not None
 1670:         and base is None
 1671:         and tax is None
 1672:         and rate is not None
 1673:         and withholding == 0
 1674:         and surcharge == 0
 1675:     ):
 1676:         divisor = Decimal("1") + rate / Decimal("100")
 1677:         inferred_base = (
 1678:             grand_total / divisor
 1679:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1680:         inferred_tax = (
 1681:             grand_total - inferred_base
 1682:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1683: 
 1684:         if abs(
 1685:             inferred_base
 1686:             + inferred_tax
 1687:             - grand_total
 1688:         ) <= AMOUNT_TOLERANCE:
 1689:             subtotal = ExtractedField(
 1690:                 value=decimal_to_string(inferred_base),
 1691:                 confidence=72,
 1692:                 source="inferred_from_total_and_tax_rate",
 1693:                 evidence=f"Total {grand_total}; IVA {rate}%",
 1694:             )
 1695:             tax_total = ExtractedField(
 1696:                 value=decimal_to_string(inferred_tax),
 1697:                 confidence=72,
 1698:                 source="inferred_from_total_and_tax_rate",
 1699:                 evidence=f"Total {grand_total}; IVA {rate}%",
 1700:             )
 1701: 
 1702:     return (
 1703:         subtotal,
 1704:         tax_total,
 1705:         withholding_total,
 1706:         surcharge_total,
 1707:         total,
 1708:     )
 1709: 
 1710: 
 1711: def extract_tax_lines(
 1712:     text: str,
 1713:     subtotal: ExtractedField,
 1714:     tax_total: ExtractedField,
 1715: ) -> list[TaxLineResult]:
 1716:     base = normalize_amount(subtotal.value)
 1717:     tax = normalize_amount(tax_total.value)
 1718:     rate = detect_tax_rate(text)
 1719: 
 1720:     if base is None or tax is None:
 1721:         return []
 1722: 
 1723:     if rate is None and base != 0:
 1724:         inferred_rate = (
 1725:             tax / base * Decimal("100")
 1726:         ).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
 1727: 
 1728:         common_rates = (
 1729:             Decimal("0.00"),
 1730:             Decimal("4.00"),
 1731:             Decimal("10.00"),
 1732:             Decimal("21.00"),
 1733:         )
 1734: 
 1735:         closest = min(
 1736:             common_rates,
 1737:             key=lambda candidate: abs(candidate - inferred_rate),
 1738:         )
 1739: 
 1740:         if abs(closest - inferred_rate) <= Decimal("0.10"):
 1741:             rate = closest
 1742: 
 1743:     if rate is None:
 1744:         return []
 1745: 
 1746:     expected_tax = (
 1747:         base * rate / Decimal("100")
 1748:     ).quantize(CENT)
 1749: 
 1750:     confidence = (
 1751:         94
 1752:         if abs(expected_tax - tax) <= AMOUNT_TOLERANCE
 1753:         else 72
 1754:     )
 1755: 
 1756:     return [
 1757:         TaxLineResult(
 1758:             tax_type="IVA",
 1759:             tax_rate=decimal_to_string(rate),
 1760:             tax_base=decimal_to_string(base),
 1761:             tax_amount=decimal_to_string(tax),
 1762:             confidence=confidence,
 1763:             source="reconciled_tax_summary",
 1764:         )
 1765:     ]
 1766: 
 1767: 
 1768: def extract_concept(text: str) -> ExtractedField:
 1769:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1770:     start_index: int | None = None
 1771: 
 1772:     for index, line in enumerate(lines):
 1773:         normalized = normalize_search_text(line)
 1774: 
 1775:         if (
 1776:             normalized.startswith("concepto")
 1777:             or normalized.startswith("descripcion")
 1778:             or normalized.startswith("descripción")
 1779:         ):
 1780:             start_index = index + 1
 1781:             break
 1782: 
 1783:     if start_index is None:
 1784:         return ExtractedField()
 1785: 
 1786:     concept_lines: list[str] = []
 1787: 
 1788:     for line in lines[start_index:start_index + 12]:
 1789:         normalized = normalize_search_text(line)
 1790: 
 1791:         if any(
 1792:             stop_label in normalized
 1793:             for stop_label in (
 1794:                 "base imponible",
 1795:                 "total iva",
 1796:                 "forma de pago",
 1797:                 "vencimiento",
 1798:                 "subtotal",
 1799:                 "total factura",
 1800:                 "proteccion de datos",
 1801:             )
 1802:         ):
 1803:             break
 1804: 
 1805:         if money_values(line) and len(line) < 30:
 1806:             continue
 1807: 
 1808:         concept_lines.append(line)
 1809: 
 1810:     concept = " ".join(concept_lines)
 1811:     concept = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", concept).strip()
 1812: 
 1813:     if len(concept) < 5:
 1814:         return ExtractedField()
 1815: 
 1816:     return ExtractedField(
 1817:         value=concept[:1000],
 1818:         confidence=78,
 1819:         source="description_section",
 1820:         evidence=concept[:300],
 1821:     )
 1822: 
 1823: 
 1824: def classify_invoice(
 1825:     text: str,
 1826:     concept: str | None,
 1827: ) -> ExtractedField:
 1828:     normalized = normalize_search_text(
 1829:         f"{text}\n{concept or ''}"
 1830:     )
 1831: 
 1832:     categories = (
 1833:         (
 1834:             "Combustible",
 1835:             (
 1836:                 "gasoleo",
 1837:                 "gasolina",
 1838:                 "euro-super",
 1839:                 "combustible",
 1840:             ),
 1841:         ),
 1842:         (
 1843:             "Servicios profesionales",
 1844:             (
 1845:                 "honorarios",
 1846:                 "arquitecto",
 1847:                 "direccion de obra",
 1848:                 "proyecto",
 1849:                 "ingenieria",
 1850:                 "estudio geotecnico",
 1851:             ),
 1852:         ),
 1853:         (
 1854:             "Informática",
 1855:             (
 1856:                 "ordenador",
 1857:                 "surface",
 1858:                 "openvpn",
 1859:                 "servidor",
 1860:                 "informatica",
 1861:                 "dispositivo",
 1862:             ),
 1863:         ),
 1864:         (
 1865:             "Limpieza",
 1866:             (
 1867:                 "limpieza de mantenimiento",
 1868:                 "servicio de limpieza",
 1869:             ),
 1870:         ),
 1871:         (
 1872:             "Prevención de riesgos laborales",
 1873:             (
 1874:                 "prevencion",
 1875:                 "vigilancia de la salud",
 1876:             ),
 1877:         ),
 1878:         (
 1879:             "Suministros y compras",
 1880:             (
 1881:                 "producto",
 1882:                 "unidades",
 1883:                 "precio unitario",
 1884:             ),
 1885:         ),
 1886:     )
 1887: 
 1888:     for category, keywords in categories:
 1889:         if any(keyword in normalized for keyword in keywords):
 1890:             return ExtractedField(
 1891:                 value=category,
 1892:                 confidence=75,
 1893:                 source="keyword_category",
 1894:                 evidence=", ".join(
 1895:                     keyword
 1896:                     for keyword in keywords
 1897:                     if keyword in normalized
 1898:                 ),
 1899:             )
 1900: 
 1901:     return ExtractedField(
 1902:         value="Otros gastos",
 1903:         confidence=40,
 1904:         source="default_category",
 1905:         evidence=None,
 1906:     )
 1907: 
 1908: 
 1909: def detect_currency(text: str) -> ExtractedField:
 1910:     normalized = normalize_search_text(text)
 1911: 
 1912:     if "€" in text or " eur" in normalized:
 1913:         return ExtractedField(
 1914:             value="EUR",
 1915:             confidence=98,
 1916:             source="currency_symbol",
 1917:             evidence="EUR / €",
 1918:         )
 1919: 
 1920:     if "$" in text or " usd" in normalized:
 1921:         return ExtractedField(
 1922:             value="USD",
 1923:             confidence=85,
 1924:             source="currency_symbol",
 1925:             evidence="USD / $",
 1926:         )
 1927: 
 1928:     if "£" in text or " gbp" in normalized:
 1929:         return ExtractedField(
 1930:             value="GBP",
 1931:             confidence=85,
 1932:             source="currency_symbol",
 1933:             evidence="GBP / £",
 1934:         )
 1935: 
 1936:     return ExtractedField(
 1937:         value="EUR",
 1938:         confidence=45,
 1939:         source="default_currency",
 1940:         evidence=None,
 1941:     )
 1942: 
 1943: 
 1944: def detect_invoice_likelihood(
 1945:     text: str,
 1946:     fields: dict[str, ExtractedField],
 1947: ) -> tuple[bool, int, list[str]]:
 1948:     normalized = normalize_search_text(text)
 1949:     score = 0
 1950:     signals: list[str] = []
 1951: 
 1952:     rules = (
 1953:         (
 1954:             "invoice_keyword",
 1955:             "factura" in normalized,
 1956:             25,
 1957:         ),
 1958:         (
 1959:             "invoice_number",
 1960:             fields["invoice_number"].value is not None,
 1961:             15,
 1962:         ),
 1963:         (
 1964:             "invoice_date",
 1965:             fields["invoice_date"].value is not None,
 1966:             10,
 1967:         ),
 1968:         (
 1969:             "supplier_tax_id",
 1970:             fields["supplier_tax_id"].value is not None,
 1971:             15,
 1972:         ),
 1973:         (
 1974:             "subtotal",
 1975:             fields["subtotal"].value is not None,
 1976:             10,
 1977:         ),
 1978:         (
 1979:             "tax_total",
 1980:             fields["tax_total"].value is not None,
 1981:             10,
 1982:         ),
 1983:         (
 1984:             "total",
 1985:             fields["total"].value is not None,
 1986:             20,
 1987:         ),
 1988:         (
 1989:             "vat_keyword",
 1990:             "iva" in normalized,
 1991:             10,
 1992:         ),
 1993:     )
 1994: 
 1995:     for signal, condition, points in rules:
 1996:         if condition:
 1997:             score += points
 1998:             signals.append(signal)
 1999: 
 2000:     score = min(score, 100)
 2001: 
 2002:     return score >= 45, score, signals
 2003: 
 2004: 
 2005: def calculate_overall_confidence(
 2006:     fields: dict[str, ExtractedField],
 2007:     invoice_likelihood: int,
 2008: ) -> int:
 2009:     important_fields = (
 2010:         "supplier_name",
 2011:         "supplier_tax_id",
 2012:         "invoice_number",
 2013:         "invoice_date",
 2014:         "subtotal",
 2015:         "tax_total",
 2016:         "total",
 2017:     )
 2018: 
 2019:     confidences = [
 2020:         fields[field_name].confidence
 2021:         for field_name in important_fields
 2022:         if fields[field_name].value is not None
 2023:     ]
 2024: 
 2025:     if not confidences:
 2026:         return min(invoice_likelihood, 30)
 2027: 
 2028:     average = sum(confidences) / len(confidences)
 2029:     completeness = len(confidences) / len(important_fields)
 2030: 
 2031:     result = (
 2032:         average * 0.70
 2033:         + invoice_likelihood * 0.20
 2034:         + completeness * 100 * 0.10
 2035:     )
 2036: 
 2037:     return min(int(round(result)), 99)
 2038: 
 2039: 
 2040: def serialize_field(field: ExtractedField) -> dict[str, Any]:
 2041:     return asdict(field)
 2042: 
 2043: 
 2044: def extract_invoice(
 2045:     path: Path,
 2046:     company_tax_id: str | None = None,
 2047:     company_name: str | None = None,
 2048: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
 2049:     (
 2050:         complete_text,
 2051:         page_count,
 2052:         requires_ocr,
 2053:         page_texts,
 2054:     ) = read_document(path)
 2055: 
 2056:     if requires_ocr:
 2057:         return {
 2058:             "extractor_name": EXTRACTOR_NAME,
 2059:             "extractor_version": EXTRACTOR_VERSION,
 2060:             "document_type": "unknown",
 2061:             "is_invoice": False,
 2062:             "invoice_likelihood": 0,
 2063:             "requires_ocr": True,
 2064:             "page_count": page_count,
 2065:             "raw_text": complete_text,
 2066:             "fields": {},
 2067:             "tax_lines": [],
 2068:             "candidates": {
 2069:                 "tax_ids": [],
 2070:             },
 2071:             "signals": [
 2072:                 "ocr_required",
 2073:             ],
 2074:             "overall_confidence": 10,
 2075:         }
 2076: 
 2077:     primary_text, primary_pages = select_primary_pages(
 2078:         page_texts
 2079:     )
 2080: 
 2081:     tax_id_candidates = find_tax_id_candidates(
 2082:         primary_text
 2083:     )
 2084: 
 2085:     (
 2086:         supplier_tax_id,
 2087:         customer_tax_id,
 2088:     ) = choose_tax_ids(
 2089:         tax_id_candidates,
 2090:         company_tax_id,
 2091:     )
 2092: 
 2093:     supplier_name = company_name_near_tax_id(
 2094:         primary_text,
 2095:         supplier_tax_id.value,
 2096:         "supplier",
 2097:     )
 2098: 
 2099:     customer_name = company_name_near_tax_id(
 2100:         primary_text,
 2101:         customer_tax_id.value,
 2102:         "customer",
 2103:     )
 2104: 
 2105:     if customer_tax_id.value and company_name:
 2106:         customer_name = ExtractedField(
 2107:             value=company_name,
 2108:             confidence=99,
 2109:             source="configured_company_name",
 2110:             evidence=customer_name.evidence,
 2111:         )
 2112: 
 2113:     invoice_number = find_invoice_number(
 2114:         primary_text
 2115:     )
 2116: 
 2117:     invoice_date = find_date_near_labels(
 2118:         primary_text,
 2119:         (
 2120:             "fecha factura",
 2121:             "fecha de factura",
 2122:             "fecha exped.",
 2123:             "fecha expedicion",
 2124:             "fecha expedición",
 2125:             "fecha",
 2126:         ),
 2127:         source="invoice_date_label",
 2128:     )
 2129: 
 2130:     if invoice_date.value is None:
 2131:         invoice_date = find_fallback_invoice_date(
 2132:             primary_text
 2133:         )
 2134: 
 2135:     due_date = find_date_near_labels(
 2136:         primary_text,
 2137:         (
 2138:             "fecha de vencimiento",
 2139:             "vencimiento",
 2140:             "vencimientos",
 2141:         ),
 2142:         source="due_date_label",
 2143:     )
 2144: 
 2145:     subtotal = find_labeled_amount(
 2146:         primary_text,
 2147:         "subtotal",
 2148:     )
 2149:     tax_total = find_labeled_amount(
 2150:         primary_text,
 2151:         "tax_total",
 2152:     )
 2153:     withholding_total = find_labeled_amount(
 2154:         primary_text,
 2155:         "withholding_total",
 2156:     )
 2157:     surcharge_total = find_labeled_amount(
 2158:         primary_text,
 2159:         "surcharge_total",
 2160:     )
 2161:     total = find_labeled_amount(
 2162:         primary_text,
 2163:         "total",
 2164:     )
 2165: 
 2166:     stacked = extract_stacked_summary(
 2167:         primary_text
 2168:     )
 2169: 
 2170:     for field_name, stacked_field in stacked.items():
 2171:         if field_name == "subtotal":
 2172:             subtotal = stacked_field
 2173:         elif field_name == "tax_total":
 2174:             tax_total = stacked_field
 2175:         elif field_name == "withholding_total":
 2176:             withholding_total = stacked_field
 2177:         elif field_name == "surcharge_total":
 2178:             surcharge_total = stacked_field
 2179:         elif field_name == "total":
 2180:             total = stacked_field
 2181: 
 2182:     (
 2183:         subtotal,
 2184:         tax_total,
 2185:         withholding_total,
 2186:         surcharge_total,
 2187:         total,
 2188:     ) = reconcile_amounts(
 2189:         subtotal,
 2190:         tax_total,
 2191:         withholding_total,
 2192:         surcharge_total,
 2193:         total,
 2194:         primary_text,
 2195:     )
 2196: 
 2197:     concept = extract_concept(primary_text)
 2198:     category = classify_invoice(
 2199:         primary_text,
 2200:         concept.value,
 2201:     )
 2202:     currency = detect_currency(primary_text)
 2203: 
 2204:     tax_lines = extract_tax_lines(
 2205:         primary_text,
 2206:         subtotal,
 2207:         tax_total,
 2208:     )
 2209: 
 2210:     fields = {
 2211:         "supplier_name": supplier_name,
 2212:         "supplier_tax_id": supplier_tax_id,
 2213:         "customer_name": customer_name,
 2214:         "customer_tax_id": customer_tax_id,
 2215:         "invoice_number": invoice_number,
 2216:         "invoice_date": invoice_date,
 2217:         "due_date": due_date,
 2218:         "subtotal": subtotal,
 2219:         "tax_total": tax_total,
 2220:         "withholding_total": withholding_total,
 2221:         "surcharge_total": surcharge_total,
 2222:         "total": total,
 2223:         "currency": currency,
 2224:         "concept": concept,
 2225:         "category": category,
 2226:     }
 2227: 
 2228:     (
 2229:         is_invoice,
 2230:         invoice_likelihood,
 2231:         signals,
 2232:     ) = detect_invoice_likelihood(
 2233:         primary_text,
 2234:         fields,
 2235:     )
 2236: 
 2237:     if len(primary_pages) < len(page_texts):
 2238:         signals.append("attachments_excluded")
 2239: 
 2240:     overall_confidence = calculate_overall_confidence(
 2241:         fields,
 2242:         invoice_likelihood,
 2243:     )
 2244: 
 2245:     return {
 2246:         "extractor_name": EXTRACTOR_NAME,
 2247:         "extractor_version": EXTRACTOR_VERSION,
 2248:         "document_type": (
 2249:             "invoice"
 2250:             if is_invoice
 2251:             else "unknown"
 2252:         ),
 2253:         "is_invoice": is_invoice,
 2254:         "invoice_likelihood": invoice_likelihood,
 2255:         "requires_ocr": False,
 2256:         "page_count": page_count,
 2257:         "processed_pages": primary_pages,
 2258:         "excluded_pages": [
 2259:             page_number
 2260:             for page_number in range(1, len(page_texts) + 1)
 2261:             if page_number not in primary_pages
 2262:         ],
 2263:         "raw_text": complete_text,
 2264:         "primary_text": primary_text,
 2265:         "fields": {
 2266:             name: serialize_field(field)
 2267:             for name, field in fields.items()
 2268:         },
 2269:         "tax_lines": [
 2270:             asdict(tax_line)
 2271:             for tax_line in tax_lines
 2272:         ],
 2273:         "candidates": {
 2274:             "tax_ids": tax_id_candidates,
 2275:         },
 2276:         "signals": signals,
 2277:         "overall_confidence": overall_confidence,
 2278:     }
 2279: 
 2280: 
 2281: def extract(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
 2282:     """
 2283:     Compatibilidad con módulos anteriores.
 2284:     """
 2285:     result = extract_invoice(path)
 2286:     fields = result.get("fields", {})
 2287: 
 2288:     def value(field_name: str) -> Any:
 2289:         return fields.get(field_name, {}).get("value")
 2290: 
 2291:     return {
 2292:         "raw_text": result.get("raw_text", ""),
 2293:         "raw_text_found": bool(
 2294:             result.get("raw_text", "").strip()
 2295:         ),
 2296:         "needs_ocr": result.get(
 2297:             "requires_ocr",
 2298:             False,
 2299:         ),
 2300:         "supplier": value("supplier_name"),
 2301:         "cif": value("supplier_tax_id"),
 2302:         "concept": value("concept"),
 2303:         "classification": value("category"),
 2304:         "base": normalize_amount(value("subtotal")),
 2305:         "tax": normalize_amount(value("tax_total")),
 2306:         "amount": normalize_amount(value("total")),
 2307:         "date": value("invoice_date"),
 2308:         "invoice_number": value("invoice_number"),
 2309:         "iva_pct": None,
 2310:         "employee": None,
 2311:     }
 2312: 
 2313: 
 2314: def compute_confidence(data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
 2315:     if data.get("needs_ocr"):
 2316:         return 10
 2317: 
 2318:     score = 0
 2319: 
 2320:     if data.get("supplier"):
 2321:         score += 20
 2322:     if data.get("cif"):
 2323:         score += 15
 2324:     if data.get("invoice_number"):
 2325:         score += 15
 2326:     if data.get("date"):
 2327:         score += 10
 2328:     if data.get("base") is not None:
 2329:         score += 10
 2330:     if data.get("tax") is not None:
 2331:         score += 10
 2332:     if data.get("amount") is not None:
 2333:         score += 20
 2334: 
 2335:     return min(score, 99)
 2336: 
 2337: 
 2338: def eur(
 2339:     value: Decimal | float | int | None,
 2340: ) -> str:
 2341:     if value is None:
 2342:         return "-"
 2343: 
 2344:     amount = normalize_amount(value)
 2345: 
 2346:     if amount is None:
 2347:         return "-"
 2348: 
 2349:     formatted = f"{amount:,.2f}"
 2350: 
 2351:     return (
 2352:         formatted
 2353:         .replace(",", "X")
 2354:         .replace(".", ",")
 2355:         .replace("X", ".")
 2356:         + " €"
 2357:     )
 2358: 
 2359: 
 2360: def is_aeat(text: str, filename: str) -> bool:
 2361:     haystack = normalize_search_text(
 2362:         f"{text} {filename}"
 2363:     )
 2364: 
 2365:     keywords = (
 2366:         "agencia tributaria",
 2367:         "aeat",
 2368:         "requerimiento",
 2369:         "providencia de apremio",
 2370:         "diligencia de embargo",
 2371:         "procedimiento sancionador",
 2372:     )
 2373: 
 2374:     return any(
 2375:         keyword in haystack
 2376:         for keyword in keywords
 2377:     )
 2378: 
 2379: 
 2380: def is_laboral(text: str, filename: str) -> bool:
 2381:     haystack = normalize_search_text(
 2382:         f"{text} {filename}"
 2383:     )

   63: def orm_to_dict(instance: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
   64:     """
   65:     Convierte un modelo SQLAlchemy en un diccionario serializable.
   66: 
   67:     Solo incluye columnas reales y excluye relaciones ORM para evitar
   68:     referencias circulares como document.invoice.document.
   69:     """
   70:     if instance is None:
   71:         return None
   72: 
   73:     mapper = sqlalchemy_inspect(instance).mapper
   74: 
   75:     return {
   76:         attribute.key: getattr(instance, attribute.key)
   77:         for attribute in mapper.column_attrs
   78:     }

  221: def validate_file_signature(
  222:     *,
  223:     file_path: Path,
  224:     extension: str,
  225: ) -> None:
  226:     if extension == ".pdf":
  227:         with file_path.open("rb") as file_handle:
  228:             signature = file_handle.read(5)
  229: 
  230:         if signature != b"%PDF-":
  231:             raise HTTPException(
  232:                 status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  233:                 detail=(
  234:                     "El archivo tiene extensión PDF, pero su contenido "
  235:                     "no corresponde a un PDF válido."
  236:                 ),
  237:             )
  238: 
  239:         return
  240: 
  241:     if extension == ".txt":
  242:         with file_path.open("rb") as file_handle:
  243:             sample = file_handle.read(8192)
  244: 
  245:         if b"\x00" in sample:
  246:             raise HTTPException(
  247:                 status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  248:                 detail=(
  249:                     "El archivo TXT contiene datos binarios y no puede "
  250:                     "procesarse como texto."
  251:                 ),
  252:             )
  253: 
  254:         return
  255: 
  256:     raise HTTPException(
  257:         status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  258:         detail=f"El tipo de archivo {extension} no está permitido.",
  259:     )

  371: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  372: 
  373: @app.post(
  374:     "/api/upload",
  375:     response_model=UploadResponse,
  376:     status_code=status.HTTP_201_CREATED,
  377:     tags=["Documentos"],
  378: )
  379: async def upload_document(
  380:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  381:     uploaded_file: UploadFile = File(...),
  382:     actor_header: ActorHeader = None,
  383: ) -> UploadResponse:
  384:     actor = normalize_actor(actor_header)
  385: 
  386:     original_filename = clean_original_filename(
  387:         uploaded_file.filename
  388:     )
  389: 
  390:     extension = Path(original_filename).suffix.lower()
  391: 
  392:     if not extension:
  393:         raise HTTPException(
  394:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  395:             detail="El archivo no tiene extensión.",
  396:         )
  397: 
  398:     if extension not in settings.allowed_extension_set:
  399:         allowed = ", ".join(
  400:             sorted(settings.allowed_extension_set)
  401:         )
  402: 
  403:         raise HTTPException(
  404:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  405:             detail=(
  406:                 f"Extensión no permitida: {extension}. "
  407:                 f"Extensiones permitidas: {allowed}."
  408:             ),
  409:         )
  410: 
  411:     content_type = uploaded_file.content_type
  412: 
  413:     temporary_filename = (
  414:         f".upload-{uuid.uuid4().hex}.tmp"
  415:     )
  416: 
  417:     temporary_path = (
  418:         settings.upload_dir / temporary_filename
  419:     )
  420: 
  421:     sha256 = hashlib.sha256()
  422:     total_size = 0
  423: 
  424:     try:
  425:         with temporary_path.open("wb") as destination:
  426:             while True:
  427:                 chunk = await uploaded_file.read(
  428:                     UPLOAD_CHUNK_SIZE
  429:                 )
  430: 
  431:                 if not chunk:
  432:                     break
  433: 
  434:                 total_size += len(chunk)
  435: 
  436:                 if total_size > settings.max_upload_size:
  437:                     raise HTTPException(
  438:                         status_code=(
  439:                             status.HTTP_413_REQUEST_ENTITY_TOO_LARGE
  440:                         ),
  441:                         detail=(
  442:                             "El archivo supera el tamaño máximo "
  443:                             f"permitido de "
  444:                             f"{settings.max_upload_size} bytes."
  445:                         ),
  446:                     )
  447: 
  448:                 sha256.update(chunk)
  449:                 destination.write(chunk)
  450: 
  451:     except Exception:
  452:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  453:         raise
  454: 
  455:     finally:
  456:         await uploaded_file.close()
  457: 
  458:     if total_size == 0:
  459:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  460: 
  461:         raise HTTPException(
  462:             status_code=status.HTTP_400_BAD_REQUEST,
  463:             detail="El archivo está vacío.",
  464:         )
  465: 
  466:     try:
  467:         validate_file_signature(
  468:             file_path=temporary_path,
  469:             extension=extension,
  470:         )
  471:     except Exception:
  472:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  473:         raise
  474: 
  475:     file_hash = sha256.hexdigest()
  476: 
  477:     duplicate_statement = (
  478:         select(Document.id)
  479:         .where(Document.sha256 == file_hash)
  480:         .limit(1)
  481:     )
  482: 
  483:     duplicate_document_id = database.scalar(
  484:         duplicate_statement
  485:     )
  486: 
  487:     if duplicate_document_id is not None:
  488:         temporary_path.unlink(missing_ok=True)
  489: 
  490:         duplicate_document = get_document(
  491:             database,
  492:             duplicate_document_id,
  493:         )
  494: 
  495:         if duplicate_document is None:
  496:             raise HTTPException(
  497:                 status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
  498:                 detail=(
  499:                     "Se detectó un documento duplicado, "
  500:                     "pero no pudo recuperarse."
  501:                 ),
  502:             )
  503: 
  504:         add_audit_event(
  505:             database,
  506:             action="document.duplicate_upload_attempt",
  507:             entity_type="document",
  508:             entity_id=duplicate_document.id,
  509:             actor=actor,
  510:             event_data={
  511:                 "attempted_filename": original_filename,
  512:                 "sha256": file_hash,
  513:                 "size_bytes": total_size,
  514:             },
  515:         )
  516:         database.commit()
  517: 
  518:         # Si la factura no está aprobada, vuelve a ejecutar la extracción.
  519:         can_reprocess = (
  520:             duplicate_document.invoice is None
  521:             or duplicate_document.invoice.review_status
  522:             != "APPROVED"
  523:         )
  524: 
  525:         if can_reprocess:
  526:             duplicate_file_path = get_document_file_path(
  527:                 duplicate_document
  528:             )
  529: 
  530:             if duplicate_file_path.is_file():
  531:                 try:
  532:                     process_document(
  533:                         database,
  534:                         document=duplicate_document,
  535:                         file_path=duplicate_file_path,
  536:                         actor=actor,
  537:                     )
  538:                 except Exception as error:
  539:                     database.rollback()
  540: 
  541:                     recovered_document = get_document(
  542:                         database,
  543:                         duplicate_document.id,
  544:                     )
  545: 
  546:                     return UploadResponse(
  547:                         success=True,
  548:                         duplicate=True,
  549:                         message=(
  550:                             "El archivo ya existía y se intentó "
  551:                             "reprocesar, pero la extracción falló. "
  552:                             f"Error: {error}"
  553:                         ),
  554:                         document=recovered_document,
  555:                     )
  556: 
  557:         refreshed_document = get_document(
  558:             database,
  559:             duplicate_document.id,
  560:         )
  561: 
  562:         return UploadResponse(
  563:             success=True,
  564:             duplicate=True,
  565:             message=(
  566:                 "El archivo ya existía y se ha vuelto "
  567:                 "a procesar correctamente."
  568:                 if can_reprocess
  569:                 else (
  570:                     "El archivo ya existía. No se ha reprocesado "
  571:                     "porque la factura está aprobada."
  572:                 )
  573:             ),
  574:             document=refreshed_document,
  575:         )
  576: 
  577: 
  578: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  579: # Listado
  580: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  581: 
  582: @app.get(
  583:     "/api/documents",
  584:     response_model=list[DocumentListItem],
  585:     tags=["Documentos"],
  586: )
  587: def list_documents(
  588:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  589:     document_status: str | None = Query(
  590:         default=None,
  591:         alias="status",
  592:     ),
  593:     source: str | None = Query(default=None),
  594:     is_demo: bool | None = Query(default=None),
  595:     offset: int = Query(default=0, ge=0),
  596:     limit: int = Query(default=50, ge=1, le=200),
  597: ) -> list[Document]:
  598:     statement = (
  599:         select(Document)
  600:         .options(
  601:             selectinload(Document.invoice).selectinload(
  602:                 Invoice.tax_lines
  603:             )
  604:         )
  605:         .order_by(
  606:             Document.created_at.desc(),
  607:             Document.id.desc(),
  608:         )
  609:         .offset(offset)
  610:         .limit(limit)
  611:     )
  612: 
  613:     if document_status:
  614:         statement = statement.where(
  615:             Document.status
  616:             == document_status.strip().upper()
  617:         )
  618: 
  619:     if source:
  620:         statement = statement.where(
  621:             Document.source == source.strip()
  622:         )
  623: 
  624:     if is_demo is not None:
  625:         statement = statement.where(
  626:             Document.is_demo.is_(is_demo)
  627:         )
  628: 
  629:     documents = database.scalars(statement).all()
  630: 
  631:     return list(documents)
  632: 
  633:

  638: @app.get(
  639:     "/api/documents/{document_id}",

  641:     tags=["Documentos"],
  642: )
  643: def document_detail(
  644:     document_id: int,
  645:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  646: ) -> Document:
  647:     document = get_document(
  648:         database,
  649:         document_id,
  650:     )
  651: 
  652:     if document is None:
  653:         raise HTTPException(
  654:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  655:             detail="Documento no encontrado.",
  656:         )
  657: 
  658:     return document
  659:

  665: @app.get(
  666:     "/api/documents/{document_id}/file",
  667:     response_class=FileResponse,
  668:     tags=["Documentos"],
  669: )
  670: def document_file(
  671:     document_id: int,
  672:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  673: ) -> FileResponse:
  674:     document = get_document(
  675:         database,
  676:         document_id,
  677:     )
  678: 
  679:     if document is None:
  680:         raise HTTPException(
  681:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  682:             detail="Documento no encontrado.",
  683:         )
  684: 
  685:     file_path = get_document_file_path(document)
  686: 
  687:     if not file_path.is_file():
  688:         raise HTTPException(
  689:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  690:             detail=(
  691:                 "El registro existe, pero el archivo original "
  692:                 "no se encuentra en el disco."
  693:             ),
  694:         )
  695: 
  696:     return FileResponse(
  697:         path=file_path,
  698:         media_type=response_media_type(document),
  699:         filename=document.original_filename,
  700:         content_disposition_type="inline",
  701:     )

  704: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  705: # Reprocesamiento
  706: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  707: 
  708: @app.post(
  709:     "/api/documents/{document_id}/reprocess",
  710:     response_model=ActionResponse,
  711:     tags=["Documentos"],
  712: )
  713: def reprocess_document(
  714:     document_id: int,
  715:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  716:     actor_header: ActorHeader = None,
  717: ) -> ActionResponse:
  718:     actor = normalize_actor(actor_header)
  719: 
  720:     document = get_document(
  721:         database,
  722:         document_id,
  723:     )
  724: 
  725:     if document is None:
  726:         raise HTTPException(
  727:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  728:             detail="Documento no encontrado.",
  729:         )
  730: 
  731:     if document.invoice is not None:
  732:         check_invoice_is_editable(document.invoice)
  733: 
  734:     file_path = get_document_file_path(document)
  735: 
  736:     if not file_path.is_file():
  737:         raise HTTPException(
  738:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  739:             detail=(
  740:                 "No se encuentra el archivo original "
  741:                 "necesario para reprocesar."
  742:             ),
  743:         )
  744: 
  745:     add_audit_event(
  746:         database,
  747:         action="document.reprocess.requested",
  748:         entity_type="document",
  749:         entity_id=document.id,
  750:         actor=actor,
  751:         event_data={
  752:             "previous_status": document.status,
  753:             "previous_extraction_status": (
  754:                 document.extraction_status
  755:             ),
  756:         },
  757:     )
  758: 
  759:     database.commit()
  760: 
  761:     try:
  762:         process_document(
  763:             database,
  764:             document=document,
  765:             file_path=file_path,
  766:             actor=actor,
  767:         )
  768:     except Exception as error:
  769:         database.rollback()
  770: 
  771:         failed_document = get_document(
  772:             database,
  773:             document.id,
  774:         )
  775: 
  776:         return ActionResponse(
  777:             success=False,
  778:             message=f"El reprocesamiento falló. Error: {error}",
  779:             document=failed_document,
  780:         )
  781: 
  782:     processed_document = get_document(
  783:         database,
  784:         document.id,
  785:     )
  786: 
  787:     if processed_document is None:
  788:         raise HTTPException(
  789:             status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
  790:             detail=(
  791:                 "El documento se reprocesó, pero no pudo "
  792:                 "recuperarse desde la base de datos."
  793:             ),
  794:         )
  795: 
  796:     return ActionResponse(
  797:         success=True,
  798:         message="Documento reprocesado correctamente.",
  799:         document=processed_document,
  800:         invoice=processed_document.invoice,
  801:     )
  802: 
  803: 
  804: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  805: # Corrección manual
  806: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  807: 
  808: @app.patch(
  809:     "/api/invoices/{invoice_id}",
  810:     response_model=InvoiceResponse,
  811:     tags=["Facturas"],
  812: )
  813: def patch_invoice(
  814:     invoice_id: int,
  815:     payload: InvoiceUpdate,
  816:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  817:     actor_header: ActorHeader = None,
  818: ) -> Invoice:
  819:     actor = normalize_actor(actor_header)
  820: 
  821:     invoice = get_invoice(
  822:         database,
  823:         invoice_id,
  824:     )
  825: 
  826:     if invoice is None:
  827:         raise HTTPException(
  828:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  829:             detail="Factura no encontrada.",
  830:         )
  831: 
  832:     check_invoice_is_editable(invoice)
  833: 
  834:     try:
  835:         updated_invoice = update_invoice(
  836:             database,
  837:             invoice=invoice,
  838:             payload=payload,
  839:             actor=actor,
  840:         )
  841:     except ValueError as error:
  842:         raise HTTPException(
  843:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
  844:             detail=str(error),
  845:         ) from error
  846: 
  847:     complete_invoice = get_invoice(
  848:         database,
  849:         updated_invoice.id,
  850:     )
  851: 
  852:     if complete_invoice is None:
  853:         raise HTTPException(
  854:             status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
  855:             detail=(
  856:                 "La factura se actualizó, pero no pudo "
  857:                 "recuperarse desde la base de datos."
  858:             ),
  859:         )
  860: 
  861:     return complete_invoice
  862: 
  863: 
  864: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  865: # Aprobación
  866: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
  867: 
  868: @app.post(
  869:     "/api/invoices/{invoice_id}/approve",
  870:     tags=["Facturas"],
  871: )
  872: def approve_invoice_endpoint(
  873:     invoice_id: int,
  874:     database: DatabaseDependency,
  875:     payload: InvoiceApprovalRequest | None = Body(default=None),
  876:     actor_header: ActorHeader = None,
  877: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  878:     actor = normalize_actor(actor_header)
  879:     force_approval = payload.force if payload is not None else False
  880: 
  881:     invoice = get_invoice(
  882:         database,
  883:         invoice_id,
  884:     )
  885: 
  886:     if invoice is None:
  887:         raise HTTPException(
  888:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  889:             detail={
  890:                 "code": "INVOICE_NOT_FOUND",
  891:                 "message": "Factura no encontrada.",
  892:             },
  893:         )
  894: 
  895:     document = invoice.document
  896: 
  897:     if document is None:
  898:         raise HTTPException(
  899:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
  900:             detail={
  901:                 "code": "DOCUMENT_NOT_FOUND",
  902:                 "message": (
  903:                     "No se encontró el documento asociado "
  904:                     "a la factura."
  905:                 ),
  906:             },
  907:         )
  908: 
  909:     # Hace la operación idempotente.
  910:     if invoice.review_status == "APPROVED":
  911:         missing_fields = get_missing_invoice_fields(invoice)
  912: 
  913:         return {
  914:             "success": True,
  915:             "already_approved": True,
  916:             "approved_with_warnings": bool(missing_fields),
  917:             "message": "La factura ya estaba aprobada.",
  918:             "missing_fields": missing_fields,
  919:             "invoice": orm_to_dict(invoice),
  920:             "document": orm_to_dict(document),
  921:         }
  922: 
  923:     # Una factura rechazada no debe aprobarse directamente.
  924:     if invoice.review_status == "REJECTED":
  925:         raise HTTPException(
  926:             status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
  927:             detail={
  928:                 "code": "INVOICE_REJECTED",
  929:                 "message": (
  930:                     "La factura está rechazada. Debe reabrirse "
  931:                     "antes de poder aprobarla."
  932:                 ),
  933:                 "can_force_approval": False,
  934:             },
  935:         )
  936: 
  937:     missing_fields = get_missing_invoice_fields(invoice)
  938: 
  939:     # Primera petición: informar, pero no aprobar todavía.
  940:     if missing_fields and not force_approval:
  941:         raise HTTPException(
  942:             status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
  943:             detail={
  944:                 "code": "MISSING_FIELDS",
  945:                 "message": (
  946:                     "La factura tiene campos sin completar. "
  947:                     "Puedes añadirlos o aprobar con advertencias."
  948:                 ),
  949:                 "missing_fields": missing_fields,
  950:                 "can_force_approval": True,
  951:             },
  952:         )
  953: 
  954:     previous_review_status = invoice.review_status
  955:     previous_document_status = document.status
  956: 
  957:     if missing_fields and force_approval:
  958:         invoice.review_status = "APPROVED"
  959:         invoice.approved_at = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
  960:         invoice.rejected_at = None
  961:         invoice.rejection_reason = None
  962: 
  963:         # Se conserva INCOMPLETE para distinguir una factura completa
  964:         # de una factura aprobada con advertencias.
  965:         invoice.validation_status = "INCOMPLETE"
  966:         invoice.validation_messages = [
  967:             {
  968:                 "code": "MISSING_FIELD",
  969:                 "field": item["field"],
  970:                 "message": f"Falta el campo: {item['label']}.",
  971:             }
  972:             for item in missing_fields
  973:         ]
  974: 
  975:         document.status = "APPROVED"
  976: 
  977:         add_audit_event(
  978:             database,
  979:             action="invoice.approved_with_warnings",
  980:             entity_type="invoice",
  981:             entity_id=invoice.id,
  982:             actor=actor,
  983:             event_data={
  984:                 "forced": True,
  985:                 "missing_fields": missing_fields,
  986:                 "previous_review_status": previous_review_status,
  987:                 "new_review_status": "APPROVED",
  988:                 "previous_document_status": previous_document_status,
  989:                 "new_document_status": "APPROVED",
  990:             },
  991:         )
  992: 
  993:         add_audit_event(
  994:             database,
  995:             action="document.approved_with_warnings",
  996:             entity_type="document",
  997:             entity_id=document.id,
  998:             actor=actor,
  999:             event_data={
 1000:                 "invoice_id": invoice.id,
 1001:                 "forced": True,
 1002:                 "missing_fields": missing_fields,
 1003:                 "previous_status": previous_document_status,
 1004:                 "new_status": "APPROVED",
 1005:             },
 1006:         )
 1007: 
 1008:         try:
 1009:             database.commit()
 1010:             database.refresh(invoice)
 1011:             database.refresh(document)
 1012:         except Exception:
 1013:             database.rollback()
 1014:             raise
 1015: 
 1016:     else:
 1017:         # Factura completa: utiliza el servicio normal existente.
 1018:         try:
 1019:             approve_invoice(
 1020:                 database,
 1021:                 invoice=invoice,
 1022:                 actor=actor,
 1023:             )
 1024:         except ValueError as error:
 1025:             raise HTTPException(
 1026:                 status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
 1027:                 detail={
 1028:                     "code": "APPROVAL_CONFLICT",
 1029:                     "message": str(error),
 1030:                     "can_force_approval": False,
 1031:                 },
 1032:             ) from error
 1033: 
 1034:     complete_document = get_document(
 1035:         database,
 1036:         document.id,
 1037:     )
 1038: 
 1039:     if complete_document is None:
 1040:         raise HTTPException(
 1041:             status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
 1042:             detail={
 1043:                 "code": "DOCUMENT_RECOVERY_ERROR",
 1044:                 "message": (
 1045:                     "La factura fue aprobada, pero el documento "
 1046:                     "no pudo recuperarse."
 1047:                 ),
 1048:             },
 1049:         )
 1050: 
 1051:     complete_invoice = complete_document.invoice
 1052: 
 1053:     if complete_invoice is None:
 1054:         raise HTTPException(
 1055:             status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
 1056:             detail={
 1057:                 "code": "INVOICE_RECOVERY_ERROR",
 1058:                 "message": (
 1059:                     "La factura fue aprobada, pero no pudo "
 1060:                     "recuperarse después de la operación."
 1061:                 ),
 1062:             },
 1063:         )
 1064: 
 1065:     return {
 1066:         "success": True,
 1067:         "already_approved": False,
 1068:         "approved_with_warnings": bool(missing_fields),
 1069:         "message": (
 1070:             "Factura aprobada con campos pendientes."
 1071:             if missing_fields
 1072:             else "Factura aprobada correctamente."
 1073:         ),
 1074:         "missing_fields": missing_fields,
 1075:         "invoice": orm_to_dict(complete_invoice),
 1076:         "document": orm_to_dict(complete_document),
 1077:     }
 1078: 
 1079: 
 1080: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
 1081: # Rechazo
 1082: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
 1083: 
 1084: @app.post(
 1085:     "/api/invoices/{invoice_id}/reject",
 1086:     response_model=ActionResponse,
 1087:     tags=["Facturas"],
 1088: )
 1089: def reject_invoice_endpoint(
 1090:     invoice_id: int,
 1091:     payload: InvoiceRejectRequest,
 1092:     database: DatabaseDependency,
 1093:     actor_header: ActorHeader = None,
 1094: ) -> ActionResponse:
 1095:     actor = normalize_actor(actor_header)
 1096: 
 1097:     invoice = get_invoice(
 1098:         database,
 1099:         invoice_id,
 1100:     )
 1101: 
 1102:     if invoice is None:
 1103:         raise HTTPException(
 1104:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
 1105:             detail={
 1106:                 "code": "INVOICE_NOT_FOUND",
 1107:                 "message": "Factura no encontrada.",
 1108:             },
 1109:         )
 1110: 
 1111:     if invoice.review_status == "APPROVED":
 1112:         raise HTTPException(
 1113:             status_code=status.HTTP_409_CONFLICT,
 1114:             detail={
 1115:                 "code": "INVOICE_APPROVED",
 1116:                 "message": (
 1117:                     "Una factura aprobada no puede rechazarse "
 1118:                     "sin un flujo previo de reapertura."
 1119:                 ),
 1120:             },
 1121:         )
 1122: 
 1123:     reason = payload.reason.strip()
 1124: 
 1125:     if not reason:
 1126:         raise HTTPException(
 1127:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
 1128:             detail={
 1129:                 "code": "REJECTION_REASON_REQUIRED",
 1130:                 "message": "El motivo del rechazo es obligatorio.",
 1131:             },
 1132:         )
 1133: 
 1134:     try:
 1135:         rejected_invoice = reject_invoice(
 1136:             database,
 1137:             invoice=invoice,
 1138:             reason=reason,
 1139:             actor=actor,
 1140:         )
 1141:     except ValueError as error:
 1142:         raise HTTPException(
 1143:             status_code=status.HTTP_422_UNPROCESSABLE_ENTITY,
 1144:             detail={
 1145:                 "code": "REJECTION_ERROR",
 1146:                 "message": str(error),
 1147:             },
 1148:         ) from error
 1149: 
 1150:     complete_document = get_document(
 1151:         database,
 1152:         rejected_invoice.document_id,
 1153:     )
 1154: 
 1155:     if complete_document is None:
 1156:         raise HTTPException(
 1157:             status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
 1158:             detail={
 1159:                 "code": "DOCUMENT_RECOVERY_ERROR",
 1160:                 "message": (
 1161:                     "La factura fue rechazada, pero el documento "
 1162:                     "no pudo recuperarse."
 1163:                 ),
 1164:             },
 1165:         )
 1166: 
 1167:     complete_invoice = complete_document.invoice
 1168: 
 1169:     if complete_invoice is None:
 1170:         raise HTTPException(
 1171:             status_code=status.HTTP_500_INTERNAL_SERVER_ERROR,
 1172:             detail={
 1173:                 "code": "INVOICE_RECOVERY_ERROR",
 1174:                 "message": (
 1175:                     "La factura fue rechazada, pero no pudo "
 1176:                     "recuperarse después de la operación."
 1177:                 ),
 1178:             },
 1179:         )
 1180: 
 1181:     return ActionResponse(
 1182:         success=True,
 1183:         message="Factura rechazada correctamente.",
 1184:         document=orm_to_dict(complete_document),
 1185:         invoice=orm_to_dict(complete_invoice),
 1186:     )
 1187: 
 1188: 
 1189: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
 1190: # Auditoría
 1191: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
 1192: 
 1193: @app.get(
 1194:     "/api/documents/{document_id}/audit",
 1195:     response_model=list[AuditEventResponse],
 1196:     tags=["Auditoría"],
 1197: )
 1198: def document_audit(
 1199:     document_id: int,
 1200:     database: DatabaseDependency,
 1201: ) -> list[AuditEventResponse]:
 1202:     document = get_document(
 1203:         database,
 1204:         document_id,
 1205:     )
 1206: 
 1207:     if document is None:
 1208:         raise HTTPException(
 1209:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
 1210:             detail="Documento no encontrado.",
 1211:         )
 1212: 
 1213:     conditions = [
 1214:         (
 1215:             (AuditEvent.entity_type == "document")
 1216:             & (AuditEvent.entity_id == str(document.id))
 1217:         )
 1218:     ]
 1219: 
 1220:     if document.invoice is not None:
 1221:         conditions.append(
 1222:             (
 1223:                 (AuditEvent.entity_type == "invoice")
 1224:                 & (
 1225:                     AuditEvent.entity_id
 1226:                     == str(document.invoice.id)
 1227:                 )
 1228:             )
 1229:         )
 1230: 
 1231:     statement = (
 1232:         select(AuditEvent)
 1233:         .where(or_(*conditions))
 1234:         .order_by(
 1235:             AuditEvent.created_at.desc(),
 1236:             AuditEvent.id.desc(),
 1237:         )
 1238:     )
 1239: 
 1240:     events = database.scalars(statement).all()
 1241: 
 1242:     return [
 1243:         AuditEventResponse.model_validate(
 1244:             event,
 1245:             from_attributes=True,
 1246:         )
 1247:         for event in events
 1248:     ]
 1249: 
 1250: 
 1251: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
 1252: # Frontend
 1253: # -------------------------------------------------------------------
 1254: 
 1255: @app.get(
 1256:     "/",
 1257:     include_in_schema=False,
 1258: )
 1259: def frontend_home() -> FileResponse:
 1260:     index_path = STATIC_DIRECTORY / "index.html"
 1261: 
 1262:     if not index_path.is_file():
 1263:         raise HTTPException(
 1264:             status_code=status.HTTP_404_NOT_FOUND,
 1265:             detail="No se encuentra app/static/index.html.",
 1266:         )
 1267: 
 1268:     return FileResponse(index_path)
 1269: 
 1270: 
 1271: app.mount(
 1272:     "/static",
 1273:     StaticFiles(directory=str(STATIC_DIRECTORY)),
 1274:     name="static",
 1275: )
 1276: 
 1277: def serialize_model(instance: Any) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
 1278:     if instance is None:
 1279:         return None
 1280: 
 1281:     try:
 1282:         mapper = sqlalchemy_inspect(instance).mapper
 1283: 
 1284:         data = {
 1285:             attribute.key: getattr(instance, attribute.key)
 1286:             for attribute in mapper.column_attrs
 1287:         }
 1288: 
 1289:         return jsonable_encoder(data)
 1290: 
 1291:     except Exception:
 1292:         data = {
 1293:             key: value
 1294:             for key, value in vars(instance).items()
 1295:             if not key.startswith("_")
 1296:         }
 1297: 
 1298:         return jsonable_encoder(data)

   94:         if value is None:
   95:             return None
   96: 
   97:         return value.upper()
   98: 
   99: 
  100: class InvoiceRejectRequest(BaseModel):
  101:     reason: str = Field(
  102:         min_length=3,
  103:         max_length=1000,

   28: def utc_now() -> datetime:
   29:     return datetime.now(timezone.utc)
   30: 
   31: 
   32: def parse_iso_date(
   33:     value: str | date | None,
   34: ) -> date | None:
   35:     if value is None:
   36:         return None
   37: 
   38:     if isinstance(value, date):
   39:         return value
   40: 
   41:     try:
   42:         return date.fromisoformat(value)
   43:     except ValueError:
   44:         return None
   45: 
   46: 
   47: def serialize_audit_value(
   48:     value: Any,
   49: ) -> Any:
   50:     if isinstance(value, Decimal):
   51:         return format(value, ".2f")
   52: 
   53:     if isinstance(value, (date, datetime)):
   54:         return value.isoformat()
   55: 
   56:     if isinstance(value, list):
   57:         return [
   58:             serialize_audit_value(item)
   59:             for item in value
   60:         ]
   61: 
   62:     if isinstance(value, dict):
   63:         return {
   64:             key: serialize_audit_value(item)
   65:             for key, item in value.items()
   66:         }
   67: 
   68:     return value
   69: 
   70: 
   71: def add_audit_event(
   72:     database: Session,
   73:     *,
   74:     action: str,
   75:     entity_type: str,
   76:     entity_id: int | str,
   77:     actor: str = "system",
   78:     event_data: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
   79: ) -> AuditEvent:
   80:     event = AuditEvent(
   81:         action=action,
   82:         entity_type=entity_type,
   83:         entity_id=str(entity_id),
   84:         actor=actor,
   85:         event_data=serialize_audit_value(
   86:             event_data or {}
   87:         ),
   88:     )
   89: 
   90:     database.add(event)
   91: 
   92:     return event

   95: def validate_invoice_values(
   96:     *,
   97:     supplier_name: str | None,
   98:     supplier_tax_id: str | None,
   99:     invoice_number: str | None,
  100:     invoice_date: date | None,
  101:     subtotal: Decimal | None,
  102:     tax_total: Decimal | None,
  103:     withholding_total: Decimal | None,
  104:     surcharge_total: Decimal | None,
  105:     total: Decimal | None,
  106:     currency: str | None,
  107: ) -> tuple[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
  108:     messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
  109: 
  110:     required_fields = {
  111:         "supplier_name": supplier_name,
  112:         "supplier_tax_id": supplier_tax_id,
  113:         "invoice_number": invoice_number,
  114:         "invoice_date": invoice_date,
  115:         "total": total,
  116:         "currency": currency,
  117:     }
  118: 
  119:     for field_name, field_value in required_fields.items():
  120:         if field_value in (None, ""):
  121:             messages.append(
  122:                 {
  123:                     "code": "required_field_missing",
  124:                     "field": field_name,
  125:                     "severity": "error",
  126:                     "message": (
  127:                         f"Falta el campo obligatorio: "
  128:                         f"{field_name}."
  129:                     ),
  130:                 }
  131:             )
  132: 
  133:     if total is not None and total < 0:
  134:         messages.append(
  135:             {
  136:                 "code": "negative_total",
  137:                 "field": "total",
  138:                 "severity": "warning",
  139:                 "message": (
  140:                     "El total es negativo. Comprueba si "
  141:                     "se trata de una factura rectificativa."
  142:                 ),
  143:             }
  144:         )
  145: 
  146:     if invoice_date is not None:
  147:         today = date.today()
  148: 
  149:         if invoice_date > today:
  150:             messages.append(
  151:                 {
  152:                     "code": "future_invoice_date",
  153:                     "field": "invoice_date",
  154:                     "severity": "warning",
  155:                     "message": (
  156:                         "La fecha de la factura es futura."
  157:                     ),
  158:                 }
  159:             )
  160: 
  161:     amounts_available = (
  162:         subtotal is not None
  163:         and tax_total is not None
  164:         and total is not None
  165:     )
  166: 
  167:     if amounts_available:
  168:         withholding = (
  169:             withholding_total
  170:             or Decimal("0.00")
  171:         )
  172:         surcharge = (
  173:             surcharge_total
  174:             or Decimal("0.00")
  175:         )
  176: 
  177:         expected_total = (
  178:             subtotal
  179:             + tax_total
  180:             + surcharge
  181:             - withholding
  182:         ).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
  183: 
  184:         difference = abs(
  185:             total - expected_total
  186:         ).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
  187: 
  188:         tolerance = Decimal(
  189:             str(settings.amount_tolerance)
  190:         )
  191: 
  192:         if difference > tolerance:
  193:             messages.append(
  194:                 {
  195:                     "code": "amount_mismatch",
  196:                     "field": "total",
  197:                     "severity": "error",
  198:                     "message": (
  199:                         "Los importes no cuadran. "
  200:                         f"El total esperado es "
  201:                         f"{expected_total:.2f} y el total "
  202:                         f"extraído es {total:.2f}."
  203:                     ),
  204:                     "difference": format(
  205:                         difference,
  206:                         ".2f",
  207:                     ),
  208:                     "expected_total": format(
  209:                         expected_total,
  210:                         ".2f",
  211:                     ),
  212:                 }
  213:             )
  214:     else:
  215:         messages.append(
  216:             {
  217:                 "code": "amounts_incomplete",
  218:                 "field": "amounts",
  219:                 "severity": "warning",
  220:                 "message": (
  221:                     "No se puede comprobar el cuadre "
  222:                     "porque faltan base, impuestos o total."
  223:                 ),
  224:             }
  225:         )
  226: 
  227:     error_messages = [
  228:         message
  229:         for message in messages
  230:         if message["severity"] == "error"
  231:     ]
  232: 
  233:     missing_messages = [
  234:         message
  235:         for message in messages
  236:         if message["code"]
  237:         == "required_field_missing"
  238:     ]
  239: 
  240:     if missing_messages:
  241:         return "INCOMPLETE", messages
  242: 
  243:     if error_messages:
  244:         return "MISMATCH", messages
  245: 
  246:     if not amounts_available:
  247:         return "INCOMPLETE", messages
  248: 
  249:     return "VALID", messages
  250:

  252: def get_document(
  253:     database: Session,
  254:     document_id: int,
  255: ) -> Document | None:
  256:     statement = (
  257:         select(Document)
  258:         .where(Document.id == document_id)
  259:         .options(
  260:             selectinload(Document.invoice).selectinload(
  261:                 Invoice.tax_lines
  262:             ),
  263:             selectinload(
  264:                 Document.extraction_runs
  265:             ),
  266:         )
  267:     )
  268: 
  269:     return database.scalar(statement)
  270:

  272: def get_invoice(
  273:     database: Session,
  274:     invoice_id: int,
  275: ) -> Invoice | None:
  276:     statement = (
  277:         select(Invoice)
  278:         .where(Invoice.id == invoice_id)
  279:         .options(
  280:             selectinload(Invoice.tax_lines),
  281:             selectinload(Invoice.document),
  282:         )
  283:     )
  284: 
  285:     return database.scalar(statement)

  288: def field_value(
  289:     result: dict[str, Any],
  290:     field_name: str,
  291: ) -> Any:
  292:     return (
  293:         result
  294:         .get("fields", {})
  295:         .get(field_name, {})
  296:         .get("value")
  297:     )

  299: 
  300: def field_confidences(
  301:     result: dict[str, Any],
  302: ) -> dict[str, Any]:
  303:     confidences: dict[str, Any] = {}
  304: 
  305:     for name, field in result.get(
  306:         "fields",
  307:         {},
  308:     ).items():
  309:         confidences[name] = {
  310:             "confidence": field.get(
  311:                 "confidence",
  312:                 0,
  313:             ),
  314:             "source": field.get("source"),
  315:             "evidence": field.get(
  316:                 "evidence"
  317:             ),
  318:         }
  319: 
  320:     return confidences

  322: 
  323: def find_fiscal_duplicate(
  324:     database: Session,
  325:     *,
  326:     invoice: Invoice,
  327: ) -> tuple[str, Invoice | None]:
  328:     supplier_tax_id = normalize_tax_id(
  329:         invoice.supplier_tax_id
  330:     )
  331: 
  332:     invoice_number = normalize_invoice_number(
  333:         invoice.invoice_number
  334:     )
  335: 
  336:     if supplier_tax_id and invoice_number:
  337:         strong_statement = (
  338:             select(Invoice)
  339:             .where(
  340:                 Invoice.id != invoice.id,
  341:                 Invoice.supplier_tax_id
  342:                 == supplier_tax_id,
  343:                 Invoice.invoice_number
  344:                 == invoice_number,
  345:             )
  346:             .order_by(Invoice.id.asc())
  347:         )
  348: 
  349:         duplicate = database.scalar(
  350:             strong_statement
  351:         )
  352: 
  353:         if duplicate:
  354:             return "STRONG", duplicate
  355: 
  356:     if (
  357:         supplier_tax_id
  358:         and invoice.invoice_date
  359:         and invoice.total is not None
  360:     ):
  361:         probable_statement = (
  362:             select(Invoice)
  363:             .where(
  364:                 Invoice.id != invoice.id,
  365:                 Invoice.supplier_tax_id
  366:                 == supplier_tax_id,
  367:                 Invoice.invoice_date
  368:                 == invoice.invoice_date,
  369:                 Invoice.total == invoice.total,
  370:             )
  371:             .order_by(Invoice.id.asc())
  372:         )
  373: 
  374:         duplicate = database.scalar(
  375:             probable_statement
  376:         )
  377: 
  378:         if duplicate:
  379:             return "PROBABLE", duplicate
  380: 
  381:     return "NONE", None
  382: 
  383: 
  384: def apply_duplicate_detection(
  385:     database: Session,
  386:     invoice: Invoice,
  387: ) -> None:
  388:     duplicate_status, duplicate = (
  389:         find_fiscal_duplicate(
  390:             database,
  391:             invoice=invoice,
  392:         )
  393:     )
  394: 
  395:     invoice.duplicate_status = (
  396:         duplicate_status
  397:     )
  398:     invoice.duplicate_of_invoice_id = (
  399:         duplicate.id
  400:         if duplicate
  401:         else None
  402:     )

  405: def update_document_status(
  406:     document: Document,
  407:     invoice: Invoice | None,
  408: ) -> None:
  409:     if document.requires_ocr:
  410:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  411:         document.extraction_status = (
  412:             "OCR_REQUIRED"
  413:         )
  414:         return
  415: 
  416:     if invoice is None:
  417:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  418:         document.extraction_status = (
  419:             "COMPLETED"
  420:         )
  421:         return
  422: 
  423:     if invoice.review_status == "APPROVED":
  424:         document.status = "APPROVED"
  425:         return
  426: 
  427:     if invoice.review_status == "REJECTED":
  428:         document.status = "REJECTED"
  429:         return
  430: 
  431:     if invoice.duplicate_status != "NONE":
  432:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  433:         return
  434: 
  435:     if (
  436:         invoice.validation_status == "VALID"
  437:         and invoice.confidence
  438:         >= settings.minimum_auto_confidence
  439:     ):
  440:         document.status = (
  441:             "READY_FOR_APPROVAL"
  442:         )
  443:     else:
  444:         document.status = "NEEDS_REVIEW"
  445:
