 1333: 
 1334: def find_labeled_amount(
 1335:     text: str,
 1336:     field_name: str,
 1337:     *,
 1338:     allow_inference: bool = True,
 1339: ) -> ExtractedField:
 1340:     del allow_inference
 1341: 
 1342:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1343:     labels = amount_labels(field_name)
 1344:     candidates: list[tuple[int, int, Decimal, str]] = []
 1345: 
 1346:     for line_index, line in enumerate(lines):
 1347:         normalized_line = normalize_search_text(line)
 1348: 
 1349:         matching_labels = [
 1350:             label
 1351:             for label in labels
 1352:             if normalize_search_text(label) in normalized_line
 1353:         ]
 1354: 
 1355:         if not matching_labels:
 1356:             continue
 1357: 
 1358:         # Impide que "TOTAL IVA" se interprete como total de factura.
 1359:         if (
 1360:             field_name == "total"
 1361:             and (
 1362:                 "total iva" in normalized_line
 1363:                 or "total impuestos" in normalized_line
 1364:                 and "incluidos" not in normalized_line
 1365:             )
 1366:         ):
 1367:             continue
 1368: 
 1369:         values = money_values(line)
 1370: 
 1371:         if field_name == "tax_total":
 1372:             values = [
 1373:                 item
 1374:                 for item in values
 1375:                 if item[0] <= Decimal("100000000")
 1376:             ]
 1377: 
 1378:         if values:
 1379:             amount, raw_value = values[-1]
 1380: 
 1381:             score = 90
 1382: 
 1383:             if field_name == "total":
 1384:                 if "total a pagar" in normalized_line:
 1385:                     score = 99
 1386:                 elif "total factura" in normalized_line:
 1387:                     score = 98
 1388:                 elif "importe total" in normalized_line:
 1389:                     score = 97
 1390:                 elif "total impuestos incluidos" in normalized_line:
 1391:                     score = 96
 1392: 
 1393:             candidates.append(
 1394:                 (
 1395:                     score,
 1396:                     line_index,
 1397:                     abs(amount)
 1398:                     if field_name == "withholding_total"
 1399:                     else amount,
 1400:                     line,
 1401:                 )
 1402:             )
 1403:             continue
 1404: 
 1405:         # Etiqueta e importe pueden estar separados.
 1406:         for offset in range(1, 5):
 1407:             candidate_index = line_index + offset
 1408: 
 1409:             if candidate_index >= len(lines):
 1410:                 break
 1411: 
 1412:             candidate_line = lines[candidate_index]
 1413:             values = money_values(candidate_line)
 1414: 
 1415:             if not values:
 1416:                 continue
 1417: 
 1418:             # Las líneas puramente monetarias son más fiables.
 1419:             amount, raw_value = values[-1]
 1420:             score = 85 - offset * 5
 1421: 
 1422:             candidates.append(
 1423:                 (
 1424:                     score,
 1425:                     candidate_index,
 1426:                     abs(amount)
 1427:                     if field_name == "withholding_total"
 1428:                     else amount,
 1429:                     f"{line} | {candidate_line}",
 1430:                 )
 1431:             )
 1432:             break
 1433: 
 1434:     if not candidates:
 1435:         return ExtractedField()
 1436: 
 1437:     # En multipágina suele ser más fiable el último resumen.
 1438:     if field_name in {
 1439:         "subtotal",
 1440:         "tax_total",
 1441:         "withholding_total",
 1442:         "surcharge_total",
 1443:         "total",
 1444:     }:
 1445:         selected = max(
 1446:             candidates,
 1447:             key=lambda item: (
 1448:                 item[0],
 1449:                 item[1],
 1450:             ),
 1451:         )
 1452:     else:
 1453:         selected = candidates[-1]
 1454: 
 1455:     score, _, amount, evidence = selected
 1456: 
 1457:     return ExtractedField(
 1458:         value=decimal_to_string(amount),
 1459:         confidence=min(score, 99),
 1460:         source=f"{field_name}_label",
 1461:         evidence=evidence[:300],
 1462:     )
 1463: 
 1464: 
 1465: def extract_stacked_summary(
 1466:     text: str,
 1467: ) -> dict[str, ExtractedField]:
 1468:     """
 1469:     Resuelve bloques como:
 1470: 
 1471:         HONORARIOS
 1472:         21% I.V.A.
 1473:         Retención 15% IRPF
 1474:         TOTAL
 1475:         1.111,11
 1476:         233,33
 1477:         -166,67
 1478:         1.177,77
 1479:     """
 1480:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1481:     result: dict[str, ExtractedField] = {}
 1482: 
 1483:     for start_index in range(len(lines)):
 1484:         labels: list[tuple[str, int]] = []
 1485: 
 1486:         for index in range(start_index, min(len(lines), start_index + 8)):
 1487:             normalized = normalize_search_text(lines[index])
 1488: 
 1489:             field_name: str | None = None
 1490: 
 1491:             if "retencion" in normalized or "irpf" in normalized:
 1492:                 field_name = "withholding_total"
 1493:             elif "iva" in normalized:
 1494:                 field_name = "tax_total"
 1495:             elif (
 1496:                 "base imponible" in normalized
 1497:                 or "subtotal" in normalized
 1498:                 or "honorarios" in normalized
 1499:             ):
 1500:                 field_name = "subtotal"
 1501:             elif normalized == "total" or "total factura" in normalized:
 1502:                 field_name = "total"
 1503: 
 1504:             if field_name and field_name not in [
 1505:                 item[0]
 1506:                 for item in labels
 1507:             ]:
 1508:                 labels.append((field_name, index))
 1509: 
 1510:         if len(labels) < 3:
 1511:             continue
 1512: 
 1513:         search_start = max(index for _, index in labels) + 1
 1514:         amounts: list[tuple[Decimal, str]] = []
 1515: 
 1516:         for index in range(
 1517:             search_start,
 1518:             min(len(lines), search_start + 10),
 1519:         ):
 1520:             values = money_values(lines[index])
 1521: 
 1522:             if len(values) == 1:
 1523:                 amounts.append((values[0][0], lines[index]))
 1524: 
 1525:             if len(amounts) >= len(labels):
 1526:                 break
 1527: 
 1528:         if len(amounts) < len(labels):
 1529:             continue
 1530: 
 1531:         for position, (field_name, _) in enumerate(labels):
 1532:             amount, evidence = amounts[position]
 1533: 
 1534:             if field_name == "withholding_total":
 1535:                 amount = abs(amount)
 1536: 
 1537:             result[field_name] = ExtractedField(
 1538:                 value=decimal_to_string(amount),
 1539:                 confidence=94,
 1540:                 source="stacked_fiscal_summary",
 1541:                 evidence=evidence,
 1542:             )
 1543: 
 1544:         break
 1545: 
 1546:     return result
 1547: 
 1548: 
 1549: def detect_tax_rate(text: str) -> Decimal | None:
 1550:     rates: list[Decimal] = []
 1551: 
 1552:     for match in re.finditer(
 1553:         r"\b(?:IVA|I\.V\.A\.)?\s*"
 1554:         r"(\d{1,2}(?:[,.]\d{1,3})?)\s*%",
 1555:         text,
 1556:         re.IGNORECASE,
 1557:     ):
 1558:         rate = normalize_amount(match.group(1))
 1559: 
 1560:         if rate is not None and Decimal("0") <= rate <= Decimal("30"):
 1561:             rates.append(rate)
 1562: 
 1563:     if Decimal("21.00") in rates:
 1564:         return Decimal("21.00")
 1565: 
 1566:     return rates[0] if rates else None
 1567: 
 1568: 
 1569: def reconcile_amounts(
 1570:     subtotal: ExtractedField,
 1571:     tax_total: ExtractedField,
 1572:     withholding_total: ExtractedField,
 1573:     surcharge_total: ExtractedField,
 1574:     total: ExtractedField,
 1575:     text: str,
 1576: ) -> tuple[
 1577:     ExtractedField,
 1578:     ExtractedField,
 1579:     ExtractedField,
 1580:     ExtractedField,
 1581:     ExtractedField,
 1582: ]:
 1583:     base = normalize_amount(subtotal.value)
 1584:     tax = normalize_amount(tax_total.value)
 1585:     withholding = (
 1586:         normalize_amount(withholding_total.value)
 1587:         or Decimal("0.00")
 1588:     )
 1589:     surcharge = (
 1590:         normalize_amount(surcharge_total.value)
 1591:         or Decimal("0.00")
 1592:     )
 1593:     grand_total = normalize_amount(total.value)
 1594:     rate = detect_tax_rate(text)
 1595: 
 1596:     if (
 1597:         base is not None
 1598:         and grand_total is not None
 1599:         and tax is None
 1600:     ):
 1601:         calculated_tax = (
 1602:             grand_total
 1603:             - base
 1604:             + withholding
 1605:             - surcharge
 1606:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1607: 
 1608:         if calculated_tax >= 0:
 1609:             tax_total = ExtractedField(
 1610:                 value=decimal_to_string(calculated_tax),
 1611:                 confidence=84,
 1612:                 source="arithmetic_total_minus_base",
 1613:                 evidence=(
 1614:                     f"{grand_total} - {base} "
 1615:                     f"+ {withholding} - {surcharge}"
 1616:                 ),
 1617:             )
 1618:             tax = calculated_tax
 1619: 
 1620:     if (
 1621:         tax is not None
 1622:         and grand_total is not None
 1623:         and base is None
 1624:     ):
 1625:         calculated_base = (
 1626:             grand_total
 1627:             - tax
 1628:             + withholding
 1629:             - surcharge
 1630:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1631: 
 1632:         if calculated_base >= 0:
 1633:             subtotal = ExtractedField(
 1634:                 value=decimal_to_string(calculated_base),
 1635:                 confidence=84,
 1636:                 source="arithmetic_total_minus_tax",
 1637:                 evidence=(
 1638:                     f"{grand_total} - {tax} "
 1639:                     f"+ {withholding} - {surcharge}"
 1640:                 ),
 1641:             )
 1642:             base = calculated_base
 1643: 
 1644:     if (
 1645:         base is not None
 1646:         and tax is not None
 1647:         and grand_total is None
 1648:     ):
 1649:         calculated_total = (
 1650:             base
 1651:             + tax
 1652:             + surcharge
 1653:             - withholding
 1654:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1655: 
 1656:         total = ExtractedField(
 1657:             value=decimal_to_string(calculated_total),
 1658:             confidence=82,
 1659:             source="arithmetic_total",
 1660:             evidence=(
 1661:                 f"{base} + {tax} "
 1662:                 f"+ {surcharge} - {withholding}"
 1663:             ),
 1664:         )
 1665:         grand_total = calculated_total
 1666: 
 1667:     # Si solo existe total y un IVA inequívoco, se puede inferir base e IVA.
 1668:     if (
 1669:         grand_total is not None
 1670:         and base is None
 1671:         and tax is None
 1672:         and rate is not None
 1673:         and withholding == 0
 1674:         and surcharge == 0
 1675:     ):
 1676:         divisor = Decimal("1") + rate / Decimal("100")
 1677:         inferred_base = (
 1678:             grand_total / divisor
 1679:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1680:         inferred_tax = (
 1681:             grand_total - inferred_base
 1682:         ).quantize(CENT)
 1683: 
 1684:         if abs(
 1685:             inferred_base
 1686:             + inferred_tax
 1687:             - grand_total
 1688:         ) <= AMOUNT_TOLERANCE:
 1689:             subtotal = ExtractedField(
 1690:                 value=decimal_to_string(inferred_base),
 1691:                 confidence=72,
 1692:                 source="inferred_from_total_and_tax_rate",
 1693:                 evidence=f"Total {grand_total}; IVA {rate}%",
 1694:             )
 1695:             tax_total = ExtractedField(
 1696:                 value=decimal_to_string(inferred_tax),
 1697:                 confidence=72,
 1698:                 source="inferred_from_total_and_tax_rate",
 1699:                 evidence=f"Total {grand_total}; IVA {rate}%",
 1700:             )
 1701: 
 1702:     return (
 1703:         subtotal,
 1704:         tax_total,
 1705:         withholding_total,
 1706:         surcharge_total,
 1707:         total,
 1708:     )
 1709: 
 1710: 
 1711: def extract_tax_lines(
 1712:     text: str,
 1713:     subtotal: ExtractedField,
 1714:     tax_total: ExtractedField,
 1715: ) -> list[TaxLineResult]:
 1716:     base = normalize_amount(subtotal.value)
 1717:     tax = normalize_amount(tax_total.value)
 1718:     rate = detect_tax_rate(text)
 1719: 
 1720:     if base is None or tax is None:
 1721:         return []
 1722: 
 1723:     if rate is None and base != 0:
 1724:         inferred_rate = (
 1725:             tax / base * Decimal("100")
 1726:         ).quantize(Decimal("0.01"))
 1727: 
 1728:         common_rates = (
 1729:             Decimal("0.00"),
 1730:             Decimal("4.00"),
 1731:             Decimal("10.00"),
 1732:             Decimal("21.00"),
 1733:         )
 1734: 
 1735:         closest = min(
 1736:             common_rates,
 1737:             key=lambda candidate: abs(candidate - inferred_rate),
 1738:         )
 1739: 
 1740:         if abs(closest - inferred_rate) <= Decimal("0.10"):
 1741:             rate = closest
 1742: 
 1743:     if rate is None:
 1744:         return []
 1745: 
 1746:     expected_tax = (
 1747:         base * rate / Decimal("100")
 1748:     ).quantize(CENT)
 1749: 
 1750:     confidence = (
 1751:         94
 1752:         if abs(expected_tax - tax) <= AMOUNT_TOLERANCE
 1753:         else 72
 1754:     )
 1755: 
 1756:     return [
 1757:         TaxLineResult(
 1758:             tax_type="IVA",
 1759:             tax_rate=decimal_to_string(rate),
 1760:             tax_base=decimal_to_string(base),
 1761:             tax_amount=decimal_to_string(tax),
 1762:             confidence=confidence,
 1763:             source="reconciled_tax_summary",
 1764:         )
 1765:     ]
 1766: 
 1767: 
 1768: def extract_concept(text: str) -> ExtractedField:
 1769:     lines = get_lines(text)
 1770:     start_index: int | None = None
 1771: 
 1772:     for index, line in enumerate(lines):
 1773:         normalized = normalize_search_text(line)
 1774: 
 1775:         if (
 1776:             normalized.startswith("concepto")
 1777:             or normalized.startswith("descripcion")
 1778:             or normalized.startswith("descripción")
 1779:         ):
 1780:             start_index = index + 1
 1781:             break
 1782: 
 1783:     if start_index is None:
 1784:         return ExtractedField()
 1785: 
 1786:     concept_lines: list[str] = []
 1787: 
 1788:     for line in lines[start_index:start_index + 12]:
 1789:         normalized = normalize_search_text(line)
 1790: 
 1791:         if any(
 1792:             stop_label in normalized
 1793:             for stop_label in (
 1794:                 "base imponible",
 1795:                 "total iva",
 1796:                 "forma de pago",
 1797:                 "vencimiento",
 1798:                 "subtotal",
 1799:                 "total factura",
 1800:                 "proteccion de datos",
 1801:             )
 1802:         ):
 1803:             break
 1804: 
 1805:         if money_values(line) and len(line) < 30:
 1806:             continue
 1807: 
 1808:         concept_lines.append(line)
 1809: 
 1810:     concept = " ".join(concept_lines)
 1811:     concept = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", concept).strip()
 1812: 
 1813:     if len(concept) < 5:
 1814:         return ExtractedField()
 1815: 
 1816:     return ExtractedField(
 1817:         value=concept[:1000],
 1818:         confidence=78,
 1819:         source="description_section",
 1820:         evidence=concept[:300],
 1821:     )

 1909: def detect_currency(text: str) -> ExtractedField:
 1910:     normalized = normalize_search_text(text)
 1911: 
 1912:     if "€" in text or " eur" in normalized:
 1913:         return ExtractedField(
 1914:             value="EUR",
 1915:             confidence=98,
 1916:             source="currency_symbol",
 1917:             evidence="EUR / €",
 1918:         )
 1919: 
 1920:     if "$" in text or " usd" in normalized:
 1921:         return ExtractedField(
 1922:             value="USD",
 1923:             confidence=85,
 1924:             source="currency_symbol",
 1925:             evidence="USD / $",
 1926:         )
 1927: 
 1928:     if "£" in text or " gbp" in normalized:
 1929:         return ExtractedField(
 1930:             value="GBP",
 1931:             confidence=85,
 1932:             source="currency_symbol",
 1933:             evidence="GBP / £",
 1934:         )
 1935: 
 1936:     return ExtractedField(
 1937:         value="EUR",
 1938:         confidence=45,
 1939:         source="default_currency",
 1940:         evidence=None,
 1941:     )

 1944: def detect_invoice_likelihood(
 1945:     text: str,
 1946:     fields: dict[str, ExtractedField],
 1947: ) -> tuple[bool, int, list[str]]:
 1948:     normalized = normalize_search_text(text)
 1949:     score = 0
 1950:     signals: list[str] = []
 1951: 
 1952:     rules = (
 1953:         (
 1954:             "invoice_keyword",
 1955:             "factura" in normalized,
 1956:             25,
 1957:         ),
 1958:         (
 1959:             "invoice_number",
 1960:             fields["invoice_number"].value is not None,
 1961:             15,
 1962:         ),
 1963:         (
 1964:             "invoice_date",
 1965:             fields["invoice_date"].value is not None,
 1966:             10,
 1967:         ),
 1968:         (
 1969:             "supplier_tax_id",
 1970:             fields["supplier_tax_id"].value is not None,
 1971:             15,
 1972:         ),
 1973:         (
 1974:             "subtotal",
 1975:             fields["subtotal"].value is not None,
 1976:             10,
 1977:         ),
 1978:         (
 1979:             "tax_total",
 1980:             fields["tax_total"].value is not None,
 1981:             10,
 1982:         ),
 1983:         (
 1984:             "total",
 1985:             fields["total"].value is not None,
 1986:             20,
 1987:         ),
 1988:         (
 1989:             "vat_keyword",
 1990:             "iva" in normalized,
 1991:             10,
 1992:         ),
 1993:     )
 1994: 
 1995:     for signal, condition, points in rules:
 1996:         if condition:
 1997:             score += points
 1998:             signals.append(signal)
 1999: 
 2000:     score = min(score, 100)
 2001: 
 2002:     return score >= 45, score, signals
 2003: 
 2004: 
 2005: def calculate_overall_confidence(
 2006:     fields: dict[str, ExtractedField],
 2007:     invoice_likelihood: int,
 2008: ) -> int:
 2009:     important_fields = (
 2010:         "supplier_name",
 2011:         "supplier_tax_id",
 2012:         "invoice_number",
 2013:         "invoice_date",
 2014:         "subtotal",
 2015:         "tax_total",
 2016:         "total",
 2017:     )
 2018: 
 2019:     confidences = [
 2020:         fields[field_name].confidence
 2021:         for field_name in important_fields
 2022:         if fields[field_name].value is not None
 2023:     ]
 2024: 
 2025:     if not confidences:
 2026:         return min(invoice_likelihood, 30)
 2027: 
 2028:     average = sum(confidences) / len(confidences)
 2029:     completeness = len(confidences) / len(important_fields)
 2030: 
 2031:     result = (
 2032:         average * 0.70
 2033:         + invoice_likelihood * 0.20
 2034:         + completeness * 100 * 0.10
 2035:     )
 2036: 
 2037:     return min(int(round(result)), 99)

 2314: def compute_confidence(data: dict[str, Any]) -> int:
 2315:     if data.get("needs_ocr"):
 2316:         return 10
 2317: 
 2318:     score = 0
 2319: 
 2320:     if data.get("supplier"):
 2321:         score += 20
 2322:     if data.get("cif"):
 2323:         score += 15
 2324:     if data.get("invoice_number"):
 2325:         score += 15
 2326:     if data.get("date"):
 2327:         score += 10
 2328:     if data.get("base") is not None:
 2329:         score += 10
 2330:     if data.get("tax") is not None:
 2331:         score += 10
 2332:     if data.get("amount") is not None:
 2333:         score += 20
 2334: 
 2335:     return min(score, 99)

 2338: def eur(
 2339:     value: Decimal | float | int | None,
 2340: ) -> str:
 2341:     if value is None:
 2342:         return "-"
 2343: 
 2344:     amount = normalize_amount(value)
 2345: 
 2346:     if amount is None:
 2347:         return "-"
 2348: 
 2349:     formatted = f"{amount:,.2f}"
 2350: 
 2351:     return (
 2352:         formatted
 2353:         .replace(",", "X")
 2354:         .replace(".", ",")
 2355:         .replace("X", ".")
 2356:         + " €"
 2357:     )

 2360: def is_aeat(text: str, filename: str) -> bool:
 2361:     haystack = normalize_search_text(
 2362:         f"{text} {filename}"

 2400: def detect_laboral_subtype(
 2401:     text: str,
 2402:     filename: str,
 2403: ) -> str:
 2404:     haystack = normalize_search_text(
 2405:         f"{text} {filename}"
 2406:     )
 2407: 
 2408:     if "contrato" in haystack:
 2409:         return "Contrato de trabajo"
 2410: 
 2411:     if (
 2412:         "modelo 145" in haystack
 2413:         or "comunicacion de datos al pagador" in haystack
 2414:     ):
 2415:         return "Modelo 145 (IRPF)"
 2416: 
 2417:     if (
 2418:         "alta" in haystack
 2419:         and "seguridad social" in haystack
 2420:     ):
 2421:         return "Alta en Seguridad Social"
 2422: 
 2423:     return "Nómina del mes"
 2424: 
 2425: 
 2426: def detect_aeat_type(
 2427:     text: str,
 2428:     filename: str,
 2429: ) -> tuple[str, int, str | None]:
 2430:     haystack = normalize_search_text(
 2431:         f"{text} {filename}"
 2432:     )
 2433: 
 2434:     if "embargo" in haystack:
 2435:         return "Diligencia de embargo", 3, "Crítica"
 2436: 
 2437:     if "apremio" in haystack:
 2438:         return "Providencia de apremio", 15, "Crítica"
 2439: 
 2440:     if (
 2441:         "procedimiento sancionador" in haystack
 2442:         or "sancion" in haystack
 2443:     ):
 2444:         return "Procedimiento sancionador", 15, "Alta"
 2445: 
 2446:     if "requerimiento" in haystack:
 2447:         return "Requerimiento de documentación", 10, None
 2448: 
 2449:     return "Notificación AEAT", 10, None

    9: def amount_to_float(a):
   10:     if not a or a == "-":
   11:         return 0.0
   12: 
   13:     cleaned = (
   14:         str(a)
   15:         .replace("€", "")
   16:         .replace(".", "")
   17:         .replace(",", ".")
   18:         .strip()
   19:     )
   20: 
   21:     try:
   22:         return float(cleaned)
   23:     except ValueError:
   24:         return 0.0
   25: 
   26: 
   27: def eur(v):
   28:     return f"{v:,.2f} €".replace(",", "X").replace(".", ",").replace("X", ".")

   65: def get_invoice_amount_parts(doc):
   66:     """
   67:     Devuelve base, iva y total de una factura.
   68: 
   69:     Punto importante:
   70:     Las facturas importadas por el conector simulado antiguo tenían solo amount,
   71:     sin base ni tax. Para que el Panel e Informes no se queden a cero,
   72:     si falta base/tax pero existe total, se calcula una estimación con IVA 21%.
   73: 
   74:     Esto es válido para demo/MVP, no para producción fiscal definitiva.
   75:     """
   76: 
   77:     total = amount_to_float(doc.get("amount"))
   78:     base = amount_to_float(doc.get("base"))
   79:     tax = amount_to_float(doc.get("tax"))
   80: 
   81:     if total <= 0:
   82:         return 0.0, 0.0, 0.0, False
   83: 
   84:     if base > 0 and tax >= 0:
   85:         return base, tax, total, False
   86: 
   87:     estimated_base = round(total / 1.21, 2)
   88:     estimated_tax = round(total - estimated_base, 2)
   89: 
   90:     return estimated_base, estimated_tax, total, True
   91: 
   92: 
   93: def descuadre_amount(d):
   94:     base, tax, total, _ = get_invoice_amount_parts(d)
   95: 
   96:     if base <= 0 or total <= 0:
   97:         return 0.0
   98: 
   99:     return round(abs(total - (base + tax)), 2)
  100: 
  101: 
  102: def build_signature(d):
  103:     supplier_key = (
  104:         d.get("cif")
  105:         or d.get("supplier")
  106:         or ""
  107:     ).strip().lower()
  108: 
  109:     _, _, total, _ = get_invoice_amount_parts(d)
  110: 
  111:     invoice_number = (
  112:         d.get("invoice_number")
  113:         or ""
  114:     ).strip().lower()
  115: 
  116:     return f"{supplier_key}|{total:.2f}|{invoice_number}"
  117: 
  118: 
  119: def find_duplicate(new, existing):
  120:     if new.get("document_type") != "Factura proveedor":
  121:         return None
  122: 
  123:     signature = build_signature(new)
  124: 
  125:     for doc in existing:
  126:         if doc.get("document_type") == "Factura proveedor" and build_signature(doc) == signature:
  127:             return doc
  128: 
  129:     return None
  130: 
  131: 
  132: def add_business_days(start, n):
  133:     current = start
  134:     added = 0
  135: 
  136:     while added < n:
  137:         current += timedelta(days=1)
  138: 
  139:         if current.weekday() < 5:
  140:             added += 1
  141: 
  142:     return current
  143: 
  144: 
  145: def compute_deadline(dtype, days=10, urgency_base=None):
  146:     if dtype != "Notificación AEAT":
  147:         return None
  148: 
  149:     today = datetime.now()
  150:     deadline = add_business_days(today, days)
  151:     days_left = (deadline.date() - today.date()).days
  152: 
  153:     urgency = urgency_base
  154: 
  155:     if not urgency:
  156:         if days_left <= 3:
  157:             urgency = "Crítica"
  158:         elif days_left <= 7:
  159:             urgency = "Alta"
  160:         else:
  161:             urgency = "Media"
  162: 
  163:     return {
  164:         "deadline_date": deadline.strftime("%d/%m/%Y"),
  165:         "days_left": days_left,
  166:         "urgency": urgency,
  167:         "note": f"Plazo estimado ({days} días hábiles). Pendiente de validación por asesor fiscal.",
  168:     }
  169: 
  170: 
  171: URGENCY_WEIGHT = {
  172:     "Crítica": 0,
  173:     "Alta": 1,
  174:     "Media": 2,
  175:     "Baja": 3,
  176: }
  177: 
  178: 
  179: def build_notifications(documents):
  180:     notifications = []
  181: 
  182:     for doc in documents:
  183:         if doc.get("document_type") != "Notificación AEAT":
  184:             continue
  185: 
  186:         deadline = doc.get("deadline") or {}
  187:         status = doc.get("status", "Pendiente")
  188: 
  189:         notifications.append(
  190:             {
  191:                 "id": doc["id"],
  192:                 "concept": doc.get(
  193:                     "aeat_type",
  194:                     doc.get("concept", "Requerimiento"),
  195:                 ),
  196:                 "supplier": doc.get("supplier", "Agencia Tributaria"),
  197:                 "filename": doc.get("filename", ""),
  198:                 "date": doc.get("date", "-"),
  199:                 "deadline_date": deadline.get("deadline_date", "-"),
  200:                 "days_left": deadline.get("days_left") if deadline.get("days_left") is not None else "-",
  201:                 "urgency": deadline.get("urgency", "Media"),
  202:                 "status": status,
  203:                 "resolved": any(
  204:                     k in status.lower()
  205:                     for k in (
  206:                         "respuesta",
  207:                         "incidencia",
  208:                         "respondida",
  209:                     )
  210:                 ),
  211:                 "summary": doc.get(
  212:                     "summary",
  213:                     doc.get("reason", ""),
  214:                 ),
  215:                 "requested_docs": doc.get("requested_docs", []),
  216:             }
  217:         )
  218: 
  219:     notifications.sort(
  220:         key=lambda x: URGENCY_WEIGHT.get(
  221:             x["urgency"],
  222:             2,
  223:         )
  224:     )
  225: 
  226:     return {
  227:         "count": len(notifications),
  228:         "open_count": len(
  229:             [
  230:                 x
  231:                 for x in notifications
  232:                 if not x["resolved"]
  233:             ]
  234:         ),
  235:         "notifications": notifications,
  236:     }
  237: 
  238: 
  239: def build_aeat_summary(text, notification_type):
  240:     text = (text or "").strip()
  241: 
  242:     if not text:
  243:         return f"{notification_type} recibida. Requiere revisión del contenido."
  244: 
  245:     lines = [
  246:         line.strip()
  247:         for line in text.splitlines()
  248:         if line.strip()
  249:     ]
  250: 
  251:     head = " ".join(lines[:4])
  252: 
  253:     return head[:257] + "…" if len(head) > 260 else head
  254:

  267:         "Facturas emitidas",

  271:         "Facturas recibidas",

  300: def detect_requested_docs(text):
  301:     haystack = (text or "").lower()
  302: 
  303:     found = [
  304:         label
  305:         for keyword, label in REQUESTED_KEYWORDS
  306:         if keyword in haystack
  307:     ]
  308: 
  309:     return found or [
  310:         "Documentación indicada en el requerimiento",
  311:     ]

  314: def build_aeat_draft(doc):
  315:     fecha = datetime.now().strftime("%d/%m/%Y")
  316: 
  317:     docs = doc.get(
  318:         "requested_docs",
  319:         [
  320:             "la documentación requerida",
  321:         ],
  322:     )
  323: 
  324:     docs_text = "\n".join(
  325:         f"   - {item}"
  326:         for item in docs
  327:     )
  328: 
  329:     ref = (
  330:         doc.get("invoice_number")
  331:         or doc.get("summary", "")[:40]
  332:         or "(referencia)"
  333:     )
  334: 
  335:     return f"""A LA AGENCIA ESTATAL DE ADMINISTRACIÓN TRIBUTARIA
  336: 
  337: Fecha: {fecha}
  338: Expediente / Referencia: {ref}
  339: 
  340: Don/Doña ____________________, con NIF __________, en representación de
  341: ____________________, con CIF __________, EXPONE:
  342: 
  343: Que ha recibido {doc.get('aeat_type','requerimiento')}, en el que se solicita:
  344: 
  345: {docs_text}
  346: 
  347: Que aporta en plazo la documentación requerida, que se adjunta.
  348: 
  349: SOLICITA se tenga por cumplimentado el trámite.
  350: 
  351: Fdo.: ____________________
  352: 
  353: ---
  354: BORRADOR generado por CapaFiscal. Revísalo y valídalo con un asesor fiscal.
  355: """

  358: def build_narrative(d):
  359:     supplier = d.get("supplier", "un proveedor")
  360:     amount = d.get("amount", "-")
  361:     classification = d.get("classification", "sin clasificar")
  362:     document_type = d.get("document_type", "documento")
  363:     status = (d.get("status") or "").lower()
  364: 
  365:     if "respondida" in status:
  366:         return f"Has marcado la notificación de {supplier} como respondida. Trámite cerrado."
  367: 
  368:     if "incidencia" in status:
  369:         return f"Has abierto una incidencia sobre la factura de {supplier} ({amount}). Pendiente de resolución interna."
  370: 
  371:     if "solicitada" in status:
  372:         return f"He solicitado una nueva factura a {supplier} por el descuadre. A la espera de la corregida."
  373: 
  374:     if "duplicado confirmado" in status:
  375:         return f"Has confirmado que la factura de {supplier} es un duplicado. Archivada."
  376: 
  377:     if "respuesta" in status:
  378:         return f"He preparado un borrador de respuesta a la notificación de {supplier}. Revísalo con tu asesor."
  379: 
  380:     if "firmada" in status:
  381:         return f"La nómina de {supplier} ({amount}) ha quedado marcada como firmada."
  382: 
  383:     if "aprobada" in status or "aprobado" in status:
  384:         return f"Has aprobado la factura de {supplier} ({amount}). Lista para contabilizar."
  385: 
  386:     if d.get("is_duplicate"):
  387:         return f"Posible factura duplicada de {supplier} por {amount}. Ya existe la #{d.get('duplicate_of')}. No pagar dos veces."
  388: 
  389:     if document_type == "Notificación AEAT":
  390:         deadline = d.get("deadline")
  391:         aeat_type = d.get("aeat_type", "notificación")
  392: 
  393:         if deadline:
  394:             return f"He recibido un(a) {aeat_type.lower()} de la Agencia Tributaria. Fecha límite estimada: {deadline['deadline_date']} (quedan {deadline['days_left']} días)."
  395: 
  396:         return f"He recibido un(a) {aeat_type.lower()} de la Agencia Tributaria. Requiere revisión prioritaria."
  397: 
  398:     if document_type == "Documento escaneado":
  399:         return "PDF escaneado sin texto legible. Necesito OCR para extraer los datos."
  400: 
  401:     if document_type == "Documento laboral":
  402:         employee = d.get("employee") or "un empleado"
  403:         subtype = d.get("laboral_subtype", "documento laboral")
  404: 
  405:         if subtype == "Nómina del mes":
  406:             return f"He detectado una nómina de {employee} por {amount}. Falta validarla antes de la firma."
  407: 
  408:         return f"He archivado el/la {subtype.lower()} de {employee} en su ficha de empleado."
  409: 
  410:     descuadre = descuadre_amount(d)
  411: 
  412:     if d.get("risk") == "Medio" and descuadre > 0:
  413:         base, tax, _, _ = get_invoice_amount_parts(d)
  414:         suma = eur(base + tax)
  415: 
  416:         return f"Factura de {supplier} con un descuadre de {eur(descuadre)} entre base+IVA ({suma}) y total ({amount}). Revísala antes de contabilizar."
  417: 
  418:     if d.get("risk") == "Medio":
  419:         return f"Factura de {supplier} por {amount} sin confianza suficiente en la clasificación. Revisión manual."
  420: 
  421:     return f"He clasificado automáticamente una factura de {supplier} ({classification}) por {amount} con un {d.get('confidence', 0)}% de confianza. No requiere acción."
  422: 
  423:

  533: def needs_action(d):
  534: 
  535:     status = d.get("status","").lower()
  536: 
  537:     if "incidencia" in status:
  538:         return True
  539: 
  540:     if is_final_state(d):
  541:         return False
  542: 
  543:     return d.get("risk") in (
  544:         "Alto",
  545:         "Medio"
  546:     )
  547: 
  548: 
  549: def task_label(d):
  550: 
  551:     status = d.get("status","").lower()
  552: 
  553:     if "incidencia" in status:
  554:         return f"Resolver incidencia en {d.get('supplier','proveedor')}"
  555:     
  556:     if d.get("is_duplicate"):
  557:         return f"Revisar posible duplicado de {d.get('supplier','proveedor')}"
  558: 
  559:     document_type = d.get("document_type")
  560: 
  561:     if document_type == "Notificación AEAT":
  562:         return f"Atender {d.get('aeat_type','requerimiento').lower()}"
  563: 
  564:     if document_type == "Documento laboral":
  565:         if d.get("laboral_subtype") == "Nómina del mes":
  566:             return f"Validar nómina de {d.get('employee') or 'empleado'}"
  567: 
  568:         return f"Archivar {d.get('laboral_subtype','documento').lower()} de {d.get('employee') or 'empleado'}"
  569: 
  570:     if document_type == "Documento escaneado":
  571:         return "Procesar documento escaneado"
  572: 
  573:     if descuadre_amount(d) > 0:
  574:         return f"Revisar descuadre en factura de {d.get('supplier','proveedor')}"
  575: 
  576:     return f"Revisar factura de {d.get('supplier','proveedor')}"
  577: 
  578: 
  579: PAYROLL_CHECKLIST = [
  580:     "Contrato de trabajo",
  581:     "Nómina del mes",
  582:     "Alta en Seguridad Social",
  583:     "Modelo 145 (IRPF)",
  584: ]

  586: 
  587: def build_payroll(documents):
  588:     employees = {}
  589: 
  590:     for doc in documents:
  591:         if doc.get("document_type") != "Documento laboral":
  592:             continue
  593: 
  594:         name = doc.get("employee") or "Empleado sin identificar"
  595: 
  596:         if name not in employees:
  597:             employees[name] = {
  598:                 "name": name,
  599:                 "docs": [],
  600:                 "subtypes": set(),
  601:                 "amount_total": 0.0,
  602:             }
  603: 
  604:         subtype = doc.get(
  605:             "laboral_subtype",
  606:             "Nómina del mes",
  607:         )
  608: 
  609:         employees[name]["docs"].append(
  610:             {
  611:                 "id": doc["id"],
  612:                 "filename": doc.get("filename", ""),
  613:                 "subtype": subtype,
  614:                 "amount": doc.get("amount", "-"),
  615:                 "date": doc.get("date", "-"),
  616:                 "status": doc.get("status", "Pendiente"),
  617:             }
  618:         )
  619: 
  620:         employees[name]["subtypes"].add(subtype)
  621: 
  622:         if subtype == "Nómina del mes":
  623:             employees[name]["amount_total"] += amount_to_float(
  624:                 doc.get("amount")
  625:             )
  626: 
  627:     result = []
  628: 
  629:     for name, data in employees.items():
  630:         checklist = [
  631:             {
  632:                 "item": item,
  633:                 "done": item in data["subtypes"],
  634:             }
  635:             for item in PAYROLL_CHECKLIST
  636:         ]
  637: 
  638:         missing = [
  639:             item["item"]
  640:             for item in checklist
  641:             if not item["done"]
  642:         ]
  643: 
  644:         payrolls = [
  645:             item
  646:             for item in data["docs"]
  647:             if item["subtype"] == "Nómina del mes"
  648:         ]
  649: 
  650:         result.append(
  651:             {
  652:                 "name": name,
  653:                 "doc_count": len(data["docs"]),
  654:                 "payroll_count": len(payrolls),
  655:                 "amount_total": eur(data["amount_total"]),
  656:                 "docs": data["docs"],
  657:                 "checklist": checklist,
  658:                 "missing": missing,
  659:                 "complete": len(missing) == 0,
  660:             }
  661:         )
  662: 
  663:     result.sort(
  664:         key=lambda employee: employee["name"]
  665:     )
  666: 
  667:     return {
  668:         "employee_count": len(result),
  669:         "employees": result,
  670:     }
  671: 
  672: 
  673: def is_reliable_invoice(doc):
  674:     if doc.get("document_type") != "Factura proveedor":
  675:         return False
  676: 
  677:     if doc.get("is_duplicate"):
  678:         return False
  679: 
  680:     if doc.get("risk") in ("Alto", "Medio"):
  681:         return False
  682: 
  683:     if not (
  684:         is_approved_state(doc)
  685:         or is_auto_reliable_state(doc)
  686:     ):
  687:         return False
  688: 
  689:     base, tax, total, _ = get_invoice_amount_parts(doc)
  690: 
  691:     if base <= 0 or total <= 0:
  692:         return False
  693: 
  694:     if abs((base + tax) - total) >= 0.02:
  695:         return False
  696: 
  697:     return True
  698: 
  699: 
  700: def build_tax_summary(documents):
  701:     total_base = 0.0
  702:     total_iva = 0.0
  703:     total_amount = 0.0
  704:     invoice_count = 0
  705:     pending_count = 0
  706:     categories = {}
  707: 
  708:     for doc in documents:
  709:         if doc.get("document_type") != "Factura proveedor":
  710:             continue
  711: 
  712:         if not is_reliable_invoice(doc):
  713:             pending_count += 1
  714:             continue
  715: 
  716:         base, iva, amount, estimated = get_invoice_amount_parts(doc)
  717: 
  718:         total_base += base
  719:         total_iva += iva
  720:         total_amount += amount
  721:         invoice_count += 1
  722: 
  723:         category = doc.get("classification") or "Sin clasificar"
  724: 
  725:         if estimated:
  726:             category = f"{category} · estimado 21%"
  727: 
  728:         if category not in categories:
  729:             categories[category] = {
  730:                 "count": 0,
  731:                 "base": 0.0,
  732:                 "iva": 0.0,
  733:                 "amount": 0.0,
  734:             }
  735: 
  736:         categories[category]["count"] += 1
  737:         categories[category]["base"] += base
  738:         categories[category]["iva"] += iva
  739:         categories[category]["amount"] += amount
  740: 
  741:     category_rows = [
  742:         {
  743:             "category": name,
  744:             "count": values["count"],
  745:             "base": eur(values["base"]),
  746:             "iva": eur(values["iva"]),
  747:             "amount": eur(values["amount"]),
  748:         }
  749:         for name, values in sorted(
  750:             categories.items(),
  751:             key=lambda item: -item[1]["amount"],
  752:         )
  753:     ]
  754: 
  755:     return {
  756:         "invoice_count": invoice_count,
  757:         "pending_count": pending_count,
  758:         "total_base": eur(total_base),
  759:         "total_iva": eur(total_iva),
  760:         "total_amount": eur(total_amount),
  761:         "raw_total_base": total_base,
  762:         "raw_total_iva": total_iva,
  763:         "raw_total_amount": total_amount,
  764:         "categories": category_rows,
  765:     }
  766: 
  767: 
  768: TIME_PER_DOC_MIN = 8
  769: TIME_PER_RISK_MIN = 15
  770: COST_PER_HOUR = 25.0
  771: 
  772: 
  773: def build_monthly_impact(documents, activity):
  774:     docs = len(documents)
  775: 
  776:     risks = len(
  777:         [
  778:             doc
  779:             for doc in documents
  780:             if doc.get("risk") in ("Alto", "Medio")
  781:             or doc.get("is_duplicate")
  782:         ]
  783:     )
  784: 
  785:     minutes = (
  786:         docs * TIME_PER_DOC_MIN
  787:         + risks * TIME_PER_RISK_MIN
  788:     )
  789: 
  790:     hours = round(minutes / 60, 1)
  791:     cost = round(
  792:         (minutes / 60) * COST_PER_HOUR,
  793:         2,
  794:     )
  795: 
  796:     return {
  797:         "docs_processed": docs,
  798:         "risks_found": risks,
  799:         "automations": len(activity),
  800:         "hours_saved": hours,
  801:         "cost_saved": eur(cost),
  802:         "note": f"Estimación: {TIME_PER_DOC_MIN} min por documento y {TIME_PER_RISK_MIN} min por riesgo analizado, a {eur(COST_PER_HOUR)}/hora.",
  803:     }
  804: 
  805:

 1028: def answer_assistant(question, documents, activity):
 1029:     q = (question or "").lower().strip()
 1030: 
 1031:     if not q:
 1032:         return {
 1033:             "answer": "Hazme una pregunta sobre tus documentos: IVA, plazos de Hacienda, facturas, gasto o empleados.",
 1034:             "sources": [],
 1035:         }

 1038:     notifications = build_notifications(documents)

 1120:             return {

 1122:                 "sources": [],

 1231:         )

   44: def classify_score(score):
   45: 
   46:     if score >= 80:
   47:         return "Factura muy probable"
   48: 
   49:     if score >= 60:
   50:         return "Posible factura"
   51:

   53:         return "Revisar"
   54: 
   55:     return "No parece factura"

   60:     sender = email.get(
   61:         "sender",
   62:         ""
   63:     )
   64:

  112: def process_all_emails():

  141: 
  142: def get_top_candidates(limit=10):
  143: 
  144:     emails = process_all_emails()
  145: 
  146:     emails.sort(
  147:         key=lambda x: x["score"],
  148:         reverse=True
  149:     )
  150: 
  151:     return emails[:limit]

  153: 
  154: def stats():
  155: 
  156:     processed = process_all_emails()

  158:     invoice_count = len([
  159:         e
  160:         for e in processed
  161:         if e["is_invoice"]
  162:     ])

  164:     review_count = len([
  165:         e

  167:         if e["classification"] == "Revisar"
  168:     ])
  169: 
  170:     ignored_count = len([
  171:         e
  172:         for e in processed
  173:         if e["classification"] == "No parece factura"
  174:     ])

   85: 
   86:             score += 5
   87: 
   88:             reasons.append(
   89:                 f"texto contiene {word}"
   90:             )
   91: 
   92:     for sender_name in KNOWN_SENDERS:
   93: 
   94:         if sender_name in sender.lower():
   95: 
   96:             score += 20
   97: 
   98:             reasons.append(
   99:                 f"remitente conocido {sender_name}"
  100:             )
  101: 
  102:             break
  103: 
  104:     score = min(score, 100)
  105: 
  106:     return {
  107:         "score": score,
  108:         "is_invoice": score >= 60,
  109:         "reasons": reasons
  110:     }

   16: BACKEND_DIR = APP_DIR.parent
   17: 
   18: DATA_DIR = BACKEND_DIR / "data"
   19: UPLOAD_DIR = BACKEND_DIR / "uploads"
   20: 
   21: DATA_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   22: UPLOAD_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   23: 
   24: DOCUMENTS_FILE = DATA_DIR / "documents.json"
   25: ACTIVITY_FILE = DATA_DIR / "activity.json"
   26: AUDIT_FILE = DATA_DIR / "audit_log.json"
   27: 
   28: _LOCK = RLock()
   29: 
   30: 
   31: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   32: # Inicialización
   33: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   34: 
   35: def _ensure_json_file(path: Path, default: Any) -> None:
   36:     if not path.exists():
   37:         _atomic_write_json(path, default)
   38: 
   39: 
   40: def _atomic_write_json(path: Path, data: Any) -> None:
   41:     """
   42:     Escribe primero en un archivo temporal y después lo reemplaza.
   43: 
   44:     Evita dejar un JSON incompleto si el proceso se interrumpe durante
   45:     la escritura.
   46:     """
   47:     path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   48: 
   49:     fd, temporary_name = tempfile.mkstemp(
   50:         prefix=f"{path.stem}_",
   51:         suffix=".tmp",
   52:         dir=str(path.parent),
   53:     )
   54: 
   55:     try:
   56:         with os.fdopen(fd, "w", encoding="utf-8") as temporary_file:
   57:             json.dump(
   58:                 data,
   59:                 temporary_file,
   60:                 ensure_ascii=False,
   61:                 indent=2,
   62:             )
   63:             temporary_file.flush()
   64:             os.fsync(temporary_file.fileno())
   65: 
   66:         os.replace(temporary_name, path)
   67:     except Exception:
   68:         try:
   69:             os.unlink(temporary_name)
   70:         except OSError:
   71:             pass
   72:         raise
   73: 
   74: 
   75: def _load_json(path: Path, default: Any) -> Any:
   76:     _ensure_json_file(path, default)
   77: 
   78:     with _LOCK:
   79:         try:
   80:             with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as file:
   81:                 return json.load(file)
   82:         except (json.JSONDecodeError, OSError):
   83:             backup = path.with_suffix(
   84:                 path.suffix + f".corrupt-{datetime.now():%Y%m%d-%H%M%S}"
   85:             )
   86: 
   87:             try:
   88:                 path.replace(backup)
   89:             except OSError:
   90:                 pass
   91: 
   92:             _atomic_write_json(path, default)
   93:             return default
   94: 
   95: 
   96: _ensure_json_file(DOCUMENTS_FILE, [])
   97: _ensure_json_file(ACTIVITY_FILE, [])
   98: _ensure_json_file(AUDIT_FILE, [])
   99: 
  100: 
  101: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  102: # Documentos
  103: # ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
  104: 
  105: def load_documents() -> list[dict]:
  106:     data = _load_json(DOCUMENTS_FILE, [])
  107:     return data if isinstance(data, list) else []
  108: 
  109: 
  110: def save_documents(documents: list[dict]) -> None:
  111:     if not isinstance(documents, list):
  112:         raise TypeError("documents debe ser una lista")
  113: 
  114:     with _LOCK:
  115:         _atomic_write_json(DOCUMENTS_FILE, documents)
  116: 
  117: 
  118: def next_document_id(documents: list[dict] | None = None) -> int:
  119:     """
  120:     Mantiene IDs enteros para no romper el frontend actual, pero evita
  121:     reutilizar len(documentos) + 1.
  122:     """
  123:     documents = documents if documents is not None else load_documents()
  124: 
  125:     valid_ids = [
  126:         document.get("id")
  127:         for document in documents
  128:         if isinstance(document.get("id"), int)
  129:     ]
  130: 
  131:     return max(valid_ids, default=0) + 1
  132: 
  133:

  135:     return next(
  136:         (
  137:             document
  138:             for document in load_documents()
  139:             if document.get("id") == document_id
  140:         ),
  141:         None,
  142:     )

  151: 
  152: 
  153: def find_document_by_hash(file_hash: str) -> dict | None:

  155:         return None
  156: 
  157:     return next(
  158:         (
  159:             document
  160:             for document in load_documents()

  163:         None,

  168:     """
  169:     Elimina rutas incluidas en el nombre recibido y conserva únicamente
  170:     el nombre final del archivo.
  171:     """

   15: class ApiError extends Error {
   16:     constructor(message, status, data = null) {
   17:         super(message);
   18:         this.name = "ApiError";
   19:         this.status = status;
   20:         this.data = data;
   21:     }
   22: }
   23: 
   24: 
   25: function apiUrl(path) {
   26:     const value = String(path || "");
   27: 
   28:     if (value.startsWith("/api/")) {
   29:         return value;
   30:     }
   31: 
   32:     if (value === "/api") {
   33:         return value;
   34:     }
   35: 
   36:     if (value.startsWith("/")) {
   37:         return `${API_BASE}${value}`;
   38:     }
   39: 
   40:     return `${API_BASE}/${value}`;
   41: }
   42: 
   43: 
   44: async function apiRequest(path, options = {}) {
   45:     let response;
   46: 
   47:     try {
   48:         response = await fetch(apiUrl(path), options);
   49:     } catch (error) {
   50:         throw new ApiError(
   51:             "No se ha podido conectar con el servidor.",
   52:             0,
   53:             {
   54:                 originalError: error,
   55:             }
   56:         );
   57:     }
   58: 
   59:     const contentType =
   60:         response.headers.get("content-type") || "";
   61: 
   62:     let data = null;
   63: 
   64:     try {
   65:         if (contentType.includes("application/json")) {
   66:             data = await response.json();
   67:         } else {
   68:             const text = await response.text();
   69:             data = text || null;
   70:         }
   71:     } catch (error) {
   72:         console.error(
   73:             "No se pudo interpretar la respuesta:",
   74:             error
   75:         );
   76:     }
   77: 
   78:     if (!response.ok) {
   79:         const detail = data?.detail ?? data;
   80: 
   81:         let message = `Error HTTP ${response.status}`;
   82: 
   83:         if (typeof detail === "string") {
   84:             message = detail;
   85:         } else if (detail?.message) {
   86:             message = detail.message;
   87:         } else if (data?.message) {
   88:             message = data.message;
   89:         }
   90: 
   91:         throw new ApiError(
   92:             message,
   93:             response.status,
   94:             detail
   95:         );
   96:     }
   97: 
   98:     return data;
   99: }

  115: function escapeHtml(value) {
  116:     return String(value ?? "")
  117:         .replaceAll("&", "&amp;")
  118:         .replaceAll("<", "&lt;")
  119:         .replaceAll(">", "&gt;")
  120:         .replaceAll('"', "&quot;")
  121:         .replaceAll("'", "&#039;");
  122: }

  125: function formatMoney(value, currency = "EUR") {
  126:     if (
  127:         value === null ||
  128:         value === undefined ||
  129:         value === ""
  130:     ) {
  131:         return "—";
  132:     }
  133: 
  134:     const amount = Number(value);
  135: 
  136:     if (!Number.isFinite(amount)) {
  137:         return escapeHtml(value);
  138:     }
  139: 
  140:     try {
  141:         return new Intl.NumberFormat("es-ES", {
  142:             style: "currency",
  143:             currency: currency || "EUR",
  144:         }).format(amount);
  145:     } catch {
  146:         return `${amount.toFixed(2)} ${currency || "EUR"}`;
  147:     }
  148: }
  149: 
  150: 
  151: function formatDate(value) {
  152:     if (!value) {
  153:         return "—";
  154:     }
  155: 
  156:     const date = new Date(value);
  157: 
  158:     if (Number.isNaN(date.getTime())) {
  159:         return escapeHtml(value);
  160:     }
  161: 
  162:     return new Intl.DateTimeFormat("es-ES", {
  163:         dateStyle: "medium",
  164:         timeStyle: "short",
  165:     }).format(date);
  166: }
  167: 
  168: 
  169: function dateInputValue(value) {
  170:     if (!value) {
  171:         return "";
  172:     }
  173: 
  174:     return String(value).slice(0, 10);
  175: }

  191:     return [];
  192: }

  195: function translateStatus(status) {
  196:     const statuses = {
  197:         RECEIVED: "Recibido",
  198:         PROCESSING: "Procesando",
  199:         EXTRACTED: "Extraído",
  200:         NEEDS_REVIEW: "Requiere revisión",
  201:         READY_FOR_APPROVAL: "Pendiente de aprobación",
  202:         APPROVED: "Aprobada",
  203:         REJECTED: "Rechazada",
  204:         FAILED: "Incidencia",
  205:         EXPORTED: "Exportada",
  206:     };
  207: 
  208:     return statuses[status] || status || "Pendiente";
  209: }

  212: function statusClassFromBackend(status) {
  213:     const value = String(status || "").toUpperCase();
  214: 
  215:     if (["FAILED", "REJECTED"].includes(value)) {
  216:         return "status-danger";
  217:     }
  218: 
  219:     if (
  220:         [
  221:             "RECEIVED",
  222:             "PROCESSING",
  223:             "EXTRACTED",
  224:             "NEEDS_REVIEW",
  225:             "READY_FOR_APPROVAL",
  226:         ].includes(value)
  227:     ) {
  228:         return "status-warning";
  229:     }
  230: 
  231:     if (["APPROVED", "EXPORTED"].includes(value)) {
  232:         return "status-success";
  233:     }
  234: 
  235:     return "status-neutral";
  236: }

  239: function confidenceClass(confidence) {
  240:     const number = Number(confidence || 0);
  241: 
  242:     if (number >= 90) {
  243:         return "risk-low";
  244:     }
  245: 
  246:     if (number >= 75) {
  247:         return "risk-medium";
  248:     }
  249: 
  250:     return "risk-high";
  251: }

  254: function requiresReview(documentItem) {
  255:     const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};
  256:     const confidence = Number(invoice.confidence || 0);
  257: 
  258:     return (
  259:         documentItem.status === "NEEDS_REVIEW" ||
  260:         documentItem.status === "FAILED" ||
  261:         !invoice.supplier_name ||
  262:         !invoice.supplier_tax_id ||
  263:         !invoice.invoice_number ||
  264:         !invoice.invoice_date ||
  265:         invoice.subtotal === null ||
  266:         invoice.subtotal === undefined ||
  267:         invoice.tax_total === null ||
  268:         invoice.tax_total === undefined ||
  269:         invoice.total === null ||
  270:         invoice.total === undefined ||
  271:         confidence < 80
  272:     );
  273: }

  276: function nullableNumber(elementId) {
  277:     const element = document.getElementById(elementId);
  278: 
  279:     if (!element) {
  280:         return null;
  281:     }
  282: 
  283:     const value = element.value.trim();
  284: 
  285:     if (!value) {
  286:         return null;
  287:     }
  288: 
  289:     const number = Number(value);
  290: 
  291:     return Number.isFinite(number) ? number : null;
  292: }

  376: function renderDashboard(documents) {
  377:     const pending = documents.filter((documentItem) => {
  378:         return [
  379:             "RECEIVED",
  380:             "PROCESSING",
  381:             "EXTRACTED",
  382:             "NEEDS_REVIEW",
  383:             "READY_FOR_APPROVAL",
  384:         ].includes(documentItem.status);
  385:     });
  386: 
  387:     const risks = documents.filter(requiresReview);
  388: 
  389:     const approved = documents.filter((documentItem) => {
  390:         return documentItem.status === "APPROVED";
  391:     });
  392: 
  393:     const approvedTotal = approved.reduce(
  394:         (sum, documentItem) => {
  395:             const total = Number(
  396:                 documentItem.invoice?.total || 0
  397:             );
  398: 
  399:             return sum + (
  400:                 Number.isFinite(total) ? total : 0
  401:             );
  402:         },
  403:         0
  404:     );
  405: 
  406:     const taxTotal = approved.reduce(
  407:         (sum, documentItem) => {
  408:             const tax = Number(
  409:                 documentItem.invoice?.tax_total || 0
  410:             );
  411: 
  412:             return sum + (
  413:                 Number.isFinite(tax) ? tax : 0
  414:             );
  415:         },
  416:         0
  417:     );
  418: 
  419:     const subtotal = approved.reduce(
  420:         (sum, documentItem) => {
  421:             const value = Number(
  422:                 documentItem.invoice?.subtotal || 0
  423:             );
  424: 
  425:             return sum + (
  426:                 Number.isFinite(value) ? value : 0
  427:             );
  428:         },
  429:         0
  430:     );
  431: 
  432:     setText(
  433:         "greetingLine",
  434:         "Resumen de documentación real"
  435:     );
  436:     setText("mRisks", risks.length);
  437:     setText("mPending", pending.length);
  438:     setText("mSpend", formatMoney(approvedTotal));
  439:     setText("mIva", formatMoney(taxTotal));
  440:     setText("cntFacturas", documents.length);
  441:     setText("cntAeat", "0");
  442:     setText("cntEmp", "0");
  443:     setText("txCount", approved.length);
  444:     setText("txBase", formatMoney(subtotal));
  445:     setText("txIva", formatMoney(taxTotal));
  446:     setText("txTotal", formatMoney(approvedTotal));
  447:     setText("roiDocs", documents.length);
  448:     setText("roiRisks", risks.length);
  449:     setText("roiHours", "—");
  450:     setText("roiCost", "—");
  451:     setText(
  452:         "roiNote",
  453:         "Métricas calculadas a partir de documentos reales."
  454:     );
  455: }

  476:         aeatList.innerHTML = emptyState(
  477:             "📭",
  478:             "Sin integración con la AEAT",
  479:             "Este módulo se habilitará en una fase posterior."
  480:         );
  481:     }
  482: 
  483:     if (payrollList) {
  484:         payrollList.innerHTML = emptyState(
  485:             "👥",
  486:             "Gestión laboral no habilitada",
  487:             "El MVP actual está centrado en facturas recibidas."
  488:         );
  489:     }
  490: 
  491:     if (connectors) {
  492:         connectors.innerHTML = emptyState(
  493:             "🔌",
  494:             "Sin conectores activos",
  495:             "La carga manual es la única fuente habilitada."
  496:         );
  497:     }
  498: }
  499: 
  500: 
  501: function renderDocuments(documents) {
  502:     const container =
  503:         document.getElementById("documentsList");
  504: 
  505:     if (!container) {
  506:         return;
  507:     }
  508: 
  509:     if (!documents.length) {
  510:         container.innerHTML = emptyState(
  511:             "📄",
  512:             "Todavía no hay documentos",
  513:             "Sube una factura PDF para comenzar."
  514:         );
  515:         return;
  516:     }
  517: 
  518:     container.innerHTML = documents
  519:         .map(realDocumentCard)
  520:         .join("");
  521: }
  522: 
  523: 
  524: function renderInbox(documents) {
  525:     const container =
  526:         document.getElementById("inboxList");
  527: 
  528:     if (!container) {
  529:         return;
  530:     }
  531: 
  532:     if (!documents.length) {
  533:         container.innerHTML = emptyState(
  534:             "📥",
  535:             "Bandeja vacía",
  536:             "Los documentos cargados aparecerán aquí."
  537:         );
  538:         return;
  539:     }
  540: 
  541:     container.innerHTML = documents
  542:         .slice(0, 5)
  543:         .map(realDocumentCard)
  544:         .join("");
  545: }
  546: 
  547: 
  548: function realDocumentCard(documentItem) {
  549:     const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};
  550:     const confidence = Number(invoice.confidence || 0);
  551:     const reviewRequired = requiresReview(documentItem);
  552:     const documentId = Number(documentItem.id);
  553:     const invoiceId = Number(invoice.id);
  554: 
  555:     const canReview = (
  556:         invoice.id &&
  557:         !["APPROVED", "REJECTED"].includes(
  558:             documentItem.status
  559:         )
  560:     );
  561: 
  562:     return `
  563:         <article class="document-card ${
  564:             reviewRequired ? "needs-review" : ""
  565:         }">
  566:             <div class="document-top">
  567:                 <div>
  568:                     <p class="doc-type">
  569:                         Factura recibida
  570:                         ${
  571:                             documentItem.source ===
  572:                             "manual_upload"
  573:                                 ? `
  574:                                     <span class="source-tag">
  575:                                         ⬆ carga manual
  576:                                     </span>
  577:                                 `
  578:                                 : ""
  579:                         }
  580:                     </p>
  581: 
  582:                     <h3>
  583:                         ${escapeHtml(
  584:                             invoice.supplier_name ||
  585:                             "Proveedor sin identificar"
  586:                         )}
  587:                     </h3>
  588: 
  589:                     <p class="filename">
  590:                         ${escapeHtml(
  591:                             documentItem.original_filename ||
  592:                             "Sin nombre"
  593:                         )}
  594:                     </p>
  595:                 </div>
  596: 
  597:                 <div class="badges">
  598:                     ${
  599:                         reviewRequired
  600:                             ? `
  601:                                 <span class="
  602:                                     priority-pill
  603:                                     priority-high
  604:                                 ">
  605:                                     Revisar
  606:                                 </span>
  607:                             `
  608:                             : `
  609:                                 <span class="
  610:                                     priority-pill
  611:                                     priority-low
  612:                                 ">
  613:                                     Normal
  614:                                 </span>
  615:                             `
  616:                     }
  617: 
  618:                     <span class="
  619:                         status-pill
  620:                         ${statusClassFromBackend(
  621:                             documentItem.status
  622:                         )}
  623:                     ">
  624:                         ${escapeHtml(
  625:                             translateStatus(
  626:                                 documentItem.status
  627:                             )
  628:                         )}
  629:                     </span>
  630:                 </div>
  631:             </div>
  632: 
  633:             <div class="document-grid">
  634:                 <div>
  635:                     <span>Número</span>
  636:                     <strong>
  637:                         ${escapeHtml(
  638:                             invoice.invoice_number || "—"
  639:                         )}
  640:                     </strong>
  641:                 </div>
  642: 
  643:                 <div>
  644:                     <span>Fecha</span>
  645:                     <strong>
  646:                         ${escapeHtml(
  647:                             invoice.invoice_date || "—"
  648:                         )}
  649:                     </strong>
  650:                 </div>
  651: 
  652:                 <div>
  653:                     <span>Importe</span>
  654:                     <strong>
  655:                         ${formatMoney(
  656:                             invoice.total,
  657:                             invoice.currency
  658:                         )}
  659:                     </strong>
  660:                 </div>
  661: 
  662:                 <div>
  663:                     <span>Confianza</span>
  664:                     <strong class="${
  665:                         confidenceClass(confidence)
  666:                     }">
  667:                         ${confidence}%
  668:                     </strong>
  669:                 </div>
  670:             </div>
  671: 
  672:             <div class="card-actions">
  673:                 <button
  674:                     class="btn-ghost"
  675:                     type="button"
  676:                     onclick="showDetail(${documentId})"
  677:                 >
  678:                     Ver detalle
  679:                 </button>
  680: 
  681:                 <a
  682:                     class="btn-ghost"
  683:                     href="/api/documents/${documentId}/file"
  684:                     target="_blank"
  685:                     rel="noopener noreferrer"
  686:                 >
  687:                     Abrir archivo
  688:                 </a>
  689: 
  690:                 ${
  691:                     canReview
  692:                         ? `
  693:                             <button
  694:                                 class="act-btn act-primary"
  695:                                 type="button"
  696:                                 onclick="
  697:                                     approveInvoice(
  698:                                         ${invoiceId},
  699:                                         ${documentId},
  700:                                         this
  701:                                     )
  702:                                 "
  703:                             >
  704:                                 Aprobar
  705:                             </button>
  706: 
  707:                             <button
  708:                                 class="act-btn act-danger"
  709:                                 type="button"
  710:                                 onclick="
  711:                                     rejectInvoice(
  712:                                         ${invoiceId},
  713:                                         ${documentId}
  714:                                     )
  715:                                 "
  716:                             >
  717:                                 Rechazar
  718:                             </button>
  719:                         `
  720:                         : ""
  721:                 }
  722:             </div>
  723:         </article>
  724:     `;
  725: }

  728: /* ================================================================
  729:    CARGA
  730: ================================================================ */
  731: 
  732: function setupUpload() {
  733:     const fileInput =
  734:         document.getElementById("fileInput");
  735: 
  736:     if (!fileInput) {
  737:         console.warn("No se encontró #fileInput.");
  738:         return;
  739:     }
  740: 
  741:     fileInput.addEventListener("change", async () => {
  742:         if (fileInput.files?.length) {
  743:             await uploadSelectedFile(fileInput);
  744:         }
  745:     });
  746: }
  747: 
  748: 
  749: async function uploadSelectedFile(fileInput) {
  750:     const file = fileInput.files?.[0];
  751: 
  752:     if (!file) {
  753:         showMessage(
  754:             "Selecciona primero un archivo.",
  755:             "warning"
  756:         );
  757:         return;
  758:     }
  759: 
  760:     const lowerName = file.name.toLowerCase();
  761: 
  762:     if (
  763:         !lowerName.endsWith(".pdf") &&
  764:         !lowerName.endsWith(".txt")
  765:     ) {
  766:         showMessage(
  767:             "El archivo debe ser PDF o TXT.",
  768:             "error"
  769:         );
  770:         fileInput.value = "";
  771:         return;
  772:     }
  773: 
  774:     const maxBytes = 20 * 1024 * 1024;
  775: 
  776:     if (file.size > maxBytes) {
  777:         showMessage(
  778:             "El archivo supera el límite de 20 MB.",
  779:             "error"
  780:         );
  781:         fileInput.value = "";
  782:         return;
  783:     }
  784: 
  785:     try {
  786:         showMessage(
  787:             `Procesando "${file.name}"…`,
  788:             "info"
  789:         );
  790: 
  791:         const formData = new FormData();
  792: 
  793:         formData.append(
  794:             "uploaded_file",
  795:             file,
  796:             file.name
  797:         );
  798: 
  799:         const result = await apiRequest("/upload", {
  800:             method: "POST",
  801:             body: formData,
  802:         });
  803: 
  804:         showMessage(
  805:             result.message ||
  806:             `Documento "${file.name}" procesado.`,
  807:             result.duplicate ? "warning" : "success"
  808:         );
  809: 
  810:         fileInput.value = "";
  811: 
  812:         await loadDocuments();
  813: 
  814:         const uploadedDocumentId = result?.document?.id;
  815: 
  816:         if (uploadedDocumentId) {
  817:             await showDetail(uploadedDocumentId);
  818:         }
  819:     } catch (error) {
  820:         console.error("Error al subir:", error);
  821: 
  822:         showMessage(
  823:             `No se pudo procesar el archivo: ${
  824:                 error.message
  825:             }`,
  826:             "error"
  827:         );
  828: 
  829:         fileInput.value = "";
  830:     }
  831: }
  832: 
  833: 
  834: /* ================================================================
  835:    DETALLE
  836: ================================================================ */
  837: 
  838: function ensureDetailDialog() {
  839:     let dialog = document.getElementById(
  840:         "documentDetailDialog"
  841:     );
  842: 
  843:     if (dialog) {
  844:         return dialog;
  845:     }
  846: 
  847:     dialog = document.createElement("dialog");
  848:     dialog.id = "documentDetailDialog";
  849:     dialog.className = "document-detail-dialog";
  850: 
  851:     dialog.innerHTML = `
  852:         <div class="detail-dialog-header">
  853:             <h2>Detalle de factura</h2>
  854: 
  855:             <button
  856:                 type="button"
  857:                 class="btn-ghost"
  858:                 id="closeDetailDialog"
  859:             >
  860:                 ✕ Cerrar
  861:             </button>
  862:         </div>
  863: 
  864:         <div id="documentDetailContent"></div>
  865:     `;
  866: 
  867:     document.body.appendChild(dialog);
  868: 
  869:     document
  870:         .getElementById("closeDetailDialog")
  871:         ?.addEventListener(
  872:             "click",
  873:             () => dialog.close()
  874:         );
  875: 
  876:     dialog.addEventListener("click", (event) => {
  877:         if (event.target === dialog) {
  878:             dialog.close();
  879:         }
  880:     });
  881: 
  882:     return dialog;
  883: }
  884:

  886: async function showDetail(documentId) {
  887:     const dialog = ensureDetailDialog();
  888:     const content = document.getElementById(
  889:         "documentDetailContent"
  890:     );
  891: 
  892:     content.innerHTML = "<p>Cargando documento…</p>";
  893:

  895:         dialog.showModal();
  896:     }

  898:     try {
  899:         const documentItem = await apiRequest(
  900:             `/documents/${documentId}`
  901:         );
  902: 
  903:         const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};
  904:         const invoiceId = Number(invoice.id);
  905:         const canReview = (
  906:             invoice.id &&
  907:             !["APPROVED", "REJECTED"].includes(
  908:                 documentItem.status

  917:                         id="detailSupplierName"

  921:                             invoice.supplier_name || ""
  922:                         )}"

  924:                 </label>

  927:                     NIF proveedor
  928:                     <input
  929:                         id="detailSupplierTaxId"
  930:                         data-invoice-field="supplier_tax_id"

  932:                         value="${escapeHtml(
  933:                             invoice.supplier_tax_id || ""
  934:                         )}"

  941:                         id="detailInvoiceNumber"

  945:                             invoice.invoice_number || ""

  953:                         id="detailInvoiceDate"

  955:                         type="date"
  956:                         value="${escapeHtml(
  957:                             dateInputValue(
  958:                                 invoice.invoice_date
  959:                             )
  960:                         )}"
  961:                     >
  962:                 </label>
  963: 
  964:                 <label>
  965:                     Base imponible
  966:                     <input
  967:                         id="detailSubtotal"
  968:                         data-invoice-field="subtotal"
  969:                         type="number"
  970:                         step="0.01"
  971:                         value="${escapeHtml(
  972:                             invoice.subtotal ?? ""
  973:                         )}"
  974:                     >
  975:                 </label>
  976: 
  977:                 <label>
  978:                     IVA
  979:                     <input
  980:                         id="detailTaxTotal"
  981:                         data-invoice-field="tax_total"
  982:                         type="number"
  983:                         step="0.01"
  984:                         value="${escapeHtml(
  985:                             invoice.tax_total ?? ""
  986:                         )}"
  987:                     >
  988:                 </label>
  989: 
  990:                 <label>
  991:                     Total
  992:                     <input
  993:                         id="detailTotal"
  994:                         data-invoice-field="total"
  995:                         type="number"
  996:                         step="0.01"
  997:                         value="${escapeHtml(
  998:                             invoice.total ?? ""
  999:                         )}"
 1000:                     >
 1001:                 </label>
 1002: 
 1003:                 <label>
 1004:                     Moneda
 1005:                     <input
 1006:                         id="detailCurrency"
 1007:                         data-invoice-field="currency"
 1008:                         type="text"
 1009:                         maxlength="3"
 1010:                         value="${escapeHtml(
 1011:                             invoice.currency || "EUR"
 1012:                         )}"
 1013:                     >

 1015:             </div>
 1016: 
 1017:             <div class="detail-summary">
 1018:                 <p>
 1019:                     <strong>Estado:</strong>
 1020:                     ${escapeHtml(
 1021:                         translateStatus(

 1023:                         )
 1024:                     )}
 1025:                 </p>
 1026: 
 1027:                 <p>
 1028:                     <strong>Confianza:</strong>
 1029:                     ${Number(invoice.confidence || 0)}%
 1030:                 </p>
 1031:

 1033:                     <strong>Archivo:</strong>
 1034:                     ${escapeHtml(
 1035:                         documentItem.original_filename
 1036:                     )}
 1037:                 </p>
