 1040:                     <strong>Creado:</strong>
 1041:                     ${formatDate(
 1042:                         documentItem.created_at
 1043:                     )}
 1044:                 </p>
 1045:             </div>
 1046: 
 1047:             <div class="card-actions">
 1048:                 ${
 1049:                     invoice.id
 1050:                         ? `
 1051:                             <button
 1052:                                 type="button"
 1053:                                 class="act-btn act-primary"
 1054:                                 onclick="
 1055:                                     saveInvoice(
 1056:                                         ${invoiceId},
 1057:                                         ${Number(
 1058:                                             documentItem.id
 1059:                                         )}
 1060:                                     )
 1061:                                 "
 1062:                             >
 1063:                                 Guardar correcciones
 1064:                             </button>
 1065:                         `
 1066:                         : ""
 1067:                 }
 1068: 
 1069:                 ${
 1070:                     canReview
 1071:                         ? `
 1072:                             <button
 1073:                                 type="button"
 1074:                                 class="act-btn act-primary"
 1075:                                 onclick="
 1076:                                     approveInvoice(
 1077:                                         ${invoiceId},
 1078:                                         ${Number(
 1079:                                             documentItem.id
 1080:                                         )},
 1081:                                         this
 1082:                                     )
 1083:                                 "
 1084:                             >
 1085:                                 Aprobar
 1086:                             </button>
 1087: 
 1088:                             <button
 1089:                                 type="button"
 1090:                                 class="act-btn act-danger"
 1091:                                 onclick="
 1092:                                     rejectInvoice(
 1093:                                         ${invoiceId},
 1094:                                         ${Number(
 1095:                                             documentItem.id
 1096:                                         )}
 1097:                                     )
 1098:                                 "
 1099:                             >
 1100:                                 Rechazar
 1101:                             </button>
 1102:                         `
 1103:                         : ""
 1104:                 }
 1105: 
 1106:                 <button
 1107:                     type="button"
 1108:                     class="btn-ghost"
 1109:                     onclick="
 1110:                         reprocessDocument(
 1111:                             ${Number(documentItem.id)}
 1112:                         )
 1113:                     "
 1114:                 >
 1115:                     Reprocesar
 1116:                 </button>
 1117: 
 1118:                 <a
 1119:                     class="btn-ghost"
 1120:                     href="/api/documents/${Number(
 1121:                         documentItem.id
 1122:                     )}/file"
 1123:                     target="_blank"
 1124:                     rel="noopener noreferrer"
 1125:                 >
 1126:                     Abrir archivo
 1127:                 </a>
 1128:             </div>
 1129:         `;
 1130:     } catch (error) {
 1131:         content.innerHTML = emptyState(
 1132:             "⚠️",
 1133:             "No se pudo abrir el documento",
 1134:             error.message
 1135:         );
 1136:     }
 1137: }

 1140: /* ================================================================
 1141:    REVISIÓN
 1142: ================================================================ */
 1143: 
 1144: async function saveInvoice(invoiceId, documentId) {
 1145:     const body = {
 1146:         supplier_name:
 1147:             document
 1148:                 .getElementById("detailSupplierName")
 1149:                 ?.value.trim() || null,
 1150: 
 1151:         supplier_tax_id:
 1152:             document
 1153:                 .getElementById("detailSupplierTaxId")
 1154:                 ?.value.trim() || null,
 1155: 
 1156:         invoice_number:
 1157:             document
 1158:                 .getElementById("detailInvoiceNumber")
 1159:                 ?.value.trim() || null,
 1160: 
 1161:         invoice_date:
 1162:             document
 1163:                 .getElementById("detailInvoiceDate")
 1164:                 ?.value || null,
 1165: 
 1166:         subtotal: nullableNumber("detailSubtotal"),
 1167:         tax_total: nullableNumber("detailTaxTotal"),
 1168:         total: nullableNumber("detailTotal"),
 1169: 
 1170:         currency:
 1171:             document
 1172:                 .getElementById("detailCurrency")
 1173:                 ?.value.trim()
 1174:                 .toUpperCase() || "EUR",
 1175:     };
 1176: 
 1177:     try {
 1178:         await apiRequest(`/invoices/${invoiceId}`, {
 1179:             method: "PATCH",
 1180:             headers: {
 1181:                 "Content-Type": "application/json",
 1182:             },
 1183:             body: JSON.stringify(body),
 1184:         });
 1185: 
 1186:         showMessage(
 1187:             "Correcciones guardadas correctamente.",
 1188:             "success"
 1189:         );
 1190: 
 1191:         await loadDocuments();
 1192:         await showDetail(documentId);
 1193:     } catch (error) {
 1194:         showMessage(
 1195:             `No se pudieron guardar los cambios: ${
 1196:                 error.message
 1197:             }`,
 1198:             "error"
 1199:         );
 1200:     }
 1201: }
 1202: 
 1203: 
 1204: function formatMissingFields(missingFields) {
 1205:     if (
 1206:         !Array.isArray(missingFields) ||
 1207:         !missingFields.length
 1208:     ) {
 1209:         return "• Campos sin identificar";
 1210:     }
 1211: 
 1212:     return missingFields
 1213:         .map((item) => {
 1214:             return `• ${
 1215:                 item?.label ||
 1216:                 item?.field ||
 1217:                 "Campo desconocido"
 1218:             }`;
 1219:         })
 1220:         .join("\n");
 1221: }
 1222: 
 1223: 
 1224: function highlightMissingFields(missingFields) {
 1225:     document
 1226:         .querySelectorAll("[data-invoice-field]")
 1227:         .forEach((element) => {
 1228:             element.classList.remove("field-missing");
 1229:             element.removeAttribute("aria-invalid");
 1230:         });
 1231: 
 1232:     if (!Array.isArray(missingFields)) {
 1233:         return;
 1234:     }
 1235: 
 1236:     let firstElement = null;
 1237: 
 1238:     missingFields.forEach((item) => {
 1239:         const fieldName = item?.field;
 1240: 
 1241:         if (!fieldName) {
 1242:             return;
 1243:         }
 1244: 
 1245:         const element = document.querySelector(
 1246:             `[data-invoice-field="${fieldName}"]`
 1247:         );
 1248: 
 1249:         if (!element) {
 1250:             return;
 1251:         }
 1252: 
 1253:         element.classList.add("field-missing");
 1254:         element.setAttribute("aria-invalid", "true");
 1255: 
 1256:         if (!firstElement) {
 1257:             firstElement = element;
 1258:         }
 1259:     });
 1260: 
 1261:     if (firstElement) {
 1262:         firstElement.scrollIntoView({
 1263:             behavior: "smooth",
 1264:             block: "center",
 1265:         });
 1266: 
 1267:         firstElement.focus();
 1268:     }
 1269: }
 1270: 
 1271: 
 1272: async function sendApproval(invoiceId, force) {
 1273:     return apiRequest(
 1274:         `/invoices/${invoiceId}/approve`,
 1275:         {
 1276:             method: "POST",
 1277:             headers: {
 1278:                 "Content-Type": "application/json",
 1279:             },
 1280:             body: JSON.stringify({
 1281:                 force: Boolean(force),
 1282:             }),
 1283:         }
 1284:     );
 1285: }
 1286: 
 1287: 
 1288: async function approveInvoice(
 1289:     invoiceId,
 1290:     documentId = null,
 1291:     button = null
 1292: ) {
 1293:     if (!invoiceId) {
 1294:         showMessage(
 1295:             "No se ha encontrado el identificador de la factura.",
 1296:             "error"
 1297:         );
 1298:         return;
 1299:     }
 1300: 
 1301:     if (approvalInProgress) {
 1302:         return;
 1303:     }
 1304: 
 1305:     approvalInProgress = true;
 1306: 
 1307:     const originalText = button?.textContent || "Aprobar";
 1308: 
 1309:     if (button) {
 1310:         button.disabled = true;
 1311:         button.textContent = "Comprobando…";
 1312:     }
 1313: 
 1314:     try {
 1315:         const result = await sendApproval(
 1316:             invoiceId,
 1317:             false
 1318:         );
 1319: 
 1320:         showMessage(
 1321:             result.message ||
 1322:             "Factura aprobada correctamente.",
 1323:             result.approved_with_warnings
 1324:                 ? "warning"
 1325:                 : "success"
 1326:         );
 1327: 
 1328:         await loadDocuments();
 1329: 
 1330:         if (documentId) {
 1331:             await showDetail(documentId);
 1332:         }
 1333:     } catch (error) {
 1334:         const detail = error.data || {};
 1335: 
 1336:         const isMissingFieldsWarning = (
 1337:             error instanceof ApiError &&
 1338:             error.status === 409 &&
 1339:             detail.code === "MISSING_FIELDS"
 1340:         );
 1341: 
 1342:         if (isMissingFieldsWarning) {
 1343:             const missingFields =
 1344:                 detail.missing_fields || [];
 1345: 
 1346:             const fieldsText =
 1347:                 formatMissingFields(missingFields);
 1348: 
 1349:             const approveAnyway = window.confirm(
 1350:                 `${
 1351:                     detail.message ||
 1352:                     "La factura está incompleta."
 1353:                 }\n\n` +
 1354:                 `Campos pendientes:\n${fieldsText}\n\n` +
 1355:                 "Aceptar: aprobar con advertencias.\n" +
 1356:                 "Cancelar: volver para completar los campos."
 1357:             );
 1358: 
 1359:             if (!approveAnyway) {
 1360:                 showMessage(
 1361:                     "La factura no se ha aprobado. " +
 1362:                     "Puedes completar los campos pendientes.",
 1363:                     "info"
 1364:                 );
 1365: 
 1366:                 if (documentId) {
 1367:                     await showDetail(documentId);
 1368: 
 1369:                     window.setTimeout(() => {
 1370:                         highlightMissingFields(
 1371:                             missingFields
 1372:                         );
 1373:                     }, 100);
 1374:                 }
 1375: 
 1376:                 return;
 1377:             }
 1378: 
 1379:             if (button) {
 1380:                 button.textContent =
 1381:                     "Aprobando con advertencias…";
 1382:             }
 1383: 
 1384:             try {
 1385:                 const forcedResult = await sendApproval(
 1386:                     invoiceId,
 1387:                     true
 1388:                 );
 1389: 
 1390:                 showMessage(
 1391:                     forcedResult.message ||
 1392:                     "Factura aprobada con advertencias.",
 1393:                     "warning"
 1394:                 );
 1395: 
 1396:                 await loadDocuments();
 1397: 
 1398:                 if (documentId) {
 1399:                     await showDetail(documentId);
 1400:                 }
 1401:             } catch (forcedError) {
 1402:                 console.error(
 1403:                     "Error en aprobación forzada:",
 1404:                     forcedError
 1405:                 );
 1406: 
 1407:                 showMessage(
 1408:                     forcedError.message ||
 1409:                     "No se pudo aprobar la factura.",
 1410:                     "error"
 1411:                 );
 1412:             }
 1413: 
 1414:             return;
 1415:         }
 1416: 
 1417:         console.error("Error al aprobar:", error);
 1418: 
 1419:         showMessage(
 1420:             error.message ||
 1421:             "No se pudo aprobar la factura.",
 1422:             "error"
 1423:         );
 1424:     } finally {
 1425:         approvalInProgress = false;
 1426: 
 1427:         if (button?.isConnected) {
 1428:             button.disabled = false;
 1429:             button.textContent = originalText;
 1430:         }
 1431:     }
 1432: }
 1433: 
 1434: 
 1435: async function rejectInvoice(invoiceId, documentId) {
 1436:     const reason = window.prompt(
 1437:         "Indica el motivo del rechazo:"
 1438:     );
 1439: 
 1440:     if (reason === null) {
 1441:         return;
 1442:     }
 1443: 
 1444:     if (!reason.trim()) {
 1445:         showMessage(
 1446:             "Debes indicar un motivo.",
 1447:             "warning"
 1448:         );
 1449:         return;
 1450:     }
 1451: 
 1452:     try {
 1453:         await apiRequest(
 1454:             `/invoices/${invoiceId}/reject`,
 1455:             {
 1456:                 method: "POST",
 1457:                 headers: {
 1458:                     "Content-Type": "application/json",
 1459:                 },
 1460:                 body: JSON.stringify({
 1461:                     reason: reason.trim(),
 1462:                 }),
 1463:             }
 1464:         );
 1465: 
 1466:         showMessage(
 1467:             "Factura rechazada.",
 1468:             "success"
 1469:         );
 1470: 
 1471:         await loadDocuments();
 1472: 
 1473:         const dialog = document.getElementById(
 1474:             "documentDetailDialog"
 1475:         );
 1476: 
 1477:         if (dialog?.open && documentId) {
 1478:             await showDetail(documentId);
 1479:         }
 1480:     } catch (error) {
 1481:         showMessage(
 1482:             `No se pudo rechazar: ${error.message}`,
 1483:             "error"
 1484:         );
 1485:     }
 1486: }
 1487: 
 1488: 
 1489: async function reprocessDocument(documentId) {
 1490:     const confirmed = window.confirm(
 1491:         "¿Quieres volver a ejecutar la extracción?"
 1492:     );
 1493: 
 1494:     if (!confirmed) {
 1495:         return;
 1496:     }
 1497: 
 1498:     try {
 1499:         const result = await apiRequest(
 1500:             `/documents/${documentId}/reprocess`,
 1501:             {
 1502:                 method: "POST",
 1503:             }
 1504:         );
 1505: 
 1506:         showMessage(
 1507:             result.message || "Documento reprocesado.",
 1508:             result.success ? "success" : "error"
 1509:         );
 1510: 
 1511:         await loadDocuments();
 1512:         await showDetail(documentId);
 1513:     } catch (error) {
 1514:         showMessage(
 1515:             `No se pudo reprocesar: ${error.message}`,
 1516:             "error"
 1517:         );
 1518:     }
 1519: }
 1520: 
 1521: 
 1522: /* ================================================================
 1523:    RIESGOS Y MENSAJES
 1524: ================================================================ */
 1525: 
 1526: function renderOpenRisks(documents) {
 1527:     const panel =
 1528:         document.getElementById("openRisksPanel");
 1529:     const text =
 1530:         document.getElementById("openRisksText");
 1531:     const list =
 1532:         document.getElementById("openRisksList");
 1533: 
 1534:     if (!panel || !text || !list) {
 1535:         return;
 1536:     }
 1537: 
 1538:     const risks = documents.filter(requiresReview);
 1539: 
 1540:     if (!risks.length) {
 1541:         panel.classList.add("hidden");
 1542:         list.innerHTML = "";
 1543:         return;
 1544:     }
 1545: 
 1546:     panel.classList.remove("hidden");
 1547: 
 1548:     text.textContent =
 1549:         `${risks.length} documento(s) requieren revisión.`;
 1550: 
 1551:     list.innerHTML = risks
 1552:         .map((documentItem) => {
 1553:             const invoice = documentItem.invoice || {};
 1554: 
 1555:             return `
 1556:                 <div class="risk-row">
 1557:                     <div>
 1558:                         <strong>
 1559:                             ${escapeHtml(
 1560:                                 invoice.supplier_name ||
 1561:                                 "Proveedor sin identificar"
 1562:                             )}
 1563:                         </strong>
 1564: 
 1565:                         <span>
 1566:                             ${formatMoney(
 1567:                                 invoice.total,
 1568:                                 invoice.currency
 1569:                             )}
 1570:                         </span>
 1571:                     </div>
 1572: 
 1573:                     <button
 1574:                         type="button"
 1575:                         class="btn-ghost"
 1576:                         onclick="
 1577:                             showDetail(
 1578:                                 ${Number(documentItem.id)}
 1579:                             )
 1580:                         "
 1581:                     >
 1582:                         Revisar
 1583:                     </button>
 1584:                 </div>
 1585:             `;
 1586:         })
 1587:         .join("");
 1588: }
 1589: 
 1590: 
 1591: function showMessage(message, type = "info") {
 1592:     let container =
 1593:         document.getElementById("appMessages");
 1594: 
 1595:     if (!container) {
 1596:         container = document.createElement("div");
 1597:         container.id = "appMessages";
 1598:         container.className = "app-messages";
 1599:         document.body.appendChild(container);
 1600:     }
 1601: 
 1602:     const item = document.createElement("div");
 1603: 
 1604:     item.className =
 1605:         `app-message app-message-${type}`;
 1606: 
 1607:     item.textContent = message;
 1608: 
 1609:     container.appendChild(item);
 1610: 
 1611:     window.setTimeout(() => {
 1612:         item.remove();
 1613:     }, 6000);
 1614: }
 1615: 
 1616: 
 1617: function renderApiError(title, detail) {
 1618:     const containers = [
 1619:         document.getElementById("documentsList"),
 1620:         document.getElementById("inboxList"),
 1621:     ].filter(Boolean);
 1622: 
 1623:     if (!containers.length) {
 1624:         showMessage(
 1625:             `${title}: ${detail}`,
 1626:             "error"
 1627:         );
 1628:         return;
 1629:     }
 1630: 
 1631:     containers.forEach((container) => {
 1632:         container.innerHTML = emptyState(
 1633:             "⚠️",
 1634:             title,
 1635:             detail
 1636:         );
 1637:     });
 1638: }
 1639:

"""
Genera PDFs de prueba realistas para CapaFiscal.
Uso:  python scripts/gen_facturas.py
Requiere: pip install reportlab
"""
from pathlib import Path
from reportlab.lib.pagesizes import A4
from reportlab.lib.units import mm
from reportlab.pdfgen import canvas

OUT = Path("facturas_prueba")
OUT.mkdir(exist_ok=True)


def factura(path, emisor, cif, direccion, num, fecha, concepto, base, iva_pct, iva_imp, total):
    c = canvas.Canvas(str(path), pagesize=A4)
    w, h = A4
    y = h - 40 * mm
    c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 20); c.drawString(25*mm, y, emisor)
    c.setFont("Helvetica", 9); y -= 6*mm
    c.drawString(25*mm, y, direccion); y -= 5*mm
    c.drawString(25*mm, y, f"CIF: {cif}")
    c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 13); c.drawString(140*mm, h-40*mm, "FACTURA")
    c.setFont("Helvetica", 9)
    c.drawString(140*mm, h-46*mm, f"Nº: {num}")
    c.drawString(140*mm, h-51*mm, f"Fecha: {fecha}")
    y -= 14*mm
    c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 9)
    c.drawString(25*mm, y, "Cliente: PYME EJEMPLO S.L.   CIF: B87654321")
    y -= 12*mm
    c.setFillColorRGB(0.15, 0.2, 0.55); c.rect(25*mm, y-2*mm, 160*mm, 8*mm, fill=1, stroke=0)
    c.setFillColorRGB(1, 1, 1); c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 9)
    c.drawString(28*mm, y, "Descripción"); c.drawString(150*mm, y, "Importe")
    c.setFillColorRGB(0, 0, 0); c.setFont("Helvetica", 9); y -= 10*mm
    c.drawString(28*mm, y, concepto); c.drawString(150*mm, y, f"{base:.2f} EUR".replace(".", ","))
    y -= 16*mm
    c.setFont("Helvetica", 10)
    c.drawString(120*mm, y, "Base imponible:"); c.drawString(160*mm, y, f"{base:.2f} €".replace(".", ",")); y -= 6*mm
    c.drawString(120*mm, y, f"IVA ({iva_pct}%):"); c.drawString(160*mm, y, f"{iva_imp:.2f} €".replace(".", ",")); y -= 6*mm
    c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 11)
    c.drawString(120*mm, y, "TOTAL:"); c.drawString(160*mm, y, f"{total:.2f} €".replace(".", ","))
    c.showPage(); c.save(); print("OK", path)


def requerimiento_aeat(path):
    c = canvas.Canvas(str(path), pagesize=A4)
    w, h = A4
    y = h - 40 * mm
    c.setFont("Helvetica-Bold", 16); c.drawString(25*mm, y, "AGENCIA TRIBUTARIA")
    c.setFont("Helvetica", 10); y -= 8*mm
    c.drawString(25*mm, y, "Requerimiento de documentacion"); y -= 6*mm
    c.drawString(25*mm, y, "Nº: REQ-2026-0088123   Fecha: 28/07/2026"); y -= 6*mm
    c.drawString(25*mm, y, "Expediente sujeto a plazo de respuesta.")
    c.showPage(); c.save(); print("OK", path)


if __name__ == "__main__":
    factura(OUT/"factura_repsol_2026.pdf", "REPSOL COMERCIAL S.A.", "A28047223",
            "C/ Mendez Alvaro 44, 28045 Madrid", "FRA-2026-00912", "15/07/2026",
            "Carburante Diesel e+ - 850 litros", 396.28, 21, 83.22, 479.50)
    factura(OUT/"factura_endesa_2026.pdf", "ENDESA ENERGIA S.A.U.", "A81948077",
            "C/ Ribera del Loira 60, 28042 Madrid", "E26-4471183", "10/07/2026",
            "Suministro electrico periodo 06/2026", 258.60, 21, 54.31, 312.91)
    factura(OUT/"factura_makro_2026.pdf", "MAKRO AUTOSERVICIO MAYORISTA S.A.", "A28647451",
            "Paseo Imperial 40, 28005 Madrid", "M-2026-778120", "12/07/2026",
            "Compra mercaderia - hosteleria", 635.01, 21, 133.35, 768.36)
    factura(OUT/"factura_vodafone_2026.pdf", "VODAFONE ESPANA S.A.U.", "A80907397",
            "Av. America 115, 28042 Madrid", "VF-2026-556231", "08/07/2026",
            "Telefonia movil y fibra empresa 07/2026", 71.90, 21, 15.10, 87.00)
    # Factura con importes descuadrados a propósito (dispara riesgo)
    factura(OUT/"factura_iberdrola_MAL.pdf", "IBERDROLA CLIENTES S.A.U.", "A95758389",
            "Plaza Euskadi 5, 48009 Bilbao", "IB-2026-99001", "20/07/2026",
            "Suministro electrico", 100.00, 21, 21.00, 200.00)
    # Requerimiento AEAT (dispara plazo)
    requerimiento_aeat(OUT/"requerimiento_aeat_2026.pdf")
    print("\nListo. PDFs en:", OUT.resolve())
